## Voice message privacy: a clip can only go to the conversation it was recorded in — 2026-11-06 (local commit on the golfme-architect worktree branch off origin/main 5058670; not pushed/deployed; client only, no migration, no backend change; NOT device / two-account verified)

Micky's privacy brief (isolated at 8bbe790 with modeled UI callbacks, not on a device or live account; no evidence a real user was affected).
- **Root cause (still present at 5058670)**: the destination was decided when `recorder.stop()` resolved, not when the recording started. `HoldToTalk` → `cbRef.current.onClip` → `useVoiceComposerProps` stable callback → the page's `voiceClipRef.current`, which `DirectMessageThread` / `GroupChat` reassign on every render to call `sendDirectVoice(other.id, …)` / `sendRoundVoice(callId, …)`. The page is reused across `/messages/:id` and round ids (notification / deep link), so a clip released in A whose stop was still pending was sent to B (with B's reply quote). That held even if the composer remounted: the old session's late callback still reached the page's newest closure. Upload, insert, Not sent and retry were already bound to their job (`photoJobsRef` stores otherId / roundId at entry), so the leak was only at the hand-over.
- **Policy (Micky's earlier approval)**: finish only in the original conversation. A released clip is delivered to A even if the golfer left A (unchanged). A clip still being recorded (or still waiting for the microphone) when the conversation changes is cancelled. Never redirected.
- **Fix**: `lib/voiceSession.ts`: each press captures a frozen `VoiceOrigin` ({kind: dm|round, id}) and a recording id; the delivered clip (`SentVoiceClip`) carries both. `originChanged()` cancels a live, unreleased hold for another origin (released clips untouched). `claimVoiceSend(clip, kind)` is the send boundary: destination = clip.origin of that kind, send-once, a clip without an origin refused. `voiceSendPlan` lets a page take the quote / clear its reply bar only if it still shows the origin. `HoldToTalk` gets an `origin` prop (read per press via a ref; an effect on origin change calls `originChanged`). `ChatComposer` / `useVoiceComposerProps(enabled, origin, onClip)` pass it. `DataContext.sendDirectVoice(clip, replyTo?)` / `sendRoundVoice(clip, replyTo?)` no longer take a destination argument. Frozen `DirectMessageThread.tsx`: only the voice lines (the import, origin passed to `useVoiceComposerProps`, the `voiceClipRef` handler); no keyboard / viewport / composer / ResizeObserver code touched. `GroupChat`: the same, and the read-only check now uses the origin round's status.
- **Tests**: new `tests/voiceRouting.test.ts` (53, in `test:chat`): the real session + plan + boundary wired like HoldToTalk / useVoiceComposerProps / the pages, a fake job-bound backend, deferred promises for mic / stop / upload / insert; all 9 brief cases for DM and round. Before: scratchpad `voice-routing/before-main.test.ts` (main's code and wiring) fails 2/2 ("A's clip went to B"); a mutant of the fixed session that reads the origin at delivery fails 17/53. After: 53/53; `voiceSession.test.ts` 26/26, `chatVoice.test.ts` 16/16 unchanged; `npm run check` 1006/1006. Headless `chat-voice/chatvoice.mjs` on a `vite preview` production build: 45/45 (demo, single conversation; it does not exercise A→B).
- **Pending (needs Micky's permission + two real test accounts)**: the manual checklist in the report (DM + round, real notification / deep-link navigation while stop / upload / insert is slow, background / resume, failures + retry, verified from the recipient side). Open questions: see the report.

## Async-state scan (f89e900), findings 1 + 4: chat photo picker scope, round message loading — 2026-11-06 (golfme-architect worktree branch off origin/main 046460c; one local commit, not pushed/deployed; client only, no migration, no Edge Function, no native change; NOT device-verified)

**1. A late photo pick could land in another chat.** The composer hands `onPickImage` to the async picker at tap time, and `useChatImageAttachment().pick` was one stable function for the page's life. DirectMessageThread keeps one instance across thread switches (GroupChat across round switches), so a native PHPicker answering after A→B put A's photo in B's composer (preview only; send was still explicit). Fix: `src/lib/chatImageAttachment.ts` (new, pure) + `src/lib/useChatImageAttachment.ts`. The hook now takes the thread/round id as `scope`. Each scope change starts a new visit generation, bumped during render. `pick` and `take` are bound to the visit they were rendered in, and `state` only shows the current visit's photo. A pick, its processing result, its failure toast and a send all check the visit plus the pick key. So a stale answer is dropped (A→B, A→B→A, out-of-order picks in one chat, failures, after unmount), its own preview URL is revoked, and it never reaches a send or upload. Call sites: one extra argument each in `DirectMessageThread.tsx` (`id`) and `GroupChat.tsx` (`callId`). ChatComposer is unchanged. No draft persistence across chats: a stale pick is discarded.

**4. Round messages: oldest-first, unlimited load.** `RealRoundsContext.refetch` loaded every visible `round_messages` row oldest-first with no limit. Under a PostgREST max-rows cap it would return the oldest slice and drop the newest messages. The live cap was not checked (unknown). Fix, following the DM pattern (RealSocialContext `MESSAGE_WINDOW`): `src/lib/roundMessagePages.ts` (new).
- The global load is now the newest `ROUND_MESSAGE_WINDOW` (1000) rows, ordered `created_at desc, id desc` and shown chronologically.
- Each open round chat also loads its own newest `ROUND_MESSAGE_PAGE` (200) rows (`watchRoundMessages`, called from GroupChat via DataContext; a no-op in demo), so busier rounds can't push a quiet round out of the shared window.
- Rows are merged by id and sorted by (created_at, id). A page is kept only if it's the latest request for that round and the chat is still open. A page is keyed by its round, so switching rounds can't leak rows.
- A local unsend patches every source. Account switch resets the store.
- The round channel now refetches when it resubscribes after a drop.
- GroupChat docking and scroll code is untouched.
- Keyset cursor for older pages: `roundMessagesBefore` / `olderPageCursor`. Built and tested, not wired: there's no "load older" UI (DMs have none either).

**Tests:** `tests/chatImageAttachment.test.ts` (15; uses the real `createMediaPicker` with a controlled native driver), `tests/roundMessagePages.test.ts` (16; an in-memory table with a small row cap; the first case shows the old query shape missing the newest row). Both run in `npm run test:chat` / `npm run test:roundchat`. `npm run check` green (993 tests).

**Live max_rows = 1000** (Micky, Supabase dashboard → Data API → Max rows, 2026-11-06). So the round window (1000) and the DM window (1000) equal the cap, and the per-round page (200) is under it.

**Follow-up (same day, Micky's decisions):** "load older" is deferred. Instead the thread now says when it isn't showing everything:
- **Round chat:** shows "Showing the latest N messages" (`chat.historyLatest`) at the top of the list. The per-round page query now asks for `count: "exact"`, which gives the round's true total whatever the row cap. `isHistoryCut` (lib/roundMessagePages.ts) is held < total. Without a total it falls back to "the response filled the limit". The line is hidden until the round's page has loaded, so it doesn't flicker.
- **DMs:** shows "Older messages may not be shown" (`chat.historyMayBeCut`). The full-refresh DM window query also asks for `count: "exact"` (RealSocialContext `messageHistoryCut`). Only the whole window is known to be cut, not which threads, hence "may".
- Both lines are static rows inside the list, above the message rows. No docking, scroll or keyboard code changed. DirectMessageThread: one destructured name and the row. Localised in all 6 languages. Never shown in demo.
- More tests: the cut rule (exactly full vs one short, both windows, cap below the limit), a server cap of 50 below the page (200) and window (1000) with busy rounds, realtime inserts and round switches, and cap = window = 1000 at the exact boundary.

**Open for Micky:** a "load older" control (deferred).

## Async-state fixes: Community upload slots + Caddie pick race — 2026-11-06 (one local commit on the golfme-architect worktree branch off origin/main 046460c; not pushed/deployed; web only, no migration, no backend, no native change; findings 2 + 3 of Micky's async-state brief scanned at f89e900)

- **Community post media (CreatePost):** overlapping photo/video batches shared one `mediaUploading` boolean (the first batch to finish re-enabled Post while another still uploaded) and the 10-item cap was checked against `mediaItems.length` only (picks made during uploads, or two before a render, each saw 10 free; a modelled 6+6 repro admitted 12). Now `src/lib/postMediaLedger.ts`: slots are reserved synchronously at the pick (accepted + uploading never passes 10), each upload ends its slot exactly once (commit or release), Remove / Replace / leaving the page cancel pending slots so a late upload can't come back, items are inserted in pick order, and `handlePost` refuses on its own (uploads pending, over the cap, or a snapshot the ledger doesn't match), not only via the disabled button. Photos still uploading count as the attached kind, so picking a swing video meanwhile asks first. Server side has no media-count limit (proposal below); a cancelled upload's already-written public object is left in storage, as before.
- **Caddie upload (AnalyzeSwing):** each pick awaited a length read and a 200 MB in-memory copy, with the picker still usable; pick A, pick B, B ready, then A finishing overwrote B's draft (or opened the trim selector on A), and it could even land after leaving the page. Now `src/lib/videoSelection.ts`: one token per pick, only the newest one of an open page sets the draft / selector / spinner; Remove and unmount invalidate; the preview object URL is created only for the winner. The reads can't be aborted, so nothing depends on cancelling them. The persisted-job architecture is untouched (this is the pre-upload draft only).
- Tests: `tests/postMediaLedger.test.ts` (13), `tests/videoSelection.test.ts` (10), deferred promises, in `npm test`, `test:trust`, `test:caddie`. `npm run check` green. Headless browser smoke not run (the local headless shell is missing libnspr4). Not checked on an iPhone.
- Open: add a server-side cap (for example a trigger on `community_post_media` refusing an 11th row per post)? Migration not written; ask Micky.

## Smooth keyboard motion (field reveal) — 2026-11-06 (branch `feat/keyboard-motion` off origin/main 1c032e1; one local commit, not pushed/deployed; golfme-architect; web-bundle only, no migration, no backend, no native change)

Micky's brief: tapping a text field moves the screen abruptly. The audit and plan were approved, with Micky's answers: Q1 yes (smooth closing move), Q2 no (sheet lift stays instant), Q3 yes (GroupChat card and PostDetail docking stay instant), Q4 yes (hidden toggle).

**Root cause (native app only).** `keyboardWillShow` arrives as the keyboard starts its slide of about 250 ms. Our reveal code then jumped to the final position on the next frame, ahead of the keyboard. The plugin itself (resize Body, `Keyboard.m`) only sets `body.style.height` about 0.45 s later; the app shell is `min-h-screen`, so that moves nothing. On close, removing the reveal's spacer let the browser snap the page back into range in one frame. On the web (Safari/PWA), none of this code runs, because the keyboard height is always 0 there; that path is unchanged.

**What changed.** `src/lib/keyboard/smoothScroll.ts` is a short scroll animation driven by requestAnimationFrame, with the target updated as things change.
- Timing: 140–260 ms depending on distance, ease-out cubic, never past the target, hard deadline.
- It ends exactly where the old code jumped to.
- One window animator is shared by every caller; scrollers get one each.
- Each frame does one scroll read and one write; no React state.
- A repeat call for the same target (within 1 px) joins the running motion. A new target continues from the current position.
- Stops when: the field loses focus, the keyboard state changes, the route changes, the scroller leaves the page, or a touch or wheel arrives (listeners exist only while it runs). Typing never stops it.
- A WebKit pan of the window is ridden through, not fought. Inside a scroller, another write stops it, so KeyboardAwareModal's close-restore wins.
- Reduced motion or the switch off: the old one-frame write.

Used in three places:
- `useRevealFocusedFieldAboveKeyboard`: the open reveal, with the same distance via `minimalReveal.pageRevealDelta`. Plus the closing glide: on the frame after willHide (after React has committed the closed layout), if the page sits past `#root.scrollHeight - innerHeight`, it glides there and only then drops the spacer. Skipped after a drag-dismiss, with reduced motion, or with the switch off; a reopening keyboard keeps the spacer.
- `revealFocusedField`, used by KeyboardAwareModal and ProfileSetup: same distance via `scrollerRevealDelta`.
- CourseAutocomplete's minimal reveal: one call.

**Not touched:** DirectMessageThread, useChatViewportBox, useWebKeyboardInset, GroupChat, PostDetail, Modal.tsx (the sheet lift and close-restore stay instant), ProfileSetup.tsx, the keyboard store, Capacitor config, CSS.

**Kill switch.** `SMOOTH_KEYBOARD_MOTION` in `smoothScroll.ts`. On the phone, per device: About, then tap the description line 7 times (founder account only, like the other test switches). It toggles localStorage `golfme:kbMotion` between "on" and "off", with a toast, and takes effect on the next field tap.

**Tests.** `tests/smoothScroll.test.ts` (18: duration and easing bounds, frames, no overshoot, exact end, deadline, instant/reduced, no-op, coalescing, retarget, guard, touch cancel, listener cleanup, external scroll, yield, clamped write terminates). `tests/minimalReveal.test.ts` gains page/scroller reveal maths, checked against the old inline expressions.

**Harness.** New screens in `scripts/dev/fake-keyboard.mjs`: `host-area-motion`, `host-area-reduced`, `host-touch-during`, `host-nav-during`, `host-field-switch`, `sheet-motion`; also a `--motion` flag and per-frame trace summaries in `--diff`. Before/after results: see the commit message.

**Needs a physical iPhone (native app).** The brief's matrix:
- Feel against the real keyboard spring (only the duration and curve constants would change).
- Field switching; a finger during the reveal; navigating during the reveal.
- Rotation; hardware keyboard; emoji keyboard; VoiceOver; Reduce Motion; IME/dictation during a reveal.
- Sheets (Report, Review, Location picker).
- GroupChat card and full-screen round chat, and DMs: should be unchanged.
- Mobile Safari: should be unchanged.

## Verified Golfer badge: phone dropped, derived in Postgres — 2026-11-06 (one local commit on the golfme-architect worktree branch, off origin/main 42a4ffc; not pushed/deployed; migration `20261107230000_verified_golfer_derived` PREPARED, NOT APPLIED)

Micky's decision: real phone verification never existed (columns frozen in 20260919030000), so nobody could earn the badge. New rule: **Verified Golfer = (a confirmed email OR an Apple/Google sign-in identity) + a profile photo + at least 1 qualifying completed round.** Reputation scoring, thresholds, tier names and the qualifying-round definition are untouched: the round check calls `get_reputation_state(uid).qualifying_rounds >= 1` (byte-identical function, checked in PGlite).

Design: `profiles.verified_golfer` stays the one column every surface already reads (profiles `select *`, directory RPCs, admin), so profile/cards/chat/community/join modal switch over with no client change; it becomes trigger-maintained and never client-settable. `protect_unverified_trust_columns()` now derives it (recompute when `photo_url` changes or the server refresh asks via a transaction-local GUC; otherwise keeps the stored value; whatever a client sends is discarded); a new BEFORE INSERT trigger derives it on insert (closes `profiles_insert_own`). `private.refresh_verified_golfer(uid)` runs from AFTER triggers on `auth.users.email_confirmed_at`, `golf_calls.status` to/from completed, and `round_participants` changes on a completed (or cascade-deleted) round; refresh errors are warnings and never fail the sign-in/round write. `public.get_my_verified_golfer_status()` (no argument, authenticated only) returns the caller's own 4 booleans and self-heals the stored value (covers linking an Apple/Google identity, which has no trigger: no new trigger on `auth.identities` on purpose). All helpers live in `private` with EXECUTE revoked; every function SECURITY DEFINER, `search_path = ''`. Other golfers see only the boolean. `phone_verified`/`email_verified` retired (always false). Backfill re-derives every row once (clears any badge self-granted before the September fix).

Client: Reputation → Trust & verification: phone row and the demo "Apply" flow gone; a Verified Golfer block with a done / not-yet checklist of the three criteria (`src/lib/verifiedGolfer.ts`, `src/lib/useVerifiedGolferChecklist.ts`, `src/pages/ReputationDetail.tsx`). Real accounts: shown only once the RPC exists (PGRST202/42883 → hidden, today's behaviour); if the server value differs from the loaded profile it refreshes the profile. Demo: the same rule over demo state, mirrored onto the demo account's own badge (`syncDemoVerifiedGolfer`); `setPhoneVerified` removed and the real-path writes to the frozen columns dropped. Strings ×6 (no emojis): `reputation.verifiedCheck.*`, new `verifiedBadgeInactive`; `phoneNumber`/`apply`/`nowVerifiedToast` removed.

Checks: `npm run check` green (919/919 incl. `tests/verifiedGolfer.test.ts`); PGlite (scratchpad `pglite/_verified_badge.mjs`, prod-like + full chains) 104/104 incl. negative controls (own badge can't be set by update or insert, another golfer's auth data can't be read, private helpers not callable, anon can't call the RPC), rollback fingerprint identical, 5/5 sabotaged migrations caught; headless on a vite-preview production build, demo, 390px, light + dark (scratchpad `verified-badge/`). Paste files: scratchpad `verified-badge-{apply,check,rollback}.sql`.

**UI v2 (Micky: "I don't like the UI… do it again"; same logic/data/migration):** one card, two states. A `VerifiedMedallion` (`src/components/golfer/VerifiedMedallion.tsx`: the same lucide ShieldCheck other golfers see, in a fairway disc, with a thin progress ring) + "Verified Golfer" + one line. Not earned: pale disc / outlined shield, ring at done of 3, "2 of 3", three step rows (MailCheck / Camera / Flag tiles; done = filled check circle; undone = inline action: Confirm email (demo modal / real resend), Add photo (`src/components/profile/AddPhotoButton.tsx`, same resize/upload path as AvatarUpload), Find a round → `/find?tab=rounds`). Earned: filled disc, one-time shine per device (`golfme:verified-shine-seen:<id>`, `animate-medallion-shine`, static under reduced motion), "Verified · other golfers see this badge on your profile", compact read-only summary. The separate Email row is merged into step 1 (kept only as the pre-migration fallback for real accounts); privacy footnote with a lock. Labelled list, per-step state and "n of 3 steps done" as screen-reader text. Strings ×6 `reputation.verified.*`; removed `verifiedBadge`, `verifiedBadgeActive`, `verifiedBadgeInactive`, `active`. Privacy Policy gets a "Verified Golfer badge" item (ships with this). Screenshots: scratchpad `verified-badge-v2/`.

**Tutorial step (Micky approved v2, "much better"):** "Get verified" after Chat and before Caddie (`VERIFIED_TUTORIAL_ID`, route `/profile/reputation`), spotlighting the whole Trust & verification card, which always renders (so also before the migration). Strings ×6 `tutorial.verified.*`; `tests/tutorialSteps.test.ts` updated. Headless on demo (tour started via the fiber tree, since it only auto-starts for real accounts): step 4 of 5, spotlight on the card, light + dark (scratchpad `verified-badge-v2/tutorial-verified-*.png`).

Next: Micky applies `verified-badge-apply.sql` (check false → true). Then on a real account: Reputation shows the checklist; add/remove a photo and complete a two-person round to see the badge flip on another account's view. Privacy Policy: mention that the badge is computed from email confirmation / sign-in provider, photo presence and completed rounds, and that only the yes/no badge is shown to others.
## Caddie club picker: requirement made explicit — 2026-11-06 (local commit, not pushed/deployed; client only, no migration, no Edge Function change)
- Reported "Driver needs a second selection": traced, not a state bug. Driver has two clubs (Driver, Mini Driver), so the card opens chips and the first chip tap picks (headless: one chip tap enables Ask Caddie; re-tapping the card keeps it). Nothing clears or delays the first pick (card tap only clears a club from ANOTHER category; draft restore and the draft write keep it).
- Card tap picks a club by itself only when its category has exactly one (`onlyClubIn` / `clubAfterCardTap` in `lib/clubCatalog.ts`); no category qualifies today, so behaviour is unchanged. Never a default in a multi-option category.
- Label "Club (required)" (`caddie.club.requiredLabel`); an opened category with no chip shows "Pick your exact club" (`caddie.club.pickExact`, aria-describedby on the chip group); when the club is the only thing blocking Ask Caddie (video present, nothing else), "Choose a club to continue" under the button (`caddie.club.chooseToContinue`, aria-describedby on the button). Reason logic: `analysisBlocker()` in `lib/trimReopen.ts` (`canStartAnalysis` = no blocker). All 6 locales.
- Only a catalog value counts as a club (`isCatalogClub`, `restoredDraftClub`): a stale draft value reads as missing. Server checks unchanged.
- A11y: both radiogroups `aria-required`; arrow keys/Home/End move focus within a row (Space/Enter picks); inset focus ring on cards (the row scrolls and would clip an outline).
- Needs a real iPhone: VoiceOver reading the hint on the disabled button; the first tap on a card/chip registering as a click (not a hover) in WKWebView.

## Community video: black screen on another account — 2026-11-06 (branch `fix/video-playback-accounts`, first made off 7a22d01, rebased onto origin/main b0621c7 on 2026-11-06; one local commit, not pushed/deployed; golfme-architect; no migration, no backend, no native change)

Micky's report: a Community post video plays on his account but shows a black screen on his mom's (native iOS app). Audit result: **not an access problem**. Community media is in the PUBLIC `community-media` bucket and every post stores a permanent `/object/public/` URL (`CreatePost.tsx`, `copyCaddieMediaToCommunity`); public URLs never consult storage RLS, and the post rows are readable by every signed-in user. PGlite (all migrations replayed): owner / follower / new user / anon all get the same answer for every path pattern; following the poster changes nothing; caddie-media stays owner-only. No signed URL is ever written to a post (guard in CreatePost since 743c7e0). So the difference is the **device**, most likely one of: (a) the file can't be decoded there (direct Community uploads are stored as-is, no transcoding: iPhone HEVC, possibly 10-bit HDR / 4K / 240 fps slo-mo; only Caddie's trimmed copies are re-encoded to H.264); (b) autoplay refused on her phone (Low Power / Low Data Mode) with no usable poster, so WebKit paints nothing; (c) a dead or slow link with no error UI. In all three the old code showed a silent black box.

Fix (client only): `src/lib/videoPlayback.ts` (pure: resolves any stored signed/authenticated link into the public bucket to its permanent public URL at render, never writes rows; private caddie-media links are never made public; first-frame hint `#t=0.001` when there's no usable thumbnail; failure classification from MediaError + one HEAD probe: 400/401/403/404/410 = unavailable, network/5xx/429/408/stall = network, reachable-but-undecodable = unsupported), `src/components/community/useVideoPlayback.ts` + `VideoStatusOverlay.tsx` (spinner after 0.5 s, gives up after 20 s, Retry only for network failures, Retry keeps the playhead, no automatic retry loop), wired into the Swing Post feed video (`PostCard.tsx`, plus a play badge when autoplay is refused) and both carousel videos (`MediaCarousel.tsx`: the card video and the fullscreen viewer's video slide, now `LightboxVideo`). Photos in the fullscreen viewer stay main's `ZoomableImage` (pinch/double-tap zoom, added after 7a22d01): the rebase only merged imports; video slides keep `data-video-controls`, image slides are untouched. 4 new strings ×6 (`media.video*`). Tests: `tests/videoPlayback.test.ts` (11). Caddie replay untouched (already re-signs per owner on error).

Checks after the rebase: `npm run check` passes (886/886 node tests); headless on a production preview build (placeholder env, demo account): the Community feed loads with no page errors and the scratchpad `zoom/zoom.mjs` suite (photo pinch/double-tap zoom, DM viewer, own avatar) is 42/42. The video overlay itself was not re-exercised after the rebase (the merge only touched MediaCarousel's imports). The original pre-rebase headless run (faked media host) was 20/20: plays, 404/400 → unavailable without Retry, garbage bytes → unsupported, loads-but-no-picture (videoWidth 0, WebKit's silent black-frame case) → unsupported, connection failure → Retry recovers and resumes the position, never-answering host → spinner then a retryable error.

Next: Micky runs `video-playback-diagnose.sql` (read-only, ids only) and sends block 6 for the failing post, plus mom's iPhone model + iOS version and whether Low Power Mode is on. If the file turns out to be HEVC/HDR/high-frame-rate, the real fix is server-side H.264 transcoding of Community uploads (needs his approval; not built). Needs a real check on two accounts / two iPhones after deploy.

## Caddie "Saved swings" — 2026-11-06 (shipped to main; migration `20261107210000_caddie_saved_swings` PREPARED, NOT APPLIED; no Edge Function change; golfme-architect)
- Micky: "a sub-section where people can save their fav swings, compiled by big categories like driver or iron or woods, so they can go back and find it."
- **Save**: a Save / Saved pill (lucide Bookmark / BookmarkCheck, `aria-pressed`, tap haptic) beside the title on a COMPLETE analysis (`CaddieAnalysisDetail`). Optimistic in `RealCaddieContext.setSaved` (rolls back unless something newer replaced it; the server's time then replaces the optimistic one); a failure shows a toast in the Caddie language. Failed / processing analyses show no Save.
- **Shelf** (`components/caddie/SavedSwings.tsx`) on /caddie between Analyze and Recent (Recent unchanged), shown once Save is available and there is at least one complete analysis. Chips All / Driver / Woods / Hybrids / Irons / Wedges / Other with counts; empty categories hidden; a filter whose last swing was un-saved falls back to All. A horizontal row of 136 px cards: the thumbnail (or the club picker's vector art when there is none, a bookmark glyph for Other), club label (`clubLabel`), date, score. Tap opens the analysis. Empty state: "Save a swing from its results to find it here." Categories come from `lib/clubCatalog` (`lib/caddieSaved.ts`: "Other" = no club, Not sure, or a typed non-club).
- **Data**: `caddie_analyses.saved_at timestamptz null`; CHECK `saved_at is null or status = 'complete'`; partial index `(owner_id, saved_at desc) where saved_at is not null`; `set_caddie_analysis_saved(id, saved) returns timestamptz` (SECURITY DEFINER, `search_path = ''`, owner check with the same answer for "not yours" and "not found", complete-only to save, writes ONLY saved_at, keeps the first saved time on a repeat; execute revoked from public/anon, granted to authenticated). Independent of `20261107140000` (applies with or without it). Known/accepted: the Option B `caddie_analyses_update_own` policy still lets an owner UPDATE their own row directly; the CHECK keeps "complete only" true on that path too. Updates flow through the existing owner-filtered realtime subscription (the list refetches); `updated_at` is bumped by the existing trigger (the pose cache ignores that for rows that already have pose frames). Translate/analyze-swing rows that don't select saved_at keep the list's value (`keepSavedAt`). Deleting the analysis or the account removes the save.
- **Feature detection**: the list select asks for `saved_at`; a 42703/PGRST204 naming saved_at re-reads without it and keeps Save + shelf hidden for the session (`savedSupported`). So this client can ship before the SQL.
- **Demo**: saves in localStorage `golfme:demoCaddieSaved` (id -> ISO time, try/catch, junk-tolerant), cleared by Reset demo data.
- Strings: 15 `caddie.saved.*` keys x6 locales, no emoji (tested).
- **Checks**: `npm run check` green (tsc, oxlint 0 errors / 22 pre-existing warnings, 882/882 node tests incl. new `tests/caddieSaved.test.ts` in `test:caddie`, build). PGlite (`scratchpad/pglite/_caddie_saved.mjs`, full chain AND without 20261107140000): 78/78 — check false/true/false, apply refuses twice, owner save/unsave, other columns unchanged, another user can't save/unsave/see (RLS + RPC), failed/processing refused by the RPC and by direct UPDATE (CHECK), saved row can't leave complete, anon refused, no allowance charge / start / notification side effects, delete removes the save, rollback restores the exact schema fingerprint, re-apply ok; 5/5 sabotage controls caught. Headless demo on `vite preview` of the production build at 390x844: 24/24 (empty state, save/unsave, chips + counts, filter, open from shelf, reload persistence, delete empties the shelf, light + dark, no console errors). Screenshots in scratchpad `caddie-saved/` (the scored cards used a temporary demo score, reverted).
- **Deploy order (Micky's call)**: client can go first (Save stays hidden). Then paste scratchpad `caddie-saved-apply.sql`, then `caddie-saved-check.sql` (must return `all_true` = true). Rollback: `caddie-saved-rollback.sql` (forgets every save; the app hides Save again on next launch).
- **Needs an iPhone**: Save tap feel + haptic, horizontal shelf scroll vs. page scroll, real thumbnails on the cards, the save showing up after backgrounding (realtime/resume refetch).
- Open: the placeholder art reuses the club-picker vector art, which is still awaiting Micky's approval; if it's replaced, the shelf follows (`CLUB_ART`).

## iOS 2.5 (1): one-tap video picking + multi-select in GolfMeMediaPicker — 2026-11-06 (local commit on `worktree-agent-ac2d9cab63b93cf65`, not pushed; golfme-architect; no migration, no backend)

- Micky: picking a VIDEO still showed Apple's extra "1 Selected" sheet; then: chat should pick several photos/videos at once (up to 10, mixed). Native change, needs a **2.5 (1)** Xcode build (`MARKETING_VERSION` 2.4 -> 2.5, Debug + Release, build 1). No new Swift file, no `project.pbxproj` file entries, no Info.plist keys (PHPicker without a library needs no permission).
- **Plugin** (`ios/App/App/MediaPickerPlugin.swift`): `pick({ kinds, limit })` (kinds image/video/both; limit 1...10, default 1 = one tap picks and closes; above 1 `.ordered` selection and iOS's own Add button). Returns `{ items: [...] }` in selection order; one item also at the top level (2.4 shape). Photos unchanged (pixels-only JPEG base64). Videos: `.compatible` representation (Micky/coordinator: iOS exports HEVC/HDR originals as H.264; HEVC is the suspected cause of the "black screen on some phones" Community video bug, so `.current` was rejected), `loadFileRepresentation(public.movie)` copied into `Caches/GolfMeMediaPicks/pick-<uuid>.<ext>` before the callback returns, result `{ kind: "video", path, webPath, mimeType, size, name?, durationMs? }`, no base64. Items load one at a time (ten 12 MP decodes never overlap). New `readChunk({ path, offset, length })` (base64, max 4 MB) and `discard({ path })`, both restricted to that folder; the folder is emptied at the start of every video pick.
- **How the bytes reach the page:** NOT `fetch(webPath)`. The page is https://golfme.app (server.url) and webPath is `capacitor://localhost/_capacitor_file_/...`, another origin; Capacitor 8's `WebViewAssetHandler` answers .mov/.mp4 with a plain `URLResponse` (no CORS header) unless the request has a Range. So the page reads the copy over the bridge in 2 MB chunks, each chunk becoming a Blob straight away, assembles one File, then calls `discard`.
- **Web** (`src/lib/mediaPicker.ts`, shared; `src/lib/chatMediaPicker.ts` re-exports it, chat API unchanged): `pickMedia` (one File) and `pickMediaFiles` (File[], for chat albums; another agent wires chat). Build detection is synchronous inside the tap, from the plugin's exported method list (`Capacitor.PluginHeaders`): 2.4 has only `pick` -> native photos (limit 1) only, video and multi-select fall back to the file input; 2.5 has `readChunk` -> photos, videos, multi. `maxVideoBytes` + `onTooLarge` skip reading an oversized clip; `onLoading` drives a spinner while a video is read. Native video Files are marked in-memory (`isInMemoryPick`) so AnalyzeSwing/CreatePost skip their second 200 MB copy.
- **Pages:** `AnalyzeSwing` Select Video and `CreatePost` swing video + Replace use `pickMedia({ kinds: ["video"] })` into the existing handlers (trim selector, size/duration checks, upload, thumbnail unchanged); `CreatePost`'s photo pill / Add more use `pickMediaFiles({ kinds: ["image","video"], limit: remaining slots })`, web fallback stays `<input multiple>`. Caddie's persisted-job architecture untouched.
- Tests: `tests/mediaPicker.test.ts` (replaces chatMediaPicker.test.ts; 25: 2.4 vs 2.5 branching, chunk assembly incl. size mismatch, cancel/error, too large, double tap during pick and during read, multi order/limit/unreadable). `npm run check` 889/889. Swift not compiled (Linux).
- **Device timing to measure:** `.compatible` makes iOS export (transcode) an HEVC clip to H.264 after the picker closes and before `loadFileRepresentation` returns; the page shows its spinner only once the plugin answers, so a long export is a gap with no feedback. Time it for a 10 s and a 30 s 4K60 HEVC clip (plus the chunk read for a ~100 MB file). If it's long, a native "preparing" signal is the follow-up. Still one tap.
- **iPhone checks (2.5 build):** Caddie Select Video: one tap, no "1 Selected", preview + trim + Ask Caddie work, a 4K60 clip under 200 MB loads in reasonable time; CreatePost swing video + multi-select (order kept, Add button); chat photo still one tap; on the 2.4 build with the new web deploy: Caddie video falls back to the old input (no error).
## Chat video + albums (DMs + round chats) — 2026-11-06 (one local commit, golfme-architect; NOT pushed, NOT deployed; migration `20261107220000_chat_video_albums` PREPARED, NOT APPLIED; delete-account + new chat-video-retention Edge Functions NOT deployed)

Micky approved the video plan ("yes for all": 60 s max, server-side transcode on our own infrastructure, videos auto-delete after 180 days, ONE picker), then added albums ("select multiple photos ... including videos ... like a deck of cards, same as iMessage or WeChat").

**Data model (one design for both):** a `media jsonb` column on `messages` and `round_messages`: an ordered array of 1..10 items. A single video = a 1-item array; an album = 2..10 photos/videos mixed. Single photos (`image_*`) and voice (`audio_*`) keep their columns and paths exactly as before (a lone picked photo still goes the old way); existing rows untouched. At most one of image_path / audio_path / media per message. Items: `{kind:"image",path,w,h,thumb?}` / `{kind:"video",path,poster,w,h,ms}` / expired `{kind:"video",expired:true,w,h,ms}`; paths are a pure function of row + position (`<scope>/<scope id>/<message id>/<i>.jpg|.mp4|.poster.jpg`), so the folded CHECK (`private.chat_media_valid`, immutable) verifies them with no lookup. jsonb chosen over a child table: same insert policy / RLS / realtime event as the row, no second insert (no empty album on a recipient's refetch), no SECURITY DEFINER send RPC duplicating the block/round rules, unsend = one column, account deletion cascades with the row. Storage read uses GIN `jsonb_path_ops` containment.

**Video pipeline:** the original goes up with a resumable TUS upload (6 MiB chunks, resumes via HEAD) to the owner-only staging bucket `chat-video-incoming` (`<uid>/<msg>-<i>.orig`, 200 MiB, `video/*`). The client then calls `POST /api/chat-video` (new Vercel function, ffmpeg-static, same plumbing as trim-video) with only `{scope, scopeId, messageId, index, stagedPath, trim}` and the golfer's own token; the function verifies the token, asks `public.chat_video_can_post` (photo membership rules), downloads the staged original through a signed URL made with THAT token, transcodes (trim window, max 60 s; long edge 1280, even sizes, autorotate; H.264 High + AAC 128k, CRF 23 capped 2.5 Mb/s, second pass at 1.5 Mb/s if > 24 MiB; `-fpsmax 30`; +faststart; HDR (PQ/HLG) tonemapped with zscale+tonemap hable, colorspace fallback when zimg is missing; `-map 0:v:0 -map 0:a:0?` drops iPhone location/data tracks; `-map_metadata -1 -map_metadata:s -1 -map_chapters -1 -fflags +bitexact`, then the empty `moov/udta/meta` shell the mp4 muxer still writes is overwritten in place as a zeroed `free` box, and the output is re-scanned: any udta/meta/ilst/keys/loci/XMP/uuid/©xxx box = refused, fail closed), makes a poster JPEG from the output, uploads both to private `chat-video` (25 MiB, video/mp4 + image/jpeg) WITH THE SENDER'S TOKEN (owner = sender; the bucket's INSERT policy is the real gate), deletes the staged original, returns paths + output size/length. Idempotent: a retry after a lost answer finds the outputs and returns them. Env: SUPABASE_URL/SUPABASE_ANON_KEY or the existing VITE_ ones; no service key. `vercel.json`: maxDuration 300 for this function. The client inserts the row afterwards. Album photos are re-encoded on the device like single photos and uploaded straight to chat-video (trigger enforces <= 2 MiB from the object's stored size).

**Migration `20261107220000_chat_video_albums`** (guard: needs 20261107180000): both buckets + 6 storage policies (chat-video: insert member + unused message id, select owner / readable row / admin+report, delete own+unused; incoming: owner-only insert/select/delete with the name bound to auth.uid(); no UPDATE anywhere); `media` folded into the single text checks; triggers `check_*_media` (authz first = no oracle, malformed arrays left to the CHECK, every object must exist owned by the sender, photo items <= 2 MiB; on update media can only be cleared or a video item expired); unsend clears media (copy stays text only); `notify_new_message` bell "<name> sent a video." / "sent 3 photos." / "sent 2 photos and 1 video.", push captions "[Video] / [Album] / [Photo] <caption>" (the old "📷 " prefix is gone); `admin_list_report_chat_media`; `chat_media_paths_for_user(uuid, text)` overload (the 1-arg version unchanged); retention `chat_video_expire_due` (180 days, kept while a matching report is `open`) + `chat_video_orphans` (unreferenced chat-video objects and staged originals > 1 day), both service-role only; `private.request_chat_video_retention()` posts to the new Edge Function with the existing `push_internal_secret`, scheduled daily 04:41 UTC by pg_cron (`create extension if not exists pg_cron` + idempotent unschedule/schedule, the same as 20261107240000, which already installed it in production; clear of its 04:17 purge and :23 sweep; rollback removes only this job). Objects can't be deleted from SQL (storage.protect_delete), so the `chat-video-retention` Edge Function (handler.ts + tests) clears rows first, then deletes objects, then sweeps orphans (a failed delete is retried next run). Expired videos show "Video expired" at their reserved size. Paste files (session scratchpad): `chat-video-apply.sql` (refuses twice), `chat-video-check.sql` (one boolean), `chat-video-rollback.sql` (exact 20261107180000 versions back; media rows become "[Video]"/"[Album]" or keep their caption; buckets dropped only when empty).

**Client:** ChatComposer: additive props only (`onPickFiles`, `mediaAccept`, `mediaMultiple`); the same image button accepts `image/*,video/*` with `multiple` once the column is detected (probe of `media`), else exactly as before; keyboard/viewport/autosize/ResizeObserver code untouched, GroupChat docking unchanged. `lib/useChatMediaAttachment` (up to 10, in pick order; a video > 60 s opens `ChatVideoTrimSheet` = the Caddie `VideoTrimSelector`, reused with chat labels), `MediaAttachmentStrip` (one row of chips with poster/duration/remove, count "3/10"; a single photo looks like the old chip). Bubbles (`components/chat/ChatMedia.tsx`): single video reserved at its aspect ratio, inline muted playsInline, autoplays only while >= 60% visible (never with reduced motion), tap = sound, corner button = fullscreen in the existing FullscreenMediaViewer; album = stacked deck (top card flat, next two fanned and peeking; reduced motion: flat, no animation), "1/5" pill, sideways swipe flips cards (no wrap), desktop arrows + keyboard, tap opens the viewer at that item with swipe between items (pinch-zoom on photos). **Gesture split:** a drag that STARTS on a deck (`[data-chat-deck]`) belongs to the deck (touch-action pan-y keeps vertical scrolling); `useSwipeToReply` and `useSwipeToRevealTimes` skip it (one line each), so albums are replied to from the hold menu; a hold still opens the menu. Hold menu: react / reply / unsend apply to the whole message; Copy hidden for media. Sending: one message; per-item progress; any failed item = "Not sent · Retry / Discard" for the album, Retry resumes (stored items never re-uploaded, an unfinished TUS upload resumes, a lost transcoder answer is idempotent). The sender's pending bubble plays its local original with the trim window (`#t=` media fragment). Previews: "[Video]", "[3 photos]", "[2 photos, 1 video]" (+ caption), 6 locales; 34 new strings x6. Admin report view lists album photos and videos. Demo: page-local object URLs like demo photos.

**Account deletion:** delete-account now purges chat-media, chat-video and chat-video-incoming by owner (needs a redeploy; harmless before the SQL).

**Checks:** `npm run check` 898/898 node tests (new: `tests/chatMedia.test.ts` 11, `tests/chatVideoTranscode.test.ts` 7 incl. a synthetic HDR HEVC HLG clip with a fake ©xyz GPS location + title (positive control finds them in the input; the output has no metadata boxes, no GPS/title/"Lavf" bytes, H.264 High, bt709, moov before mdat, poster without EXIF/XMP), rotated portrait stays portrait, 65 s -> 60 s, trim window honoured, non-video -> 422; `supabase/functions/chat-video-retention/handler.test.ts` 5), lint 0 errors, build. Strict tsc on `api/chat-video.ts`: clean. `deno check` chat-video-retention: clean; delete-account: the same `ReturnType<typeof createClient>` rpc-typing errors main already has (3 on main, 4 now; type-only). PGlite `pglite/_chat_video.mjs` 108/108 incl. 9 negative controls (each fails on the rule it sabotages), apply/check/rollback exact fingerprint/re-apply, earlier checks still true. Headless on a production build (vite preview, demo, VP8 WebM fixtures): `chat-video/chatvideo.mjs` 46/46 with light + dark screenshots; regressions: chatimages-bracket 48/48 (copy whose only change is the file-input selector, since accept now includes video), chatvoice 45/45, reactions 28/28, holdlift 39/39, roundchat 41/41.

**Deploy order:** 1. client (feature-detected: nothing changes before the SQL); 2. Vercel env check (SUPABASE_URL/ANON_KEY or VITE_ ones available to functions; fluid compute on, so maxDuration 300 is accepted; Supabase global upload limit >= 200 MiB, which caddie-media's 200 MB bucket suggests); 3. apply `chat-video-apply.sql` -> check true; 4. deploy `delete-account`; 5. deploy `chat-video-retention --no-verify-jwt` (its daily job is already scheduled by step 3). Native (rebased onto 46e8806): ChatComposer calls `pickChatMediaFiles` (lib/mediaPicker.ts) with kinds image + video, limit 10, 200 MiB video cap (`onMediaTooLarge` toast); 2.5+ builds pick natively in selection order, 2.4 and the web fall back to the file input (`multiple`, accept image/*,video/*). The handler of the tap is kept for the web input's later change event too, so every pick is bound to its chat visit. Visit scoping: the album composer (`useChatMediaAttachment(scope)`) uses the same visit model as 52f95c1's photo attachment (cherry-picked onto this branch): a pick or result arriving after A->B or A->B->A is dropped and its URLs revoked.

**Needs a real iPhone + two accounts:** HEVC/HDR iPhone clips end to end, a 4K clip near 200 MB (upload time, function time), the WKWebView file input for video (iOS may compress on pick), autoplay in Low Power Mode, tap-for-sound, deck swipe vs. list scroll vs. edge-back, trim sheet with the keyboard up, push text, unsend on the other phone, Retry after airplane mode mid-upload.

## Zoomable photos in the fullscreen viewer + your own avatar fullscreen — 2026-11-06 (LIVE: main 7a41fed deployed; web only, no migration, no backend)

- Micky: chat photos couldn't be zoomed like other social apps. Every photo in `FullscreenMediaViewer` (DM + round chat, Community posts, anything else using it) is now a `components/community/ZoomableImage.tsx`: pinch 1x–4x anchored at the fingers, pan while zoomed with edges clamped to the screen, double tap fit <-> 2.5x at the tap, rubber-band overshoot that springs back on release (instant with reduced motion), ctrl+wheel / wheel zoom and mouse-drag pan on a computer. Transform-only on the `<img>` from refs + rAF (no React state per frame). At 1x the slide is `touch-action: pan-x pan-y`, so swipe-between-photos (native scroll-snap) and drag-down-to-close still work; zoomed it's `touch-action: none` + `[data-zoom-active]` (the viewer's dismiss tracking skips it) so a sideways drag pans instead. Safari `gesture*` events are cancelled inside the photo. Resets on slide change and close. Videos unchanged. Pure math in `lib/zoomPan.ts`, tests `tests/zoomPan.test.ts` (in `test:trust`).
- Micky add-on: on your OWN profile, tapping your photo (Me tab header, and the avatar in `AvatarUpload` — the photo sheet and ProfileSetup) opens it fullscreen in the same zoomable viewer with a "Change photo" action (header: opens the photo sheet; AvatarUpload: opens the picker). No photo yet: tap keeps opening the sheet/picker. Other golfers' profiles unchanged. The viewer is on the modal stack now, so Escape over the photo sheet closes only the viewer. New key `avatarUpload.viewPhoto` x6. Note: stored avatars are only 320 px (`resizeImageToSquareBlob`), so zoom on them is soft; Google photos are asked for at 1024 (`largestPhotoUrl`). Raising the stored size is a separate decision (bandwidth on every avatar).
- Headless-checked on a production build with the demo (CDP touch at 390x844). Needs a real iPhone: pinch feel in WKWebView, that Safari never page-zooms, swipe/dismiss hand-off.

## Scan of 7a22d01: stale trim selector, rejected round message, sign-in wording — 2026-11-06 (branch `fix/scan-7a22d01`, first made off 7a22d01, rebased onto origin/main b0621c7 on 2026-11-06; one local commit, not pushed/deployed; no migration, no Edge Function, no dashboard change required)

All three were confirmed still present on 7a22d01 before fixing (headless before/after against a 7a22d01 build), and re-checked against main b0621c7 at rebase time: main's `changeTrim` is unchanged (the filming tips card and the new club picker are separate and kept), and main's round chat still dropped a rejected text message. Launch class: B.
- **Caddie "Change section" stale selector** (`pages/AnalyzeSwing.tsx`, f153572). Root cause: when the preview `<video>` had no length yet, `changeTrim` awaited `readVideoDuration` and then called `setPendingTrim` with the file/URL it closed over, with no check that the draft was still the same; Remove and Ask Caddie stayed enabled during the wait. Remove + late metadata reopened the selector on the removed video. Fix: new `src/lib/trimReopen.ts` (request token gate + draft identity check after the await: same File object AND preview URL; `canStartAnalysis`). While reopening, Remove / Ask Caddie / Change section are disabled (`aria-busy`), `handleAnalyze` itself refuses with the selector open or reopening, Remove and unmount cancel the gate, a stale answer does nothing (no selector, no toast). Cancel in the selector still keeps the current window. Tests: `tests/trimReopen.test.ts` (13; in `test:caddie`).
- **Round chat: content-filter rejection lost the message** (`components/chat/GroupChat.tsx`, `context/RealRoundsContext.tsx`, `lib/roundChat.ts`). Root cause: on `objectionable_content` the context removed the pending bubble and GroupChat's `restoreRejectedText` only put A back if the composer was empty, so with B typed A was gone; it also wrote into whatever composer was on screen (GroupChat is reused across `/messages/round/:id`), so a late answer could put round 1's text into round 2's composer. Fix: a rejected text message is now a failed pending message with `failure: "objectionable"` (`settleRoundSend`, keyed by clientId; nothing in the send path touches a composer). The bubble says "Not sent: it contains language that isn't allowed on GolfMe" with **Edit** and **Discard** (read-only round: Copy + Discard); no Retry, and `retryRoundMessage` / `markRoundMessageRetrying` refuse it (never auto-resent; a second tap while a retry is in flight is refused too). Edit follows the DM rule (`dmFailedSend.failedSendOutcome`, via `editRejectedOutcome`): empty composer with no reply picked = put A (and its quote) back and drop the bubble; otherwise "Replace your draft?" Replace / Copy / Keep draft. The moderation toast only shows if the round it was sent in is still on screen. Network / RLS failures keep Retry. Photo / voice sends (added on main after 7a22d01) keep their own path: a refused photo caption stays as its failed photo bubble with Retry / Discard (toast only, as on main), and `retryRoundMessage` still routes photo/voice jobs to `attemptPhoto` before the text-only guard. 6 new `roundChat.*` keys in all 6 locales. Tests: `tests/roundChatRejected.test.ts` (12; in `test:roundchat`).
- **Sign-in wording** (flow since 8eb645b: 6-digit email code, plus Apple / Google; the email still carries a fallback link). Changed: Terms "Your account" ("You sign in with Google or an email link" -> "Apple, Google, or a one-time code we email you"; a description, not an obligation; the responsibility sentence unchanged); Privacy "How we use it" and the Resend line ("sign-in links" -> "sign-in codes"; same purpose, legal meaning unchanged); `auth.code.linkExpiredBody` in 6 locales now offers a new code instead of "get a new one (link)"; `supabase/templates/README.md` row/test step. LAST_UPDATED dates on Terms (Sep 29) and Privacy (Oct 17) NOT bumped: Micky's call. Left as is (accurate): Support "Signing in", "That sign-in link didn't work" notices (shown only after a link was opened), the email template itself (code first, link fallback). Internal docs still saying "magic link" not edited.
- **Dashboard (none required):** the Supabase "Magic link" / "Confirm signup" templates already match `sign-in-email.html`. Optional, Micky's decision: drop the button + fallback link from both templates to make the email code-only (then the expired-link notices become dead paths).
- **Checks (after the rebase):** `npm run check` passes (900/900 node tests); on a production preview build (placeholder env, demo), scratchpad `roundchat/roundchat.mjs` 41/41 and `chat-images/chatimages-bracket.mjs` 48/48. **Not tested:** a real Supabase content-filter rejection, two real accounts, iPhone keyboard with the Edit button focusing the docked composer (same focus call as swipe-to-reply), Caddie on a real account/iPhone (the WKWebView metadata timing).

## Community notifications: likes + comment pushes — 2026-11-06 (LIVE: main 7a41fed deployed; migration `20261107200000_post_like_and_comment_pushes` APPLIED by Micky 2026-11-06, check true; two-account push test pending)

- Micky: "when someone commented or liked someone's post they should receive the notification."
- **Audit (before):** `notify_community_comment()` (20260816143618, never redefined) writes `post_reply` (top-level comment -> post author) / `comment_reply` (reply -> parent comment's author), never to yourself, and the bell shows it (realtime refetch, `/community/<id>` clears it on open). Gaps: **no phone push for comments at all** (only DMs, joins, round updates, Caddie and the fan-out push); the row was written **even across a block**; text English for everyone. **Likes** (`community_post_votes`, one row per (post, voter), no downvotes) **wrote nothing**. Settings: `push_enabled` is the master switch; `notify_new_rounds` / `notify_circle_posts` exist only for broadcast fan-outs, so no new switch (same as DMs/round updates). No "push only for messages" rule in code; the Settings description "New matches, join requests, and messages." is just stale copy.
- **Migration 20261107200000:** (1) `notify_community_comment()` replaced: same recipients/links, plus `private.has_block_between` skip, bell text by recipient `profiles.language` (6 locales; English sentences unchanged), push via the live `private.send_round_push` (push_enabled + a device; title = commenter, body "Commented on your post: <preview>" / "Replied to your comment: <preview>", preview = `private.fanout_text_preview`); push errors swallowed. (2) New type `post_liked` + `trg_notify_post_liked` (AFTER INSERT on community_post_votes, SECURITY DEFINER, search_path '', no client EXECUTE): "<name> liked your post." -> post author, link `/community/<id>`, not own post, not across a block; push title = liker, body "Liked your post", APNs collapse id `post-like-<post id>`. **Anti-spam: one bell row per (liker, post), ever** — the existing notifications row is the ledger (partial index `notifications_post_liked_dedupe_idx`); unlike keeps it, re-like never re-notifies; clients can't delete notifications so it can't be reset. Aggregation rejected (rewrites rows/unread state on every like). (3) `notifications_type_check` rebuilt from the catalog (current list, same order, + `post_liked`), so it neither depends on nor drops the unapplied booking types; **`20261107150000_booking_status_and_evidence` now also lists `post_liked`** (and its scratchpad apply/rollback were patched; booking rollback keeps `post_liked` only if the likes trigger exists). RLS unchanged. Needs live: 20260924100300, 20261030120000, 20261105130000 (the apply file checks).
- **Client:** `post_liked` type, ThumbsUp badge (`like` kind, fairway green) in `NotificationAvatar` / `lib/notificationInbox`; opening `/community/<id>` already clears it; not in the in-app popup banner (bell + push only). Demo: `demoTogglePostUpvote` mirrors the rule (once per liker per post, not own post, not across a block; English like other demo rows). Harmless before the SQL (nothing writes the type).
- **Paste files (scratchpad):** `post-likes-notify-apply.sql` / `-check.sql` (one boolean) / `-rollback.sql`; harness `pglite/_post_likes.mjs`: 113/113 incl. 10 negative controls, prod-like chain and full chain, booking applied after/before, exact rollback fingerprint. `_bk_full.mjs` (booking) still all pass. `npm run check` (856/856 tests), headless demo on a preview build `likes/likes.mjs` 15/15.
- **Deploy order:** client (any time) -> apply `post-likes-notify-apply.sql` -> check true. No Edge Function redeploy (send-push already accepts `collapse_id`). **Not done:** two real accounts + iPhone: like/comment pushes arrive, tap opens the post, re-like sends nothing, block silences.

Includes the one-tap chat photo picker: app-local `ios/App/App/MediaPickerPlugin.swift` (PHPicker, selection 1, no library permission, metadata-free JPEG) + `src/lib/chatMediaPicker.ts` (native only when `GolfMeMediaPicker` is available, else the old file input, so 2.3/web are unchanged); `@capacitor/camera` was rejected (needs "Done" + full library access). The web view, its scroll view and the window get a dynamic page colour (cream / `#0d1210`). Web: `src/lib/nativeAppearance.ts`, called from `useAppearance.apply()`. ## Course overview: speed, layout fallback bug, privacy — 2026-11-07 (LIVE: main 2afd0c9 deployed; migration `20261107190000_course_imagery_detail_cache` APPLIED by Micky 2026-11-06, check true; course-photos v2 deployed)

A review of 116c441 (drone v2).

- **Measured on main (headless, demo, real data)**:
  - Tiles are not the slow part: the map shows in ~1.0–1.6 s.
  - The picture was downloaded **twice** per view (Image preload + MapLibre's own fetch).
  - A cold NAIP export takes **1.5–20 s, bimodal**. It is independent of size: 512 px is no faster than 2048, PNG is 10× the bytes, and four parallel quadrants finish with the slowest one. A warm export takes ~0.1 s (CloudFront, 12 h, per edge).
  - Overpass: overpass-api.de answered in 10–37 s or a fast 502. maps.mail.ru sometimes took 18–50 s or returned a 504.
  - **Bug reproduced** (unit + real browser): a fast HTTP 200 carrying `remark: "runtime error: Query timed out…"` was parsed as "no layout", won `Promise.any`, aborted the slower good answer, and was cached as null for 30 days (localStorage v1 key).
  - With every tile failing, the overview gave up even though the picture had loaded. The no-WebGL still view did the same.
- **Fixes (client)**:
  - `lib/courseDetailImagery.ts` rewritten. The picture is downloaded once (`fetch` → blob: URL shared by the overview and the still view, `priority: "low"` so tiles go first).
  - `classifyOverpassAnswer` + `settleLayoutAttempts` (`_shared/courseLayout.ts`): an error remark, non-JSON or HTML response, or HTTP error is "invalid" (transient). "unmapped" only counts after every endpoint finished; the first "ok" wins.
  - Layout cache: ok 30 days, unmapped 7 days. Transient failures are not cached and wait 2 minutes before the next ask, except a Layout off/on, which retries at once. The cache moved to v2 and the old v1 keys are deleted.
  - **maps.mail.ru removed**: the direct path (demo, or before the function is deployed) uses overpass-api.de only.
  - The layout is requested only while the overview shows with Layout on. The switch is remembered per device (`golfme:overviewLayout`), so "off" means no request on later course pages too.
  - The status line shows "Loading…", "Course layout isn't available for this course yet." or "Couldn't load… turn Layout off and on" (×6 locales).
  - Tiles-all-fail rescue: the overview waits up to 20 s for the picture before falling back. The still view keeps the picture when its tiles fail.
- **Fixes (server, prepared)**:
  - course-photos takes `{courseIds:[id], picture:true}` or `{…, layout:true}`, one course per request.
  - **Picture**: rendered once per course with a hedge (a second, 5 cm-nudged request after 4 s; the first valid JPEG wins). It is checked (content-type, 20 KB–3 MB, JPEG magic) and stored in the public `course-imagery` bucket at `naip/v1/<id>/<w>x<h>-<tag>.jpg` (cache 1 year, path versioned by box). Every later viewer gets it warm from Storage, and the browser never renders it.
  - **Layout**: overpass-api.de only, with a GolfMe User-Agent. Stored in `course_imagery.layout` (ok re-checked after 90 days, unmapped after 7); transient failures are never stored.
  - Caps: `overpass_layout` 40/day, `usgs_export` 300/day. Shared code moved to `_shared/naipExport.ts` and `_shared/usCoverage.ts`.
  - An old function (no `pictures`/`layouts` in its answer), or none at all, falls back to the direct path. A server "none" or "transient" never triggers a direct request behind its back.
- **Overpass policy** (OSM wiki): usage counts across all of an app's users; regular use should stay ≈100 queries / 10 MB a day; send a User-Agent; pause after 429/406; commercial use should self-host or pay. The server-side once-per-course store keeps GolfMe far below that; the 116c441 per-device, two-endpoint race did not. FairwayMapper (golf-focused Overpass, free tier for OSM contributors) is an option if volume grows.
- **Migration 20261107190000** (needs 20261106140000 applied first):
  - Changes: course_imagery `layout`/`layout_status`/`layout_checked_at`/`naip_*` with null-safe shape checks; the public bucket (3 MB, JPEG only) with no storage policies, so only the service role writes. Access to course_imagery is unchanged (service role only).
  - PGlite: 34/34 after replaying 103 earlier migrations, including apply-guard, check, rollback-exact-schema, re-apply and a negative control. The first run caught NULL-passing checks, fixed with coalesce/num_nonnulls.
  - Paste files are in the session scratchpad: `overview-speed/imagery-detail-{apply,check,rollback}.sql`.
- **Checks**:
  - Tests: new `tests/overviewDetail.test.ts` (17) and 8 new handler tests (picture store/serve, failures, hedge, US/cap, layout store, error-remark transient, unmapped 7-day recheck, one course per request).
  - tsc, handler strict tsc, lint 0 errors (21 warnings), node 768/768, build, imagery suite 38/38.
  - Headless behaviour: 9/9 on the fix, while main fails 4 of the same checks.
  - After the fix: 1 picture request per view (was 2), only overpass-api.de.
- **Deploy order**: SQL `20261106140000` (already APPLIED by Micky 2026-11-06; course-photos v1 already deployed) → `20261107190000` → deploy course-photos (`deno check` first) → client. The client alone is safe at any point: it falls back to the direct path.
- **Open / needs iPhone**: GPU memory for a 2048-px picture (≈12–16 MB texture), the fade during orbit, Storage egress (~0.15–0.7 MB per view), and whether the status pill should auto-hide.

- **Chat voice toggle, 2026-11-06 (client only, not pushed/device-verified)**: tapping the keyboard icon to leave voice mode now just shows the textarea, unfocused, so the keyboard stays down until the user taps the textarea (ChatComposer `toggleVoice`, `flushSync` + `focus()` removed; DM + GroupChat page/card verified headless, chatvoice.mjs 45/45).

## Voice recording lifecycle fix — 2026-11-06 (local commit on the golfme-architect worktree branch, on top of main 69238aa; not pushed/deployed; client only, no migration; NOT device-verified)

Micky's review brief: at 69238aa a late microphone answer could become the wrong hold's recording.
- **Root cause (confirmed at 69238aa)**: HoldToTalk accepted any getUserMedia result while `state.kind === "starting"`, with no attempt identity. So: hold A, release, hold B, A answers → A's stream became B's recording (the stale attempt is what gets sent). If B was then denied, `voiceStep("failed")` reset to idle and A's microphone stayed live (recRef was never discarded). Separately, `voiceRecorder.ts` called `recorder.start()` outside any try, so a throw there leaked the tracks and the AudioContext. Its `release()` was not idempotent (a second onstop closed the context twice), `discard()` only released on a later onstop, and a stop whose onstop never came held the microphone forever.
- **Fix**:
  - New `lib/voiceSession.ts` (pure, node-testable) owns every attempt. Each press gets its own id and AbortController. Only the current, still-live attempt may become the recording, change state, notify or deliver. Release, cancel, too-short, interrupt, a newer press and dispose all end the attempt. A late answer is released by the recorder itself (signal checked after the await), and the session discards it again if it arrives anyway. A stale failure changes nothing. A released clip is delivered at most once.
  - `voiceRecorder.ts`: one idempotent, throw-proof `release()` (handlers nulled, recorder stopped, every track stopped, context closed) on every exit, including when the constructor or `start()` throws. `discard()` releases at once. `stop()` has a 4 s watchdog: it releases and reports "failed", and sends nothing.
  - `voiceStep("failed")` now only acts from `starting`.
  - HoldToTalk wires the session, created in the effect so StrictMode gets a fresh one. Interrupts come from visibilitychange, pagehide and the Capacitor App `pause` event (not `appStateChange`, whose resign-active fires for the permission alert itself).
  - DataContext `sendDirectVoice` / `sendRoundVoice` claim each clip once (`claimVoiceClip`, demo + real).
  - ChatComposer / DirectMessageThread / keyboard hooks are not touched.
- **Tests**: new `tests/voiceSession.test.ts` (26, in `test:chat`) runs the real recorder + session against deferred getUserMedia promises and fake MediaStream / MediaRecorder / AudioContext. It asserts track.stop / context.close counts and send counts. It covers both race orders, late grant + denial, a stale failure while B is active, release / unmount / background while pending and while recording, start / constructor / meter / track-stop / recorder-stop throwing, the watchdog, late callbacks, and a normal send once. 18 of the 26 fail against a transcription of the 69238aa logic. `npm run check` 851/851. Headless on a production build (`vite preview`): `chatvoice.mjs` 43/43. `chatimages.mjs` is 42/48 only because its preview expectations are stale since 2afd0c9 (`📷 Photo` → `[Photo]`); a copy expecting `[Photo]` passes 48/48.
- **Not done**: no physical-device or two-account test; the live DB state of `20261107180000` was not checked (no DB access).

## Voice messages in chat (DMs + round chats, WeChat-style) — 2026-11-06 (LIVE: main 69238aa + lifecycle fix 6c79426 deployed; migration `20261107180000_chat_voice` APPLIED by Micky 2026-11-06, check true; no Edge Function redeploy; iPhone two-account checks pending)

Micky (after photos): a bottom-left composer toggle turns the text box into "Hold to talk": hold records, release sends, slide up cancels, timer + level meter, 60 s max, haptics; voice bubbles with play/pause + progress + length, react / reply / unsend like text, "[Voice message]" previews.
- **Client**: `components/chat/HoldToTalk.tsx` (touch events, not pointer events: WebKit fires pointercancel on a finger held still; touchcancel / app hidden / leaving = discard, never send), state machine `lib/chatVoice.ts voiceStep` (press → starting → recording; release before the mic answers = abort; < 0.7 s = "too short"; slide up ≥ 72 px arms cancel; 60 s = sent automatically; last 10 s count down), `lib/voiceRecorder.ts` (getUserMedia + MediaRecorder 64 kb/s + AnalyserNode level), `components/chat/VoiceMessage.tsx` (one plays at a time; width grows with length, fixed from the stored duration so nothing moves), `lib/useVoiceComposerProps.ts`. ChatComposer: mic/keyboard toggle as the row's first button (36 px, row height measured unchanged); the bar covers the textarea's own box (textarea kept, `invisible`, in a plain flex wrapper; autosize / mirror / ResizeObserver code untouched). Send path = the photo machinery (upload first, then the row with the device-made id; Not sent · Retry / Discard). Copy hidden on voice. Feature-detected twice: the backend (a select of audio_path) and the browser (getUserMedia + MediaRecorder + a recordable type; otherwise no mic button). Mic denied → toast explaining Settings.
- **Format**: AAC in MP4 (`.m4a`, `audio/mp4;codecs=mp4a.40.2`) wherever MediaRecorder can make it (iPhone Safari / WKWebView, Safari on Mac, Chrome/Edge with AAC), because every iPhone plays it; else WebM/Opus (Firefox, Chromium without AAC — the headless shell records `audio/webm;codecs=opus`). iOS 17.4+ / Safari 18 play WebM/Opus; an older iPhone shows "Can't play here" (no retry loop). No transcoding (would mean a ~25 MB WebAssembly encoder for a minority of senders). Nothing on the server processes, transcodes or transcribes recordings.
- **Migration `20261107180000_chat_voice`** (separate from photos so photos ship / roll back alone and keep their reviewed paste files): bucket types += audio/mp4, audio/webm (still private, 2 MiB); `audio_path` + `audio_duration_ms` (1..61000) on messages + round_messages, folded into the single text check (voice = no text, no photo, own `<scope>/<id>.m4a|.webm` path); triggers `check_*_audio` (object must exist owned by the sender, authz first; audio only clearable); re-created with audio added: `private.can_upload_chat_image`, `chat_image_in_use`, `chat_image_reported`, the `chat_media_select_visible` policy, both unsend functions (copy stays text only), `notify_new_message` (bell "<name> sent a voice message.", push "<name> sent a voice message"); `admin_list_report_chat_voice` (admin dashboard plays reported voice).
