## Tutorial Appearance step + sharper drone overview (v1) — 2026-11-06 (LIVE on the user's "ship"; v2 below LIVE 2026-11-06 on Micky's "yes live"; no migration, no Edge Function deploy needed)

- **Tutorial: light/dark mode** (Micky: "include the light and dark mode in tutorial from now on"). It is now step 5 of 5: Play → course map → Chat → Caddie → **Settings → Appearance**. It uses `APPEARANCE_TUTORIAL_ID` on the AppearancePicker card and route `/settings`, the same pattern as 999f548's course-map step. Settings is a lazy page, so TutorialOverlay now makes two more bounded measure attempts (800 and 1600 ms), only while no target has been found. A spotlight already placed is never re-scrolled. Strings `tutorial.appearance.title/text` ×6.
- **Who sees it**: same as 999f548. Completion is one boolean (`profiles.onboarding_tutorial_completed`), with no step list or version stored. Accounts that already finished or skipped the tour are **not** shown it again. Only accounts that never completed the tour (new real accounts) see the new step. There is no Replay Tutorial entry in Settings, despite the TutorialContext comment saying there is, so demo never runs the tour. Checked headless on demo: the target is on screen after the overlay's scrollIntoView at 375×667, 390×844 and 1280×800.
- **Drone overview, sharper (free fix)**. The root cause was a unit mismatch. `overviewFraming`/`fitZoom` use 256 px tile zoom, but that value went to MapLibre unchanged, and MapLibre's zoom is 1 lower for the same scale. So the camera sat a whole level closer than its framing meant: Forest Park was at tile zoom ~16.7, with z16 NAIP scaled 1.6× (≈5× device px at DPR 3). Pitch 60 added stretch on top. The fix:
  - `lib/droneOrbit`: `maplibreZoom()` (−1).
  - `overviewZoomCap()`: the source's max + 0.3, so USGS ≤ 16.3 and MapTiler keeps 17.5. `overviewFraming(…, maxZoom)` frames the outline up to that cap, and map `maxZoom` is the same cap, so pinching in can't go past it either.
  - `OVERVIEW_PITCH` 60 → 45.
  - `overviewTileSize()`: on DPR ≥ 1.5, USGS tiles are declared 128 px, so MapLibre requests the next zoom up and draws it at native density. MapTiler stays at 256 because it bills per tile.
  - Reduced motion: still view, unchanged.
  - Effect: the view is wider. The whole outline is now in frame, which is what the framing intended.
- **Thumbnails**: `tileZoomFor` now upscales tiles at most 1.4× (`MAX_TILE_UPSCALE`) past the source's max. Before, it could scale up to 2^1.5 at the 17.5 framing cap.
- **Checks**:
  - Tests: new `tests/droneOrbit.test.ts` (7) in test:courses and `tests/tutorialSteps.test.ts` (4) in test:onboarding. `courseBounds.test.ts` deep-zoom assertion 1024 → 358.4 px.
  - tsc, lint (0 errors, 21 warnings = main), node 648/648, build OK.
  - imagery suite 38/38, with no assertions changed.
  - Before/after on real USGS tiles at 390×844, DPR 3: scratchpad `drone-sharp/` (before: z15/z16 tiles overzoomed; after: same tile zooms covering a wider, sharper view).
- **Needs an iPhone look**: is the wider framing the right drone feel, or should `OVERVIEW_TILT_ZOOM` go up a little? Also check orbit smoothness with the extra 128 px tile requests.

### v2 (LIVE 2026-11-06): sharp NAIP picture + OSM course layout (Micky: "even more detailed… the layout more visible and authentic"; $0, no keys, no migration applied)

- **Sharp picture** (`src/lib/naipExport.ts`): one `exportImage` from the USGS **NAIP Plus ImageServer** (`https://imagery.nationalmap.gov/arcgis/rest/services/USGSNAIPPlus/ImageServer/exportImage`).
  - Terms: public domain (USGS/USDA, "USGS, USDA, The National Map: Orthoimagery"). The existing USGS credit is shown. No key needed and `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *` is sent.
  - Service: 0.3 m pixel mosaic, max 4000×4000, Web Mercator. The cached tiles go no deeper than z16 (~1.8 m in NYC).
  - The request covers the course's outline box plus 15% on each side, in EPSG:3857. The box is grown so its aspect exactly matches the pixel size. The long side is 2048 px at DPR ≥ 2 and 1024 px otherwise (Forest Park ≈ 0.5 m/px).
  - The URL is deterministic per course and screen class.
- **Measured 2026-11-06**:
  - Cold render: **11–44 s** (any size).
  - Repeat of the same URL: **~0.03–0.2 s**. CloudFront serves it with `max-age=43200`, and the browser caches it too.
  - Size: 0.3–0.7 MB per picture.
  - So tiles always show first. The picture is a MapLibre image source over the z16 tiles and under the layout: added at once if it arrives within 3 s, faded in over 700 ms if later, dropped after 60 s (`detailReveal`).
  - The same picture shows in the still view (`SatelliteTiles` `detail`, course page Satellite tab only), placed with `imageRectInView`.
  - It is never requested in lists, and at most once per course view (`loadDetailPicture` memo).
- **OSM layout** (`supabase/functions/_shared/courseLayout.ts`, pure, reusable server-side): golf=fairway/green/bunker/tee/(lateral_)water_hazard ways + multipolygon outers, and golf=hole lines (ref → hole number, "Yellow - 7" → 7).
  - Multi-course facilities are split by `golf:course:name`. Each area goes to its nearest hole, so Bethpage Black shows only Black's 18.
  - Shapes are simplified to ~1 m (Bethpage Black 68 KB, Forest Park 18 KB).
  - Drawing: greens lit, white outlines, dashed hole lines, and 16 px hole-number markers at the tees. A **Layout** toggle (default on, top-right, strings ×6) controls it.
  - When our course's holes cover < 60% of the outline (multi-course facility), the camera glides onto them over 1.4 s. The orbit holds during the glide and continues the bearing. Under reduced motion the camera jumps instead. It never reframes once the golfer has touched the map.
- **Layout fetch** (`src/lib/courseDetailImagery.ts`): sent from the browser to both Overpass endpoints at once (overpass-api.de, maps.mail.ru); the first good answer wins and the other is aborted. 50 s timeout.
  - Cached per device in localStorage for 30 days (max 12 courses). Failures are not cached, and each course is asked once per session.
  - Measured: 10–35 s per answer, with occasional 502/504 from either endpoint.
  - **Server-side cache needs a migration** (jsonb `course_imagery.layout`), not applied. Proposed SQL is in the session scratchpad (`drone-sharp/PROPOSED_20261107120000_add_course_imagery_layout.sql`), and it also needs a course-photos change + deploy.
- **Checks**:
  - Tests: new `tests/naipExport.test.ts` (9) and `tests/courseLayout.test.ts` (12, including fetch race/cache/storage-failure and picture timeout/retry with stubs), both in test:courses.
  - node 669/669, tsc, lint 0 errors (21 warnings = main), build, imagery suite 38/38 (no assertion changes).
  - Real-data before/after (Forest Park, Van Cortlandt, Bethpage Black): scratchpad `drone-sharp/v2-*`.
- **Risks / open**:
  - The first viewer of a course per ~12 h waits 10–45 s for the sharp picture (tiles meanwhile).
  - Overpass is slow and flaky. maps.mail.ru gets the viewer's IP.
  - Every device fetches the layout itself until the server-side cache exists.
  - Hole labels can overlap at tight tee clusters (no collision handling).
  - Bethpage's picture covers the whole facility (1.6 m/px) because it is requested before the layout is known.
  - Still view: past the 15% pad the blurrier tiles show at the edges.
  - iPhone: GPU memory for a 2048² texture, and the fade/glide during orbit.

## Caddie renders entirely in the Caddie feedback language — 2026-11-05 (branch `fix/caddie-language-scope` on 1453a88, uncommitted/not deployed; no migration, no Edge Function change)

- **Why**: Micky picked 简体中文 as Caddie's feedback language: AI text was Chinese, but all Caddie UI stayed English. "The language switcher should switch all languages within Caddie", "all parts within Caddie should be fully translated".
- **Approach**: `LocaleScope` (i18n/LocaleContext.tsx) is a nested provider that overrides only `locale`/`t` for its subtree. It loads the dictionary on demand, keeps the surrounding language until that load finishes (no English flash, never remounts), passes the parent's `setLocale` through, and never writes `golfme:locale`. Its pure rules are in `i18n/translate.ts`. `components/caddie/CaddieLocaleScope` mounts it with `useCaddieFeedbackLanguage()` around the three Caddie routes in App.tsx and the Caddie panel of RootTabCarousel. The top bar, bottom/side nav, Home's Caddie card, the bell/push notifications and every other screen keep the app language.
- **Decisions**: toasts raised from Caddie screens use the Caddie language, because their text comes from the scoped `t` and they are about what is on screen. Toasts and notifications raised outside Caddie stay in the app language. Dates on Caddie screens follow the scope too (the list and the detail use `formatShortDate(…, scoped locale)`).
- **Strings**: almost everything was already keyed; the gap was the scope. Newly localized:
  - Request errors: the English sentences from analyze-swing/translate-caddie-analysis used to be toasted verbatim. `lib/caddieErrorText.ts` maps every one of them, plus the daily-limit regex, codes and Storage errors, to keys. Anything unknown gets a translated generic line.
  - The "Share to Community" caption (`caddie.shareCaption`, was `${swingType} swing`).
  - The demo's fixture feedback (`caddie.demo.*` via `demoLegacyFeedback`).
  - es: "Feedback"/"coach" replaced (`caddie.shareFeedback`, `caddie.feedbackPostTitle`, `caddie.thinking4`, `coachReplay.title`, `coachReplay.coachMode`) and `swingAnalysis.followThrough` → "Terminación".
  - 26 new keys ×6.
- **Translate button**: `translateOffer()` (lib/caddieLanguage). When the recorded language equals the Caddie language, no button (it used to show "Translate to 简体中文" on Chinese feedback). A different recorded language shows the notice + Translate. A row with no recorded language shows the plain button. There is no "Show original": a translation overwrites the stored text.
- **Still not translated**: Spanish keeps the golf loanwords Swing/Driver/Wedge/Backswing/Downswing, which are standard in Spanish golf (Micky to confirm). "AI" on the cap logo, "Caddie"/"GolfMe", "MB" and the language picker's native names stay as they are. Server-side follow-up (not built): give every analyze-swing/translate-caddie-analysis error a stable `code` so the client stops matching English sentences. A kill-switch refusal is already coded, but RealCaddieContext localizes it in the app language before throwing. caddieErrorText re-reads the code, so Caddie screens still show it in the Caddie language.
- Tests: `tests/caddieLocale.test.ts` (13, in test:caddie):
  - the scope overrides `t` without touching the parent/app locale;
  - Caddie routes are wrapped;
  - new keys ×6 with matching placeholders, and every key a Caddie file uses is translated;
  - a strict source scan of every Caddie file for JSX text, attributes, toasts, `err.message` and English literals, with a self-test;
  - every server error sentence is mapped;
  - translateOffer and the demo feedback.
- Totals: 598/598 node tests, tsc, lint (0 errors) and build all pass. Headless demo probe (`scratchpad/caddie-lang/probe.mjs`; demo-caddie-2 patched with fixture details/pose/phases/cues and a generated webm, plus processing/failed/other-language rows) covered app en + Caddie zh-CN, app en + Caddie es, and app zh-CN + Caddie en. Across 24 screens (list, analyze, processing, failed, legacy demo + demo Coach Replay cards, detail, Slow Mode, Swing Check per phase, Coach Replay cards, other-language notice, after share), the DOM text contained no English UI string; the nav kept the app language and `golfme:locale` was untouched. **Not tested**: physical iPhone, real accounts (real translate/analyze errors).

## Dark mode (Settings → Appearance) — 2026-11-06 (LIVE on Micky's "ship dark"; default "Match iPhone" (keeps the app matching the iPhone-driven status bar); no migration, no backend, no native change)

Micky approved a full dark mode: Light / Dark / Match iPhone, per device.
- **Setting**: Settings → App → Appearance (3-option radio group; "Match device" off iOS). `localStorage golfme:appearance` = light | dark | system, **default `system`** (`DEFAULT_APPEARANCE` in `src/lib/appearance.ts`; set it to `"light"` to make dark opt-in). Live: applies at once, follows the OS change in system mode, other tabs (storage event). Sets `<html class="dark">`, `color-scheme` and `<meta name="theme-color">` (light keeps `#166534`, dark `#0d1210`). Strings ×6 (`settings.appearance*`).
- **No flash**: an inline script in `index.html` (after the gm-app/gm-static-landing guard and the gm-entry-boot script) applies the same rules before first paint; `html.dark` paints `#0d1210`. EntryOverlay's `gm-entry-boot` green still wins. Same key/values/default/colour pinned by `tests/appearance.test.ts`.
- **Landing stays light**: `gm-static-landing` skips dark, and `Welcome` holds light while mounted (`useForceLightAppearance`, layout effect); sign-in and every app screen follow the setting.
- **How it recolours (no per-file dark: classes)**: `src/index.css` tokens. Tailwind v4 resolves a colour in the utility's own namespace first (`--background-color-*`, `--text-color-*`, `--border-color-*`, `--ring-color-*`), so `:root.dark` flips e.g. `bg-white` (surface `#161d19`) while `text-white` stays white. Slate scale inverted (green-tinted); pinned: `bg-slate-500…950` (scrims, tooltips, video), `text-slate-50/100`, slate shadows, `text-fairway-50…300`. Fairway/sun/red/rose/amber/sky/violet/teal: 50/100/200 tints become hue-in-surface mixes, 600–900 text lightens; solid fills (buttons, mine bubble, gold CTAs) unchanged; brand-forest button gradient lifted one step in dark. New tokens: `bg-page` (replaces every in-app `bg-[#faf9f6]`), `fixed-white` (white that never flips: glass on green/video, toggle thumbs, tutorial ring, carousel dots). Opacity modifiers compile to `color-mix(var())` under `@supports` (light fallback below iOS 16.2). `dark:` variant now follows the class (`@custom-variant`).
- **Hand-tuned**: GolfMeLoader scene + CourseHero placeholder at dusk (dark: fill classes), DM hold menu (stronger dim, raised menu surface), Apple sign-in button white on dark, logo chip ring, MapLibre attribution/zoom chrome, ErrorBoundary now follows the setting via tokens, edge-swipe page colour. Map imagery, course photos/aerials, Caddie video, crest artwork untouched. Frozen files (DirectMessageThread, ChatComposer, keyboard hooks, BottomNav, ios/**) and the booking-status files (GolfCallCard, round detail, join sheet) not edited: tokens recolour them.
- **Checks**: tsc, lint (21 warnings = main), node 590/590 (new `tests/appearance.test.ts`, 6, in `test:trust`), build ("[prerender] injected"). Headless (scratchpad `dark/`): 31 screens 390x844 + 8 at 1280x800 in both modes; **light pixel-identical to 7c1d3de on 30/30 comparable screens** (landing differs only by its animation); in-page AA audit of dark: no new failures (remaining: white on fairway-600 3.3:1 mine bubble/Great Match, white on fairway-500 like pill 2.3:1 — identical in light, pre-existing); no leftover light surfaces. `live.mjs` 15/15 (live switch, system follow, reload, other tab, landing light → sign-in dark, blocked storage). Existing suites in light: reactions 28/28, holdlift 39/39, mapshots hold OK.
- **iPhone checks pending**: native status bar text follows the iPhone, not the app (Dark on a light iPhone = dark text on dark; needs a StatusBar plugin or native change, not done); `capacitor.config.ts` backgroundColor is still cream (rubber-band/keyboard edges may show it in dark); WKWebView `prefers-color-scheme` live change; keyboard appearance; safe areas.

## Tap haptics + press feel — 2026-11-05 (branch `feat/tap-haptics` off origin/main 1453a88, uncommitted/not deployed; golfme-architect; no migration, no backend, no native change)

Micky: "buttons like inbox should have haptic feedback when I click too, make it satisfying to click."
- **`lib/tapFeedback.ts`**: `tapHaptic(kind)`: `tap` = Haptics impact Light, `selection` = selection tick (the plugin's iOS `selectionChanged` is a no-op without `selectionStart`, so the first tick starts the generator once), `medium` = impact Medium, `success` = notification Success. Native only (`Capacitor.isNativePlatform()`); **web does nothing** (iOS Safari has no vibrate; per-tap `navigator.vibrate` on Android is noise; the swipe-to-reply/hold gestures keep their own vibrate fallback). Never throws, rejections swallowed; throttled (taps/selection share 80 ms, medium/success share 400 ms, so the Join tap and the "joined" success both land). No app "reduce haptics" setting exists; iOS Settings > Sounds & Haptics > System Haptics off silences UIFeedbackGenerator, so it is respected for free.
- **One delegated listener** (`installTapFeedback()` in `main.tsx`): document click capture reads the haptic from the pre-click state and arms a one-off listener on the tappable; it fires only if the click reaches it. So the DM hold menu's capture-phase swallow of the lifting finger's click never buzzes, keyboard/programmatic clicks (detail 0) never buzz, a scroll never produces a click. Opt-in by `data-haptic="<kind>"` (`"none"` opts out); `role=tab|radio` default to `selection` and skip when already selected (also `aria-pressed`/`aria-current`), `role=switch|menuitemradio` default to `tap`.
- **Wired**: shared `Button` (`haptic` prop, default `tap`; covers Join/Host/Post/confirm sheets and ~150 call sites), bottom-nav tabs (`selection`, none on the open tab; drag keeps its own tick; attribute only, no layout change), bell + avatar menu (`tap`), Inbox DM/round rows (`tap`), Inbox/CourseMedia tabs + CourseThumb Photo/Satellite (`selection`, via role), Find Rounds/Golfers + Caddie Original/Slow (`selection` + `aria-pressed` added), Toggle/CoachModeSwitch (`tap`, via role), reaction emojis (`tap`, via role, no edit to MessageActionsMenu) and my-reaction pill (`tap`), DM/round-chat send (`medium`, attribute only: no handler, focus/keyboard untouched). Success: `useJoinRound` after the join resolves, `CreateGolfCall` after demo/real host, `CreatePost` after publish.
- **Press feel** (`cardStyles.ts`): `PRESS_SCALE_CLASS` `active:scale-[0.97] active:duration-75` on Button and the avatar pill, `ICON_PRESS_CLASS` 0.92 on the bell, `CARD_PRESS_CLASS` 0.985 on Inbox rows; release springs back on the element's own 200 ms ease-out. Tailwind v4 `scale` is its own property, so it composes with Button's `hover:-translate-y-px active:translate-y-0`. `motion-reduce:active:scale-100`. Deliberately NOT on `CLICKABLE_CARD_CLASS`: `:active` matches ancestors, so post/round cards would shrink whenever a button inside them is pressed (and toggle a stacking context on playing video, see fca9a96).
- **Checks**: `tests/tapFeedback.test.ts` (9: mapping, web no-op, throttle windows, no throw, element resolution, capture swallow, detail 0); tsc, lint (0 errors), node tests 594/594, build OK. Headless (prod build + `vite preview`, demo, 390x844 touch): 30/30 incl. one haptic per tap, none on the open tab / selected segment / scroll / keyboard Enter / hold-menu lift, Join = tap, tap, success, send = medium with the textarea still focused, scale 0.97/0.92/0.985 while pressed with zero layout change, no scale under reduced motion. Existing suites: reactions 28/28, hold-lift 39/39.
- **Needs a physical iPhone**: the actual feel (Light vs selection strength, success after Join/Host/Post, Medium per send), whether `:active` scale shows on quick taps in WKWebView and doesn't flicker when a scroll starts on an Inbox row.

## Caddie upload: "Change section" for trimmed clips — 2026-11-05 (LIVE on Micky's "ship"; no migration, no backend change)

- Micky: "after selecting the section of a long video they should have the option to go back and change it." Under the video on Caddie > Upload a swing video, the "0:07 – 0:17 selected" line now has a **Change section** button (scissors icon, 44 px tall). It shows only for clips that were trimmed (longer than 10 s) and is disabled while uploading/analyzing.
- It reopens `VideoTrimSelector` on the window already chosen (new optional `initialStart`/`initialEnd` props). **Use This Clip** writes the new window into the draft. **Cancel** keeps the old one. In this "editing" mode the preview URL belongs to the draft, so cancel never revokes it. Ask Caddie is disabled while the selector is open. The draft doesn't store the clip length, so it is read from the preview `<video>` (or `readVideoDuration` as a fallback). If neither gives a length, a toast says so (`caddie.trimChangeError`).
- `src/lib/trimWindow.ts` `initialTrimWindow()` clamps the reopened window to the clip: at most 10 s, at least 2 s, inside [0, duration], with no NaN. Tests: `tests/trimWindow.test.ts`. New keys `caddie.trimChange` and `caddie.trimChangeError` in all 6 locales. These are plain `t()` keys, so they also work under the pending Caddie `LocaleScope`.
- How the window reaches analyze-swing (`startSeconds`/`endSeconds`) and the persisted-job flow are unchanged.
- Checked headless on the production build (390 px, reduced motion; demo gate patched out in flight for the test only, nothing uploaded). Steps: 25 s clip, picked 0:07–0:17, Change section reopened on 0:07–0:17, Cancel kept it, picked 0:00–0:10 and the label updated.

## Bell cleanup + notification avatars — 2026-11-05 (LIVE on Micky's "ship bell after"; no migration, no backend change)

Micky: "a tapped notification should clear out of the bell so it doesn't stack up" and "internal notifications should include the person's profile picture, it looks preliminary".
- **Bell** (`NotificationsPanel.tsx`): unread rows are the list; read ones move into a collapsed "Earlier (N)" section (tap to expand, newest 50, `EARLIER_LIMIT`). "Mark all as read" kept (shown while anything is unread); with nothing unread a "You're all caught up." line sits above Earlier. Main already hid read rows; what stacked up was every other notification for the same thing (5 messages from one golfer = 5 rows, tapping one cleared one).
- **Opening the thing clears it** (`NotificationRouteClearer`, mounted once in AppShell; no frozen file touched): a DM thread `/messages/<golferId>` clears that golfer's `new_message` rows (and anything linking there); `/golf-calls/<id>` and its chat `/messages/round/<id>` clear that round's rows (both links); any other detail screen (`/community/<id>`, `/caddie/<id>`, `/golfer/<id>`) clears rows whose link path is exactly it. Tabs/lists and `new`/`analyze`/`guidelines` screens never clear. Rows that arrive while you're on the screen are cleared too; only while the page is visible (a background browser tab on a thread clears nothing). Works however it was opened (bell, banner, push, Inbox). Rules in `src/lib/notificationInbox.ts`.
- **Batched mark-read**: `markNotificationsRead(ids)` (DataContext + RealSocialContext): one `update notifications set read, read_at where user_id = me and read = false and id in (...)` (chunks of 100). Allowed by the existing `notifications_update_own` RLS policy, so **no migration**. Real rows are shown read at once via a local overlay (`withLocallyRead`), so the bell dot and list update before the refetch and a refetch already in flight can't flip them back; a failed update takes them back out. `markNotificationRead(id)` (BottomNav/SideNav/Caddie detail, unchanged callers) now goes through it; "Mark all read" is also optimistic. Demo: one state change.
- **Avatars** (`NotificationAvatar.tsx`, bell rows + the in-app banner): the actor's `Avatar` (photo, else initials), else a GolfMe-mark avatar (system types like Caddie, no actor, unresolvable/deleted actor), plus a small type badge (message, comment, round, round alert, follow, Caddie, post, booking; colour per kind, per-type glyph). Actor profiles come from what RealSocialContext already batch-fetches for every notification actor (`fetchPublicProfiles` / golfer_public_profiles, blocked-either-way excluded); nothing new is fetched. A blocked actor never shows a photo (initials if a stale profile is cached, else the GolfMe mark). `new_round_nearby`: the server already clears a hidden host's actor_id; the client also shows a person only if the host is in the public directory (`visibleGolfers`), covering rows from before that trigger. The avatar is decorative inside the row/banner button, so tapping it does what the row does. Banner tap now also marks its row read.
- Strings ×6: `notifications.earlier`, `notifications.caughtUp`, `notifications.unread` (the unread dot's label, was hard-coded English).
- Banner (Micky 2026-11-05: the text was cut off with "…"): the line now wraps to 2 lines (`line-clamp-2`) instead of truncating. Wording stays "sent you a message" (Micky tried "text", then chose to keep "message").
- Also fixed `test:onboarding`'s missing space (authSession + onboardingIntent tests now run under it).
- **Checks**: `npx tsc -b`, `npm run lint` (no new warnings), node 490/490 (new `tests/notificationInbox.test.ts`, 16, in `test:trust`), build with dummy env ("[prerender] injected"). Headless demo (scratchpad `bell/bell.mjs`) 50/50 at 390x844 and 1280x800: seeded notifications (photo, initials, blocked, Caddie, cleared/listed nearby host), Earlier collapsed/expanded, avatar tap opens the round and the row leaves the list, opening a DM clears both of that golfer's rows, opening a round clears its alert, Mark all read + dot gone, banner with the actor's photo and avatar tap opens the thread, no overflow, no page errors. **Not tested**: real accounts against Supabase (the filtered update, realtime refetch after it, actor profiles from golfer_public_profiles), push-opened threads on a physical iPhone.

## DM hold focus (Instagram/Telegram-style lift) + neutral reaction pill — 2026-11-05 (LIVE on Micky's "ship hold"; golfme-architect; no migration, no backend, no native change)

Micky: "more of an emphasis or enlargement animation when I hold down on the message I want to react to, like Instagram or Telegram"; also no stuck-looking green outlines.
- **Press cue**: 100 ms into a hold (`PRESS_FEEDBACK_DELAY_MS`) `useMessageLongPress` sets `data-pressing` on the bubble and it eases to 0.97 over the remaining 350 ms via the CSS `scale` property (composes with useSwipeToReply's inline `transform`; no layout change). Removed on lift / move past the slop / scroll / open. Haptic unchanged (fires on open).
- **Focus overlay** (`components/chat/MessageActionsMenu.tsx`): a fixed full-page layer: dim + 3 px backdrop blur; a COPY of the bubble (`cloneNode`, data-*/aria/role/ids stripped, inert, buttons tabIndex -1) at the real bubble's measured box, springing from the pressed scale to 1.05 with a soft shadow; menu pops in, emojis staggered 28 ms (all WAAPI). The real bubble only gets `opacity: 0` (still a hit target) and is restored on unmount. Why a copy: the real bubble sits in the overflow-clipped list inside the swipe-to-reveal rows wrapper (own transform), so lifting it above a page backdrop would mean re-parenting or changing ancestors' stacking/transform, and enlarging near the list edges would clip; the copy leaves the list's scroll, size and stacking untouched. Green `ring-fairway-300` on the held bubble removed.
- **Placement** (`lib/messageFocus.ts` `focusLayout`, pure): scale capped so bubble + its reaction-pill overhang fit (never < 1); menu above, else below; if neither fits the bubble moves by the smallest amount; bubble + menu taller than the screen = unscaled, unmoved, menu clamped on screen. Visible area = visualViewport, inset by safe-area top/bottom.
- **Close**: tap on the dim (only a tap that started on it), Escape, scroll, resize, or any action. 150 ms exit (`FOCUS_EXIT_MS`, `lib/useExitPresence.ts`): copy returns to where the bubble is now (re-copied and re-measured, re-aimed 2 frames later if the list re-pinned after a first reaction added the pill/margin); during the exit the overlay is aria-hidden and `pointer-events: none` (composer and bubbles reachable at once; a new hold remounts). Unsend / message gone = no exit animation. Nothing takes focus (mousedown preventDefault), so the keyboard stays as it was.
- **Fix found on the way**: a very long bubble puts the menu under the holding finger; Chromium fires a click on lift, which hit "Copy". Menu items now act only on a click whose press started on the menu (keyboard click, detail 0, still works). Was latent before this change too.
- **Reduced motion**: no press scale, no enlargement/move/spring; dim + menu (opacity fades only).
- **Pill**: `ReactionPill` mine = same neutral white pill as anyone's (Micky).
- Checks: tsc, lint (0 errors), 492/492 node tests (new `tests/messageFocus.test.ts`, 18, in `test:chat`), build ("[prerender] injected"); headless demo: `reactions.mjs` 28/28, `reactions_extra.mjs` 17/17, `hold.mjs` phone + desktop OK, new scratchpad `hold-lift/holdlift.mjs` 39/39 (press scale, open theirs/mine/quoted/long/medium-bottom/top-edge/375x667, scroll + list size unchanged, copy has no hooks, focused composer stays focused, closing overlay doesn't intercept the composer, swipe-to-reply, reduced motion). Screenshots in scratchpad `hold-lift/`.
- **iPhone checks pending (app + Safari)**: feel/timing of the press + spring, haptic vs. the visual lift, blur cost in WKWebView (drop to dim-only if it stutters), hold with the keyboard open (menu placement against the visual viewport, keyboard stays), iOS not firing its own callout over the overlay, long message near the composer.

## Honest booking status, Stage 1 (copy + labels) — 2026-11-05 (branch `feat/booking-status`, rebased onto 7a22d01 on 2026-11-06, uncommitted; Micky approved shipping Stage 1; golfme-architect; no migration, no backend)

Micky's brief "Honest tee-time status and host booking evidence". Audit, staged plan and decisions in `docs/booking-status-plan.md` (Stage 2/3 prepared on the same branch, nothing applied).
- **Finding (§0 of the plan):** evidence stays private (PGlite: anon, members and other hosts see 0 proof rows and 0 objects), but any host could get the old "✓ Booking Proof Attached" badge by calling `attach_booking_proof` directly with a made-up path and any public "source" text. Stage 1 defuses it on the client; Stage 2 refuses it on the server.
- **Micky's decisions (2026-11-05, "defaults"):** Micky reviews, no promised turnaround; joining allowed while booking is pending, with the warning; images only, 5 MB, re-encoded on the device, no PDFs; retention: replaced files 7 days, raw files 30 days after the round, minimal audit 12 months; the reviewed badge expires after the round or on any change, no automatic recheck; optional host-reported booked party size; booking reference no longer collected, "Booked via" a fixed list; a "No advance booking needed" status; "booking changed" → joined players bell + push (existing push rules), "evidence reviewed" → host bell only. No staging project: PGlite only; the real Storage API is untested.
- **What changed:** `src/lib/bookingStatus.ts` (pure): only `tee_time_source = 'user_verified'` reads "Booked · Host reported"; everything else, legacy or unknown, is "Booking status not provided"; no booking line on cancelled, completed or past rounds (claim expires at the tee time); the stored free-text source is never shown (five picker values, else "Host reported"). Neutral chip (no ✓, no shield) on cards and the round page; round page has a "Booking" block with "Course availability is not connected…"; join sheet shows the booking line + "Joining this GolfMe group does not reserve a tee time or a spot with the course. Coordinate booking details with the host."; counts say "GolfMe places" / "GolfMe group"; join toast "You joined the GolfMe group for {date}."; cancel confirm adds "This doesn't cancel any booking with the course."; course page "Search tee times" + "Opens a web search. GolfMe doesn't book tee times." + availability line ("yet" dropped); hidden tee-times copy no longer claims live availability; +2 ranking boost for an unreviewed upload removed (`GolfCalls.tsx`); match-reason "✓ Booking proof attached" lines removed (the chip says it once). Uploads: images only, re-encoded, ≤ 5 MB uploaded, translated errors; the booking reference field is gone (sent as null) and is no longer kept in the Host a Round draft. 6 locales.
- **Not in Stage 1 (SQL, so done in the Stage 2 migration below):** notification text "added booking proof…" / "removed the booking proof…" (English, server-generated) and the new-round push "· 3 spots open" (`private.fanout_round_copy`, 6 languages).
- **Checks:** `tests/bookingStatus.test.ts` (in `test:trust`): legacy/unknown never booked, expiry, forged source, locale keys ×6, banned words in en. Headless demo 390×844 (scratchpad `booking-stage1/`, 18/18 UI checks; a seeded forged source renders "Host reported"; cancelled claim shows nothing). Not on a device, not on a real account.

## Honest booking status, Stages 2 + 3 (server states, evidence, review, lifecycle) — 2026-11-05 (same branch, uncommitted; client ships dormant behind feature detection; migration `20261107150000` PREPARED, NOT APPLIED; golfme-architect)

- **Migration `20261107150000_booking_status_and_evidence`** (renamed from 20261107120000 so it sorts after `20261107130000_round_chat_parity` and the Caddie fix's `20261107140000`): `golf_calls` gets `booking_status` (unknown/not_needed/needed/pending/booked/changed/cancelled), `booking_status_updated_at`, `booking_party_size` (1–8, host-reported), `evidence_status` (none/submitted/reviewed/needs_correction), `evidence_reviewed_at`, `booking_version`; all frozen for direct client writes (guard extended) and constrained (evidence only on booked, reviewed ⇔ review time, `booking_source` a fixed list). Private `booking_evidence` (versioned, snapshot of course/date/time/party/version, path pinned to `<host>/<round>/<uuid>.jpg`, JPEG ≤ 5 MB, one current version, idempotent `client_request_id`) and append-only `booking_evidence_reviews`; RLS host-or-reviewer / reviewer only, no client writes. Bucket `booking-proofs`: 5 MB, `image/jpeg` only; uploads only into a live round you host; the file of current evidence can't be deleted; reviewers read current evidence only; admins read/delete non-current files (purge). RPCs: `set_booking_status`, `submit_booking_evidence` (checks the object exists, is yours, jpeg, size), `withdraw_booking_evidence`, `my_booking_evidence` (outcome + reason, never reviewer/note), `list_booking_evidence_queue`, `review_booking_evidence` (reviewer = admin or new `booking_reviewer` role; never your own round; current version only; booking unchanged since submission, else 40001), `admin_booking_evidence_sweep` + `admin_mark_booking_evidence_purged` (retention). `edit_golf_call_tee_time` (same signature) invalidates evidence and marks a booked round "changed"; cancelling/completing the round closes evidence (booking_status kept: the GolfMe round isn't the course booking). `attach_booking_proof`/`remove_booking_proof` retired (raise 0A000). Notifications: old proof triggers dropped; `booking_status_changed` (host marks changed/cancelled → joined players, bell + push via `send_round_push`, collapse `round-<id>`; silent for the tee-time edit, which already pushes) and `booking_evidence_reviewed` (host, bell only), 6 languages; new-round push "N GolfMe places open". Backfill: nothing reviewed; `user_verified` → booked; its legacy proof becomes a submitted version only for an upcoming round with an existing JPEG ≤ 5 MB; confirmation numbers nulled; free-text sources → 'Other'; `tee_time_source` kept as a mirror for cached Stage 1 clients.
- **Client (feature-detected; works before and after the migration):** `lib/bookingStatus.ts` server model (labels "No advance booking needed · Host reported", "Booking needed", "Booking confirmation pending", "Booked · Host reported", "Booking evidence reviewed" + dated scope sentence, "Booking changed · Updated evidence needed", "Booking cancelled", "Booking evidence submitted · Awaiting review"); `BookingHostPanel` (status, optional party size, booked via, screenshot upload/replace/remove/view, staged progress, safe retry) replaces the Stage 1 controls when the row has `booking_status`; `lib/useBookingEvidence.ts`; Host a Round submits through the new RPCs when available; admin dashboard "Booking evidence" section (`components/admin/BookingEvidenceReview.tsx`: queue with snapshot-vs-round comparison, 60 s signed image, reviewed / needs update / reject with reason, purge button); round report category "Misleading or outdated booking info"; new notification types in the bell/popups.
- **Paste files** (scratchpad `booking-stage23/`): `apply.sql` (guard + migration verbatim), `check.sql` (save-before queries; 21 post-apply checks), `rollback.sql` (restores previous definitions verbatim; lists what can't be restored).
- **Re-verified 2026-11-06 after the rebase onto 7a22d01** (bell cleanup, round chat parity, dark mode, haptics, course imagery merged by hand: the two new notification types get the "booking" badge in `lib/notificationInbox.ts` and icons in `NotificationAvatar.tsx`): tsc, lint 0 errors (21 pre-existing warnings), node 660/660 (`tests/*.test.ts`, `supabase/functions/_shared/*.test.ts`, `supabase/functions/*/handler.test.ts`), build; PGlite with every repo migration replayed (incl. `20261106120100` and `20261107130000`; only the production-data backfill `20261006120100` skipped, it needs prod user ids) plus `20261107150000`: 28/28; headless on the production build (`vite preview`, port 5410): Stage 1 checks 22/22 in light and 22/22 in dark (adds: appearance applied, no Stage 2/3 labels and no host booking panel before the SQL), server-model labels 10/10, round chat suite 41/41. The only failing request is `/_vercel/insights/script.js` (404 off Vercel).
- **Checks (first pass):** PGlite (all migrations replayed) 28/28: `scripts/staging/booking-evidence-authz.sql` (anon/member/other host/stranger see and change nothing, the forged badge path refused, upload/submit rules, idempotent retry, version guard, no self-review, edit/replace/cancel invalidation, group cancel, past round, admin-only sweep), legacy backfill mapping, push queued once, apply → check → re-apply refused → rollback (schema fingerprint identical, 845 items) → re-apply; 5 negative controls make the suite fail. Node 505/505, tsc, lint 0 errors, build. Headless demo with seeded server fields 10/10 (cards, reviewed/changed/submitted detail, join sheet). **Not tested:** the real Storage HTTP API (signed URLs, bucket MIME/size enforcement, no staging project), the host panel and admin page against a real backend, any device.
- **Rollout:** client first (Stage 1 behaviour without the migration), then Micky runs `apply.sql` + `check.sql`; then a two-account check (host books + uploads, Micky reviews from a second admin or `booking_reviewer` account, member sees badge, host edits → "changed"). Micky's own rounds can't be reviewed by Micky (no self-review).

## Directory follow-ups: unblock list + Auto-Match travel area — 2026-11-05 (client LIVE as dd57e84 on Micky's "ship directory fixes"; golfme-architect; migrations `20261106130000` + `20261106130100` PREPARED, NOT APPLIED until Micky runs them)

Two findings from Micky's review against 411c39f, both **confirmed in source** (not reproduced on production; no live DB checked).
- **Unblock list lost rows (regression from directory step 1, LIVE).** Settings built the list as `blockedIds -> getGolfer -> filter(Boolean)`. Blocked ids were never in the profile cache's "needed" set, and since step 1 every lookup (directory, `golfer_public_profiles` for rounds/community/DMs) drops anyone blocked either way, so after a cold reload a blocked contact had no profile and the row (and its Unblock) disappeared while the block stayed in force. Fix: the list is the caller's own block rows (`lib/blockedContacts.ts`), always one row each; name/avatar when allowed, else "Blocked contact" (6 locales) with a working Unblock (disabled while pending, row stays on failure, translated toasts). Names come from new `my_blocked_profiles()` (id, name, avatar fields only; only golfers the caller blocked and who have NOT blocked the caller back; never anyone else), then the cache. Block/unblock now run a full refetch. Deleted accounts: block rows cascade (FK on delete cascade), so no orphan rows.
- **Auto-Match "Playing Somewhere Else?" used home-relative buckets.** `AutoMatch.tsx` fell back to `golferDistanceMiles(currentUser, g)` (bucket from the saved area) for real golfers, so NYC home + LA search at 10 mi suggested NYC golfers and never LA ones. Fix: `lib/travelGolfers.ts` picks the source (home / demo-local / travel / pending / hidden); a temporary area fetches `golfer_directory_from(origin)` (`lib/useTravelGolferDirectory.ts`, origin snapped to its ~5 km cell before sending; no table fallback). Without the function (or on error) no golfers are suggested and a note says so (`autoMatch.golfersHiddenForArea`, 6 locales). The area stays URL-only; Auto-Match's only area write is the explicit "set your location" choice, which a search with its own area never reaches. The Golfers tab has no temporary area (home-relative is correct there), unchanged.
- **Migrations (independent, either order; need directory step 1, which is live)**: `20261106130000_add_my_blocked_profiles` (one definer function). `20261106130100_add_golfer_directory_from_origin`: the directory rules move verbatim into `private.golfer_directory_rows(uid, origin...)` (no app-role execute); `golfer_directory(p_limit, p_offset)` keeps signature/grants/output (create or replace, one-line call to the core; step 2's guard still finds it); new `golfer_directory_from(lat, lng, limit, offset)`: origin required + range-checked (22023), snapped to its 0.05-degree cell, same columns (no coordinates), not stored.
- **Paste files** (scratchpad): `blocked-names-apply/check/rollback.sql`, `directory-from-apply/check/rollback.sql` (apply = guard + migration verbatim; rollback restores `golfer_directory`'s step-1 body verbatim).
- **Checks**: PGlite (all migrations replayed): new `scripts/staging/directory-followups-authz.sql` + the step-1 suite pass before and after step 2; home `golfer_directory` output identical before/after the refactor (30 fixtures, paging); negative controls bite; paste files apply -> check all true -> re-apply refused -> suites -> rollback exact (definition + grants) -> re-apply (16/16). Node 474/474 (new `tests/blockedContacts.test.ts`, `tests/travelGolfers.test.ts`), tsc, lint 0 errors, build with "[prerender] injected". Headless demo 390x844 (12/12): blocked list named + "Blocked contact" rows, unblock, Auto-Match home vs LA area, saved area unchanged, Golfers tab. Not on Supabase/staging, not on device.
- **Rollout**: client first (it works without either function: fallback label, hidden travel golfers), then either paste file in any order, then the checks below. Step 2 is unaffected.
