## DM reactions: server-side write ordering (late writes) — 2026-11-05 (LIVE on Micky's "ship reaction order"; golfme-architect; migration `20261106160000` APPLIED by Micky 2026-11-06, reactions-order-check.sql true)

- **Authz re-review of the applied `20261105160000`** (Micky applied it 2026-11-05; his screenshot of reactions-authz-check.sql shows true): no gap found. The lock (`for share`, lines 105–113) and the unsent check only run after the user_id / participant + cleared_before / both-way block checks (lines 82–103). The one unlocked read before them (lines 77–80) is unavoidable, because membership is defined by the message's conversation. Its result never leaves the function except through the single `42501 reaction_not_allowed` (lines 115–119). Unsend clearing (`messages_clear_reactions_on_unsend`) is unchanged.
- **Late writes reproduced** (PGlite on the applied state and in a simulated network): a request that timed out but reaches Postgres after the newer write was applied last, so a late add resurrected a removed reaction and a late replace won. (Writes that reach Postgres concurrently are already ordered by the row lock; the gap is a late arrival.)
- **Fix: migration `20261106160000_order_message_reaction_writes`.**
  - **Sequence column:** `client_seq bigint` on the row. A BEFORE UPDATE trigger `message_reactions_sequence` (runs after `_prepare`, so authz comes first) skips, with no error and 0 rows, any write whose seq is lower than the stored one.
  - **Removal is a tombstone:** an upsert with `emoji` NULL; `emoji` is now nullable and the six-emoji check still applies. A late add can't resurrect a removal. The client drops NULL rows, and so does the live client.
  - **Unchanged:** the policies, the authz and unsend triggers, and realtime.
- **Client (`src/lib/reactionOrder.ts`, wired in RealSocialContext).**
  - **Sequence:** seq = max(now in µs, last issued + 1, my stored seq for that message from the last fetch + 1).
  - **Fetch:** uses `select("*")` and reads `client_seq` to learn what's stored.
  - **Migration missing:** the first sequenced write gets PGRST204 client_seq; ordering is then off for the session and that write is retried once the old way. `isMissingReactionsTableError` no longer mistakes a missing column for a missing table.
  - **Refused tombstone** (block / unsent): falls back once to the old DELETE.
  - **Queue (`reactionWrites.ts`):** after an uncertain (timed-out) settle, the next tap on that message is always sent, even when it matches what's shown, so its newer seq beats a late request.
  - **Account switch:** resets the clock and the ordering state.
- **Trade-off:** two devices of the same user. A device with a slower clock that hasn't fetched the other device's newer write can have its tap ignored (no error, no retry); the refetch shows the server's state. Old clients' writes aren't ordered against sequenced ones during the rollout.
- **Rollout:** migration first, then the client. Each half is safe alone.
- **Tests:**
  - `tests/reactionOrder.test.ts` (18): simulated server + network with late arrivals; the old-table control reproduces the bug.
  - `tests/reactionWrites.test.ts`: +1 test.
  - 478/478 node tests; tsc, lint and build pass.
  - PGlite 162/162 (the prior 96 + 66 ordering; the control reproduces on the applied state).
  - Headless demo: 28/28 + 17/17 (the demo has no server path).
  - Not run: real accounts, two devices, iPhone.

## DM reactions: write ordering + trigger authorization — 2026-11-05 (LIVE: queue in 53578e6/f1c08c4; migration `20261105160000` APPLIED by Micky 2026-11-05, reactions-authz-check.sql true per his screenshot)

- Context: `20261105150000_add_dm_message_reactions` was APPLIED to production by Micky on 2026-11-05 (dm-reactions-check.sql returned true; recorded in 4f67dbf on `fix/chat-notif-jump`), and the reactions client is live. Both issues below come from Micky's review of 16bbcc3, not from production reports.
- **Ordering** (RealSocialContext): every tap sent its own upsert/delete at once, so a quick A-then-B whose older request finished last ended on A, and add-then-remove could bring the reaction back. Now `src/lib/reactionWrites.ts` `createReactionWriteQueue`: per message one write in flight, the latest tap queued (a newer tap replaces the queued one, never sent), no write when the wanted state is what the server has (add → remove → add during the add = one write). Failure: the newer queued tap is still sent; with nothing newer the pill settles on the last state the server confirmed (no stale rollback). Toast (Micky 2026-11-05): only when the latest intent ends up failed; a failed tap that a newer tap superseded stays quiet, and one failed write shared by several taps gives one toast. Timeout (Micky 2026-11-05): each write is bounded at 15 s (`REACTION_WRITE_TIMEOUT_MS`, aborted via supabase-js `.abortSignal()`) and counts as a failure; the server may still have applied it, so its state becomes unknown (the next queued tap is always sent, never skipped as "already there") and a settle after a timeout is flagged `uncertain`; the context rolls back and refetches, and the fetched rows win. A success arriving after its timeout triggers another refetch. Pending states are overlaid on every fetch/realtime refetch; one fetch when a message settles. `reset()` on account switch/sign-out: nothing queued is sent, late answers ignored, no stale toast. Demo (DataContext) has no requests, so taps already apply in order; its page-local reactions are now cleared when the signed-in identity changes.
- **Trigger authz** (migration `20261105160000_harden_message_reaction_trigger_authz`, replaces `private.prepare_message_reaction()` only): the SECURITY DEFINER BEFORE trigger runs before RLS WITH CHECK and raised `reaction_not_allowed: message not found` / `reaction_message_unsent` before any visibility check, while an existing visible-to-nobody message got the RLS error: an existence/unsent oracle for any signed-in user guessing a message id. Now it checks user_id = auth.uid(), the messages_select_participant rule (participant + newer than own cleared_before) and the both-way block rule first, takes the FOR SHARE lock only after that, and every refusal is the same `42501 reaction_not_allowed`. Policies, unsend trigger, realtime unchanged. The client maps it to the existing `chat.reactionFailed` toast (not a missing table). Paste files: scratchpad `reactions-authz-apply.sql` / `-check.sql` (one row, true) / `-rollback.sql`. Safe to apply before or after the client.
- Known limit: a timed-out write that the server applies AFTER a newer write could still end on the older state (no server-side ordering column); the late-success or realtime refetch then shows the server's truth, but the latest tap is lost. Only after a 15 s stall.
- Tests: `tests/reactionWrites.test.ts` (25, incl. out-of-order add→replace, add→remove, replace→remove→add, older/newer error, retry, reset, overlay, toast only for the latest intent, hung request → timeout, timeout → late success, timeout with a newer tap queued, 300 seeded random-timing runs); 398/398 node tests, tsc, lint, build (`[prerender] injected`); PGlite 96/96 (original 51 + 45 authz: identical error for a non-participant on existing/missing/unsent/blocked messages, participant flows, rollback, re-apply; a control shows the old function's 3 distinct errors); headless demo `reactions.mjs` 28/28 + extra 17/17 (rapid changes, plain unsend, quote then unsend shows "Message unsent", reload, sign-out → demo clears reactions). Not tested: real accounts against Supabase, two-phone realtime, physical iPhone.

## One-tap Auto-Match that never dead-ends — 2026-11-05 (branch `feat/automatch-one-tap` off origin/main, uncommitted/not deployed; no migration, no backend)

Micky: "Auto-Match often shows no results; match people with one button press." Option A approved.
- **Why it came up empty (confirmed in code)**: (1) under 3 Match Preferences set (new accounts set only Round Length) Auto-Match showed the "choose one more" form instead of results, even from the one-tap button; (2) Find a Round opens on **Today**, so its "Auto-Match Me" searched only today's rounds; (3) the radius filter drops every round with an unknown distance, and a real account without saved coordinates has no distance for any round, so everything was filtered out with no explanation; (4) the default radius is the saved travel radius (5-100, default 25) and never widened; (5) non-quick mode showed only rounds hitting 3+ preferences, "closest" only rounds hitting 1+, and preferences like "Prefer mixed group" (needs a multi-gender roster, never true for a host-only round) or "Open to Networking" (needs a Networking-vibe round) rarely match; (6) the non-quick page needed a second "Auto-Match Me" tap.
- **Now**: Auto-Match runs at once on every entry (Home card "Auto-Match Me", Rounds tab button, Find a Round). `src/lib/autoMatchLadder.ts` (pure, `tests/autoMatchLadder.test.ts` in `test:play`): `isEligibleRound` (open, a free spot, today or later, not yours/joined/requested, nobody in it blocked either way), Find a Round's advanced filters kept as chosen, then `buildLadder`: preference bar (strong = min(3, prefs set), any overlap, none) > radius (chosen, 25, 50) > date (chosen day, the 7 days from it, any upcoming), nested so each wider notch retries the narrower ones first; the first step with rounds wins. Ranking unchanged (shared preferences, then `rankCallsWithPreferences` score, then distance). A note says what was widened ("No rounds today within 10 mi. Showing Nov 5 to Nov 11.", "Widened to 25 mi", preference notes). Unknown-distance rounds are never counted as near.
- **Golfers**: `visibleGolfers()` (discoverability + blocks) ranked by `computeCompatibility`, inside the first radius (chosen/25/50) that has any; top 3 under the rounds, top 5 when there are none. Card `components/find/AutoMatchGolferCard.tsx`: match tier + 2 reasons, "Invite to play" (`lib/useInviteToPlay.ts`, now also used by the profile page; prefills the DM draft, never sends) and "View profile". Distance text uses the public directory's `golferDistanceLabel` (a server range such as "5–10 mi", else whole miles), and the same label feeds the card's match reasons.
- **Nothing at all**: "Host a round" opens Host a Round prefilled from preferences (`lib/hostPrefill.ts`: holes, format, Networking vibe, walk, skill, day; CreateGolfCall reads them, a saved draft still wins) + "Invite a friend" (`shareApp`). **No location** (no coordinates in the saved/temporary area): "Where do you play?" + Set location (LocationPicker, saves coarsened like Find a Round), never a silent search everywhere.
- Round distance in Auto-Match is now the one the round card shows (`call.distanceMiles`, from the saved area) unless a temporary area is set; demo's mock distances otherwise contradicted the notes.
- Micky approved the defaults (2026-11-05): nested widening order + note wording, explicit Find a Round filters never widen, unknown-distance rounds/golfers excluded, Home keeps 4 cards. The 11 `autoMatch.*` strings of the old gate/empty states (helpFindBetter, chooseOneMore, notEnoughPrefs(+Desc), usingPreferences, outsideNormalCriteria, finding, noStrongMatches(+Desc), showClosest, nothingNearby) were unused and are removed from all 6 locales.
- Page subtitle names the chosen day only while the results are still for that day; after a date widening, or with no rounds, it uses the neutral any-day subtitle (existing key, all 6 locales).
- The box before "Great Match" in headless screenshots is the real emoji U+1F7E2 (🟢 from `matchTier`, shared CompatibilityBadge) with no emoji font on the WSL box (`fc-list` has none); not a character bug, left as is.
- Checks (after rebasing onto f1c08c4): tsc, lint (0 errors), all node tests, build (prerender injected), headless demo 26/26 at 390x844 and 375x667 (reduced motion on the 375 run): Home + Rounds tab one tap, today->week, radius note, golfers fallback, Invite opens DM with the draft, empty state, Host prefill URL, needs-location + picker, no horizontal overflow. **Not tested**: real accounts (real `discoverableGolfers`, real rounds' distances, a real account without coordinates, blocked-by on real data).

## Sign-in: code-flow bugs + login redesign — 2026-11-05 (branch `fix/signin-code-flow`, rebased onto f1c08c4, uncommitted/not deployed; golfme-architect; no migration, no native change)

- **Code-flow bugs (from Micky's review against 16bbcc3, all three were still present):**
  - *Email changed while sending*: the field stayed editable and the code screen/verify read the field, so a send to A could verify B. Now the address is captured at submit and bound (`sentTo`, also in the sessionStorage draft; old drafts fall back to their email); the field is disabled while sending; sends and verifies go through request gates (`lib/emailCodeFlow.ts` `createRequestGate`/`gatedRequest`): one in flight, answers after "Change email"/"Use a different method" are stale and ignored. New "Change email" returns to the email step keeping the address.
  - *Expired/used link with a restored code screen*: the notice only rendered on the email step. Now a role=alert notice with "Send a new code" on whichever step is showing (`classifyAuthNotice`).
  - *Resend countdown after backgrounding*: it counted timer ticks. Now computed from the send time (or the last 429) and the clock (`resendSecondsLeft`, `watchResendCountdown`), recomputed on visibilitychange/focus/pageshow and Capacitor `appStateChange` (same signals as resumeRefetch/FoundingRevealHost); clock skew (start in the future, non-finite) ends the countdown; the server's rate limit still applies.
- **Login redesign** (Micky's brief + coordinator decisions): `/login` and `/signup` are one 3-step screen in `src/pages/Auth.tsx`: welcome (photo band, official `GolfMeIcon` + `GolfMeWordmark`, "Never golf alone.", Apple / Google / Email, terms) -> "Enter your email" -> "Check your email" (`components/auth/CodeInput.tsx`: six boxes over ONE input, `autocomplete=one-time-code`, numeric, paste/full-width digits, auto-submit once per distinct code, wrong code clears, network error keeps it for "Sign in with code"). Email/code steps are top-aligned (keyboard-safe at 375x667). The "Already have an account? / New here?" links are gone (one flow; returning vs new is decided by the existing route guards -> /profile-setup or Home).
  - Photo: `public/courses/skyway-aerial.jpg` (USGS NAIP aerial, public domain; the only golf photo in the repo), zoomed onto the course, green wash + fade, fixed-height band (`clamp(150px, 100svh-500px, 380px)`), width/height set, no layout shift (measured CLS < 0.01). Micky 2026-11-05: keep the aerial for now. The photo is one constant, `WELCOME_PHOTO` in `src/lib/welcomeImagery.ts` (src, size, focal point, zoom, credit), so the course-imagery work can swap it without touching Auth.tsx.
  - Apple: native unchanged (plugin sheet + `signInWithIdToken`, live since 1.1). Web: `signInWithOAuth('apple')` behind `VITE_APPLE_WEB_SIGN_IN=true` (default off, `isWebAppleSignInEnabled`). Order Apple, Google, Email. A cancelled Apple redirect shows nothing.
  - Errors: new kinds `serverError` (5xx; for a send = "We couldn't send your code") and `accountConflict` (identity_already_exists / email_exists...); `auth.appleFailed`; a session that ends by itself (SIGNED_OUT not preceded by log out / account deletion, `noteIntentionalSignOut`) shows "You've been signed out" on the sign-in screen. Never raw Supabase text.
  - Analytics (`lib/analytics.ts`, dev-log only, not server-counted): login_viewed, auth_apple_selected, auth_google_selected, auth_email_selected, code_requested {resend}, auth_completed {provider} (AuthContext, SIGNED_IN for a new identity), auth_failed {reason}. No emails/codes/tokens.
  - Kept as they were: Google web/native calls, demo (`/login?demo`). Relay emails never become a placeholder name (`placeholderNameFromEmail`).
  - **Deliberate exception to "no passwords" (Micky 2026-11-05):** the hidden App Review password sign-in stays exactly as it was: the small "Sign in with a password" link in the iOS app, and on the web only with `?review=1` (opens the form) or `?demo`. The public web login shows no password anywhere. Reason: the App Store review notes sign in with the reviewer password account (LAUNCH_CHECKLIST "Review / demo credentials"); removing it would break the next review. Only revisit together with new review notes.
  - Removed the 8 translation keys this redesign made unused (`auth.createAccount`, `auth.signupSubtitle`, `auth.loginSubtitle`, `auth.signInWithApple`, `auth.alreadyHaveAccount`, `auth.newHere`, `auth.sendMagicLink`, `auth.code.checkEmailBody`) from all 6 locales. Five `auth.*` keys were already unused before this work and are left alone: `auth.welcomeBack`, `auth.logIn`, `auth.newAccount`, `auth.roundsPlayed`, `auth.resendEmail`.
- **Sign in with Apple on the web, setup Micky must do before turning `VITE_APPLE_WEB_SIGN_IN` on** (none done; no credentials invented; production auth untouched):
  1. Apple Developer > Identifiers: App ID `com.golfme.ios` already has Sign in with Apple (native). Create a **Services ID** (e.g. `app.golfme.web`), enable Sign in with Apple, Configure: primary App ID `com.golfme.ios`; Domains: `<project-ref>.supabase.co`; Return URL: `https://<project-ref>.supabase.co/auth/v1/callback`.
  2. Apple Developer > Keys: a Sign in with Apple key (.p8). The existing one (used by delete-account) can be reused; note Key ID + Team ID.
  3. Generate the OAuth client secret (ES256 JWT: iss = Team ID, sub = Services ID, aud = https://appleid.apple.com, max 6 months). **It expires every 6 months: put a rotation reminder in the calendar.**
  4. Supabase > Authentication > Providers > Apple: keep it enabled; **add** the Services ID to Client IDs next to `com.golfme.ios` (never remove the bundle ID: native sign-in uses it); paste the secret.
  5. Supabase > Authentication > URL Configuration: `https://golfme.app` is already a redirect URL (Google); add any preview origin used for testing.
  6. Apple Developer > Sign in with Apple for Email Communication: register GolfMe's sending domain/address (with SPF/DKIM) so Hide My Email relays deliver GolfMe's emails.
  7. Vercel: `VITE_APPLE_WEB_SIGN_IN=true`, redeploy; test in Safari (new Apple ID, returning Apple ID, Hide My Email, cancel).
  - Identity linking: Supabase links an Apple/Google identity to an existing account only on a matching **verified** email (Apple and Google emails are verified); a relay address is a new account. GolfMe never merges accounts itself; manual linking isn't used.
- **Session persistence audit**: supabase-js defaults (persistSession in localStorage, autoRefreshToken, refresh on visibilitychange, implicit flow, URL hash cleared); AuthContext's single listener, no remount on same-user events, profile-load retry screen, GolfMeLoader slowLoadRetry. Two changes: the "signed out" notice above, and:
  - **Offline cold start FIXED** (Micky 2026-11-05). Root cause: launching with an expired access token while Supabase is unreachable, auth-js retries the refresh (~30 s), then emits `INITIAL_SESSION` with no session but KEEPS the stored session (it removes it only when Supabase rejects the refresh token). The app read that as signed out and showed Welcome/sign-in. Now AuthContext sets `restorePending` when INITIAL_SESSION is empty but `hasStoredSession()` (`src/lib/authSession.ts`: an `sb-*-auth-token` entry with a refresh token) is true; `authLoading` stays true, so AppGate keeps the full-screen loader with its existing "Try again" (which checks reachability, then reloads). It retries `getSession()` on `online`, focus, visibilitychange, native `appStateChange` and every 15 s; success arrives as TOKEN_REFRESHED and the normal listener path takes over (profile, routing). A rejected token removes the stored session (SIGNED_OUT) and the signed-out screen shows; never for demo. auth-js caches a failed refresh for 60 s, so recovery without a tap takes up to about a minute after the network returns (measured 53 s); "Try again" is immediate.
  - **Still open:** iOS WKWebView storage: whether localStorage (and so the session) survives long idle periods (WebKit's 7-day script-storage cap) needs a device check; if not, a Capacitor Preferences storage adapter needs a native build (golfme-architect).
- Tests: unit `tests/emailCodeFlow.test.ts` (gates, binding, Change email, double tap, failures, verify, draft, link errors, notices, code sanitising, auto-submit), `tests/authInput.test.ts` (error kinds, relay, sign-out marker, all new strings in 6 locales with matching placeholders), `tests/authSession.test.ts` (stored-session detection, junk/blocked storage); fake-timer countdown/background/resume tests (node mock.timers); headless `scripts/dev/signin-flow.mjs --apple` (mocked Supabase auth on 127.0.0.1:9, page.clock; ~4-5 min): 105/105, i.e. 390x844 flows + 375x667 / 390x844 / 1280x800 layout (no scroll needed, no CLS, field/boxes above a 260 px keyboard, reduced motion) + Apple flag on + offline cold start (holds 45 s without showing sign-in, recovers on its own after the network returns, recovers at once via Try again, revoked token signs out and clears the session). After rebasing onto f1c08c4: tsc, lint (0 errors), 423 node tests, build ("[prerender] injected").
- **Not tested:** real Supabase auth (send/verify/expiry/refresh), the real email link, real Apple/Google pages, WKWebView (OAuth, the Apple sheet, and storage persistence over long idle periods), a physical iPhone (Mail autofill of the code, resume from Mail, keyboard, airplane-mode cold start), small-iPhone keyboard for real.

## Course imagery: real photos, course-framed aerials (USGS NAIP, $0), drone overview — 2026-11-05 (branch `feat/course-imagery` on main 1ecc1f9, uncommitted/not deployed; golfme-architect; migration `20261106140000` NOT APPLIED)

Micky: course pictures didn't show the actual course; real photos of that course first, satellite last; a "drone shot" overview on the course page. Acceptance bar: "the photos showcase the courses so users know what the course is like" (the course itself, not a clubhouse, sign, logo, car park or a nearby place). AI preview clips (Higgsfield) are PARKED on Micky's word (no budget now): kept as a separate patch, not in this branch.
- **Root cause**: (1) the only real-photo source was a browser-side Wikimedia lookup (thin), (2) its rules took a facility's sibling course photo (Bethpage Blue/Red showed Bethpage Black, reproduced headless) and any golf-titled file (clubhouses, signs), (3) satellite views were centred on our single point, often the clubhouse/car park or ~1 km off (demo: Forest Park, Split Rock, Cantiague, Cherry Valley; Liberty National ~2 km), (4) the Mapbox token is Production-only, so Preview builds showed a pin.
- **Photos (server, `course-photos` Edge Function)**: Google Places (New) when `GOOGLE_PLACES_API_KEY` is set (one Text Search per course ever, name/distance match in `_shared/placesMatch.ts`, only the place id stored; per view a free `photos`-only Place Details + one Place Photo; photo names/URLs never stored). Google photos have no captions, so only large landscape ones are used (no square/portrait: logos, menus, signs) else none. Then Wikimedia with license + content rules (`photoCourseScore`/`photoShowsCourse` in `_shared/coursePhotoMatch.ts`: title/description/categories must show holes/fairways/greens/aerial; logos, maps, signs, clubhouses, parking, PNG/SVG refused; candidates ranked, up to 4 described). Daily caps per provider (`course_imagery_take_quota`). Client `lib/courseImageryClient.ts`: batched, session memory only; satellite-only views ask for the outline alone (`outlineOnly`, nothing billable). Demo / undeployed function: same rules in the browser (`lib/coursePhotos.ts`, cache bumped to v2).
- **Framing the course, not its pin**: the course's OpenStreetMap leisure=golf_course outline (bounding box), from Geoapify Place Details (1 credit, existing `GEOAPIFY_API_KEY`, the course's own OSM object via `course_external_ids`) else Overpass (two endpoints, 8 s; the main one was mostly 502/504/timeouts on 2026-11-05), cached in `course_imagery.outline_*` (none re-checked after 180 days, provider failures not recorded). Thumbnails, still views and the overview fit the outline (`_shared/courseBounds.ts`: `pickCourseOutline`, `fitZoom`, `stillFraming`, `overviewFraming`); without one, centred on the point a little wider. Demo fixture outlines fetched once from Overpass (`src/lib/courses.ts`, © OSM contributors).
- **Aerial imagery, option A ($0, Micky 2026-11-05)**: USGS NAIP orthoimagery (The National Map `USGSImageryOnly`, `/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}`, public domain, no key) is the default for US courses; cached to z16, closer views scale z16 up (thumbs via `tileZoomFor`, MapLibre via the source's `maxzoom`); credit "USGS The National Map: Orthoimagery" + "© OpenStreetMap contributors" (outlines) on every view. MapTiler only when `VITE_MAPTILER_KEY` is set (option C: then everywhere). Non-US without the key: no aerial (Mapbox image if its token is set, else the old placeholder). "Is this US?" = the course's Geoapify `country` (already in `public.courses`, now passed to the client), else coarse boxes incl. Alaska, Hawaii and territories (`isUsCourse` in `lib/satelliteTiles.ts`; the contiguous box also covers southern Canada, where NAIP 404s and the view falls back). Rate safety: thumbs are lazy `<img>`s (on-screen only), browser-cached, plus a client cap of 150 distinct tile URLs a minute (`TileBudget`). Photos: Wikimedia only unless `GOOGLE_PLACES_API_KEY` is set (lists say "Google Maps" only once a Google photo was actually served).
- **Course page + map card (client)**: thumbs = 1-4 raster tiles as `<img>` (no map session, no live map in lists). `CourseMedia`: one reserved 16:10 box, tabs Overview / Photo (Satellite still without WebGL). `CourseOverviewMap`: MapLibre (lazy chunk), pitch 60, orbit 6°/s around the outline's centre, starting after the first draw (fix: an orbit started while the first tiles load kept the map from ever settling, so the 12 s timeout killed it); falls back when no tile loaded at all (MapLibre is silent about 404 tiles), drag/pinch pauses 8 s, reduced motion still, off-screen/background pause, a late outline moves the same map. Map card: thumbnail + a credit naming what the thumbnail really shows (`onShown`).
- **Migration NOT APPLIED**: `20261106140000_add_course_imagery` (renamed from 120000, which collided with main's public directory migration): course_imagery (Google place id, Wikimedia file + license, outline bbox, provided image) + usage + quota fn, RLS on, service role only. Paste-ready apply/check/rollback SQL in the session scratchpad (see the session report).
- **Parked (Higgsfield phase 2)**: `higgsfield-phase2.patch` + `higgsfield-phase2-{apply,check,rollback}.sql` in the session scratchpad (function `course-preview-clips`, migration `20261106150000`, admin panel, AI preview tab). Re-based onto the option A tree; applies cleanly; with it applied: tsc, 111 course tests, Deno 12/12.
- Tests: `tests/placesMatch.test.ts`, `tests/courseImagery.test.ts` (incl. source choice, US check, USGS URL order, tile budget), `tests/courseBounds.test.ts`, `supabase/functions/course-photos/handler.test.ts` (19; Deno + node, CI glob extended); PGlite 27/27 replaying all 98 earlier repo migrations (one data-only backfill skipped) + this one + paste files; headless demo 38/38 twice (default USGS, MapTiler keyed, reduced motion, no WebGL, refused tiles, never above z16); real USGS screenshots loaded directly (99 tile requests, none above z16). Also fixed on main's line: `test:onboarding` missing space (58 tests now run). Not seen: real Google/MapTiler/Geoapify responses, a real account's course-photos call, iPhone WebGL/gestures, a real non-US course.

## Caddie club picker with sketches — 2026-11-06 (separate local commit on top of the pose/tips commit c5970ec, so that one can ship alone; NOT pushed, NOT deployed; no migration, no Edge Function change; golfme-architect)
- Micky: "include more club choices in I'm hitting; ideally a little sketch of what the club looks like". The upload screen's `<select>` is replaced by `src/components/caddie/ClubPicker.tsx`: a horizontally scrolling row of category cards (Driver, Wood, Hybrid, Iron, Wedge, then "Not sure") with line-art sketches in currentColor (`src/components/caddie/clubSketches.tsx`: big round driver, flatter wood, hybrid with a rounded back, a thin blade iron and a high-loft grooved wedge seen from the toe), then chips for the exact club. Both rows are `role="radiogroup"` of `role="radio"` buttons, so each pick gets the existing selection haptic (lib/tapFeedback). "Not sure" is the default and sends no club, as before. Switching to another card clears a club from a different card. About 142 px tall with Driver open, about 330 px with all 14 wedges.
- Values (`src/lib/clubCatalog.ts`): every old dropdown value is kept exactly (test-guarded). New: Mini Driver, 2/4 Wood, 6/7 Hybrid, Gap/Sand/Lob Wedge, 46°, 48°, 50°, 62° and 64° Wedge. No Putter. A saved draft still restores (`ALL_CLUB_VALUES`). `clubLabel()` translates the new names in all 6 locales (`club.miniDriver`, `club.gapWedge`, `club.sandWedge`, `club.lobWedge`, `club.category.*`, `caddie.club.notSure`, `caddie.club.typeLabel`). `caddie.swingTypeSelectPlaceholder` is now unused.
- **v2 art (Micky rejected the line sketches: "realistic, well animated, accurate ... premium, not AI")**, a further local commit, screenshots to Micky before anything ships: `clubSketches.tsx` is now detailed vector art at a 3/4 address angle with hosel and a short shaft: carbon-crown driver (deep pear) and fairway wood (shallower, smaller) with titanium faces and scoring lines, a compact gunmetal hybrid with a brushed, grooved face and a deeper back, a cavity-back iron in brushed chrome shown from behind (top line, cavity, blank badge, sole flange), and a satin wedge face with full grooves, a curved leading edge, visible bounce and the back thickness of a lofted blade. Layered linear/radial gradients, one feTurbulence brushed-metal filter, contact shadow, rim light, light from the top left; dark mode only changes `--club-shadow`/`--club-rim` on the picker. All paint defined once (`ClubArtDefs`, rendered by ClubPicker; ~20 KB source for all five). Motion (index.css `club-settle` 360 ms rotateY 8 deg spring, `club-shine` 700 ms clipped sweep, card lift on hover/press; transform/opacity only; the global reduced-motion rule collapses both to their end frame). Cards 84x88 px, art 76 px wide. Screenshots: scratchpad `club-picker-v2/` (row, Driver/Iron selected, 3x close-ups, 3 animation frames). Known gaps: the iron is a rear view, so its grooves are not visible (the wedge shows a grooved face); it is stylised vector art, not photoreal.
- Server: no change. analyze-swing stores `swingType` as free text (no allowlist, no DB check) and only tells Gemini "The golfer says this is a: <value>.", which reads "Gap Wedge" and "Mini Driver" fine.
- Tests: `tests/clubLabel.test.ts` (old values kept, new ones parse/translate/sit in their card, no putter, no emoji). Screenshots in scratchpad `club-picker/` (temporary demo bypass, reverted). On an iPhone: horizontal scroll of the cards vs. the page's vertical scroll, the selection haptic, and how the tall wedge list feels.

## Caddie "usable body in 0/N frames": diagnostics, filming tips, clearer failure, full-resolution safety net — 2026-11-06 (local commits only, NOT pushed, NOT deployed, no migration; golfme-architect)
- **Trigger**: 6 failed analyses since 2026-11-04 (two golfers, `ce6e7674` x4, `81be0235` x2), all `pose_not_found` with 0 usable frames (0/80 with the default trim window 0-10 s, and once 0/24), while other golfers' analyses completed the same days.
- **Ruled out offline** (synthetic ffmpeg fixtures only, the same ffmpeg-static 7.0.2 build `api/` uses; no user video, no vendor call): HEVC Main10 HLG/BT.2020 decodes to normal frames in both trim-video (libx264 yuv420p) and extract-frames (JPEG); a -90 degree display matrix is auto-rotated in both (406x720 portrait frames); a non-zero start_time (`-ss 0` is relative to it) and VFR/60 fps sources give the expected frame counts. 80 frames for a 0-10 s window also means the trim and the sampling covered the whole window, and the count is what Roboflow ANSWERED, so it is not an outage, a quota, or a decode failure: Roboflow returned every frame and none had a reliable shoulders+hips+wrist body.
- **Not pinned**: whether there was no golfer in frame, a golfer below Roboflow's reliability bar (small/far, or made smaller by the 720 px frame downscale from 2026-10-30, whose effect on real swings was never checked), or a response shape we don't read. Read-only, metadata-only queries for Micky: scratchpad `caddie-pose-diagnose.sql` (failed vs complete rows' file type/size/trim, the two golfers' history, daily pose coverage around the downscale go-live, golfer size in pixels).
- **Change (diagnostics only, no behavior change)**: `_shared/caddieReliability.ts` gains `tallyRoboflowOutput` (raw, pre-filter counts: frames with a keypoints object, with any keypoint, with a metric_reliable keypoint, max keypoint and person confidence), `jpegSizeFromBase64` and `formatPoseGateDetail`. `analyze-swing` tallies each answered coarse frame and the pose gate's failure detail (in `private.caddie_job_diagnostics`) plus the `pose_coverage` log line now read e.g. `usable body in 0/80 (min ratio 0.1) kp_obj 80/80 kp_any 80/80 kp_reliable 0/80 max_kp_conf 0.31 max_person_conf 0.80 frame 406x720 scale 2.66`. Numbers only. Tests: `tests/caddiePoseGate.test.ts` (in `test:caddie`) and a new pose_not_found scenario in `tests/deno/analyzeSwing.harness.ts` (22/22).
- **Production metadata (Micky's read-only SQL, 2026-11-06)**: all 6 failures were untrimmed direct uploads (<= 10 s): `ce6e7674` .mov 16.4 MB x2, .mp4 2.6 MB, .mp4 0.3 MB (the 0/24); `81be0235` .mov 5.5 MB x2, then 2 COMPLETE a minute later with a different clip. `ce6e7674` has 0 complete. Complete rows before/after the 720 px downscale show similar coverage (0.29-0.96, torsos down to ~116 px). Conclusion: content (no clearly visible whole golfer), not a pipeline bug.
- **Follow-up, approved by Micky (same branch, 2026-11-06)**:
  - **Filming tips** (`src/components/caddie/FilmingTips.tsx`) on the upload screen, replacing the single camera-angle line: whole body in frame, 3-4 m away, phone at hip height and steady, good light, face-on or down the line. Compact (one 40 px row folded, ~150 px open), lucide icons, folds away with Hide/Show, remembered per device in `localStorage` (`golfme:caddieFilmingTipsCollapsed`, try/catch, starts open without storage). Real accounts only, like the rest of the upload form (demo shows its "needs a real account" card). `caddie.cameraAngleTip` is now unused (left in the locales).
  - **pose_not_found copy**: "We couldn't see your whole body in this video" + "Caddie needs to see you from head to feet ...", the same tips inside the failure card, the existing Try again, and a new "Choose another video" (to `/caddie/analyze`). "This didn't use one of your daily analyses" was already shown (only a `complete` transition charges, migration `20261107140000`; the harness asserts 0 charges for pose_not_found). 6 locales, no emoji (`tests/caddieFailure.test.ts` checks every locale).
  - **Full-resolution safety net** (`analyze-swing`): when the pose gate fails on a downscaled pass (`coordinateScale` > 1.01) and >= 215 s of the 330 s deadline remain (`shouldRunFullResPoseRetry`, `FULL_RES_RETRY_BUDGET_MS`), ONE more extract-frames call at `maxLongSide` 1920 (`FULL_RES_RETRY_LONG_SIDE`: 1080p at full size, 4K halved) plus one coarse Roboflow pass. Rescued: the analysis continues on those frames (dense pass at the same long side), and `roboflow_analysis_json.video.pose_pass = "full_resolution_retry"` (else `"standard"`). Not rescued, or the retry itself fails: the original pose_not_found, detail `<first pass> | fullres usable u/t kp_reliable r/t frame WxH` or `| fullres error <category>@<stage>`. Logs: `pose_fullres_retry`, `pose_fullres_retry_result`, `pose_coverage` with `pass`. Same row and attempt; nothing moved out of the persisted job.
  - **Cost/latency**: zero on clips that pass the gate. On a failing downscaled clip: +1 extract-frames call and +N Roboflow calls (N = the coarse frame count, 8 fps, max 150, typically 24-88), about +10-30 s; Gemini is still never called unless rescued. A Try again repeats it (each attempt is one run).
  - Tests: `tests/caddiePoseGate.test.ts` (+ retry rule), 3 new harness scenarios (rescued; still nothing at full res; full-res extraction fails), 25/25. Screenshots (demo build with a temporary failed row / upload-form bypass, both reverted): scratchpad `caddie-tips/`.
- **Next**: Micky reviews the screenshots; with his OK, push (client) and `supabase functions deploy analyze-swing`. On an iPhone: the tips card on the upload screen (fold, reopen app, still folded), a deliberately bad clip (feet cut off) shows the new failure card, and a good clip still completes in the usual time.

## Caddie failures: security, failure taxonomy, retry on the same job, failures never cost a slot, delete — 2026-11-06 (branch `fix/caddie-failures` on origin/main 7a22d01, uncommitted, NOT deployed, migration `20261107140000` (renamed from 20261106100000) NOT APPLIED; golfme-architect)
- **Release prep (2026-11-06)**:
  - Rebased onto main 7a22d01 (patch + `git apply --3way`). Conflicts kept both sides: Caddie language scope, "Work on this next", trim "Change section".
  - Refusal codes now flow through `caddieErrorText()` in the Caddie language. RealCaddieContext no longer translates with the app-language `t`; it throws `CaddieRequestError(serverMessage, status, code)`. The new keys reuse main's (`tooSoon`, `signedOut`, `ownPostsOnly`, `videoUnusable`, `analysisNotFound`, `unavailable`, `dailyLimit`), with no duplicates. `tests/caddieLocale.test.ts` passes.
- **Gemini credits ran out (Micky)**: 429 RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED and billing/credit 4xx are classified `provider_quota_exhausted`, are NOT retried, and are NOT charged. The golfer sees "Caddie is temporarily unavailable / This is on our side, not your video" plus "This didn't use one of your daily analyses", and the case is counted in the admin failure stats. A per-minute 429 (its message names `PerMinute`) is still retried with back-off.
- **Kill switch**: `CADDIE_DISABLED=unavailable` (new) refuses with code `unavailable` ("temporarily unavailable"). `CADDIE_DISABLED=1` still means "busy, try in a minute". Both act before any row, allowance slot or Roboflow/Gemini call. Set or unset either in Supabase > Edge Functions > Secrets, or with `supabase secrets set CADDIE_DISABLED=unavailable` / `supabase secrets unset CADDIE_DISABLED`; no redeploy needed. Recommended while the Gemini credits are empty, otherwise every attempt still spends Roboflow credits before failing at Gemini.
- **Delete (Micky: "a delete button on Caddie")**: a trash button on each history row and a Delete button on the detail page, each with a confirm sheet in the Caddie language, 6 locales. It works for failed, complete and dead rows; a live job hides the button and the RPC refuses it.
  - `delete_caddie_analysis(id)` (owner only) deletes the row and returns the caller's now-unreferenced caddie-media paths (video, thumbnail, every per-attempt trimmed copy); the app removes them through the Storage API under the owner-only policy (storage.protect_delete blocks SQL deletes).
  - The ledgers (charges, starts, diagnostics) have no FK to the row, so deleting never refunds a slot or a run (PGlite: 30 delivered-then-deleted analyses still hit the limit). Deleting the account still removes them (owner_id → auth.users cascade).
  - Admin failure stats now come from `private.caddie_job_diagnostics`, one row per failed attempt written by the trigger `record_caddie_failure`, so the totals survive deletes. Legacy failed rows were backfilled as `unclassified`.
  - Community: a shared post has its own community-media copies and an Ask-Caddie analysis reads its post's media, so no post changes and no community-media object is ever returned. When the analysis was shared or came from a post, the confirm sheet adds "Anything you shared to Community stays there".
Prompted by 3 failed analyses for one user on 2026-11-04 (root cause still needs production data: read-only diagnostics in the scratchpad `caddie-failures-diagnose.sql`, plus Edge/Vercel log checks). Micky's decisions: failures never use an allowance slot (only a delivered analysis does); 60 pipeline runs/user/24 h hidden cost ceiling; Try again re-runs the SAME row, capped at 3 retries; Option B (charge ledger + DEFINER triggers) now, Option A (client UPDATE limited to `shared_to_community`, job writes via service role) is a documented follow-up.
- **Security (separately deployable subset, scratchpad `caddie-security-only.patch`)**: the Gemini key moved from `?key=` URLs to the `x-goog-api-key` header (analyze-swing + translate-caddie-analysis). Deno fetch errors embed the URL, so a network error could write the key (or a signed-URL token) into the owner-readable `error_message` and the logs. Thrown errors now contribute only their class name (`safeErrorName`), provider bodies only allow-listed tokens (`providerErrorHint`), and `redactDetail()` is a safety net at every sink, `formatFailure` included. `api/extract-frames.ts` and `api/trim-video.ts` return classified tokens (`decode_failed` 422, `no_video_stream` 422, `resources_exhausted` 500, `internal` 500, `source_fetch_failed` 502) and never ffmpeg stderr, which can echo QuickTime GPS/device metadata. Tests: `tests/caddieRedact.test.ts`.
- **Taxonomy**: `_shared/caddieReliability.ts` now has `CADDIE_STAGES` and `CADDIE_FAILURE_CATEGORIES`, `classifyHttpFailure`, `classifyThrown`, `classifyGeminiFileState`, `stepRetryDelayMs` and `legacyCodeFor`. The row gets `failure_stage`, `failure_category`, `failure_retryable` and `provider_http_status`. `error_message` keeps the legacy `code:` (+ `window:`) prefix for old app builds, then `<category>@<stage>` with no free text. The redacted detail goes to `private.caddie_job_diagnostics`, through the `record_caddie_diagnostic` RPC. Every event writes one structured JSON log line (`{"fn":"analyze-swing","evt":...}`).
- **Retries (server)**: `runStep()` retries a failed step up to 2 more times, for transient categories only (429/5xx/408/network/timeout; bad model output gets 1 more try), with 2 s then 6 s back-off (Retry-After honored, capped at 10 s), and only while the 330 s deadline covers the wait plus another run of the step. All retries stay on the same row and the same attempt.
  - Roboflow re-runs only the failed frames, and a hard 4xx such as the credit cap is no longer retried per frame.
  - Gemini's ACTIVE wait grew from a fixed 12 s to a budget of up to 90 s. A `FAILED` file state now maps to `video_unreadable`, not "busy".
  - Every fetch has a timeout.
  - When a trim window is set, the original is never buffered in the Edge worker (trim-video fetches it). An untrimmed original over 120 MB fails as `video_too_long_or_large` instead of risking a silent memory kill.
  - The trimmed copy is named per attempt (`caddie-trimmed-<id>-a<n>.mp4`).
  - Streaming the original straight to Gemini is a follow-up.
- **Try again (same row)**: analyze-swing takes `{ retryAnalysisId }`. It signs the media first, then does an atomic flip (`update … where attempt = n` → `processing`, `attempt + 1`). Of two concurrent taps exactly one runs and the other gets the row back. Every write is guarded by `status = 'processing' AND attempt = n`, so a late writer from an older attempt can't overwrite a newer one. The client sends the legacy fields too, so an older analyze-swing build still works and creates a new row, as before. The stale check and the server's in-flight check key off `attempt_started_at`, not `created_at`.
- **Dead workers**: each request first calls `sweep_stale_caddie_jobs()` (invoker, owner-scoped). A `processing` row older than 390 s becomes `failed/worker_lost`, keeping the last persisted `stage` (persisted at trim, frame_extract, pose, gemini_upload and gemini_generate).
- **Allowance (migration `20261107140000`, RLS/grants in the same file)**:
  - `private.caddie_allowance_charges` is append-only, written by the trigger `charge_caddie_allowance` on the first transition to `complete`.
  - `private.caddie_pipeline_starts` records one row per insert-as-processing and per retry.
  - `enforce_caddie_daily_limit()` is now DEFINER. It returns early unless `new.owner_id = auth.uid()`, so a client can't probe another user's usage, then checks charged + live jobs against 30 and starts against 60.
  - `private.guard_caddie_job()` allows attempt changes only by +1, only from failed or stale (> 6 min), up to 4. It also enforces the limits, stamps `attempt_started_at` and `finished_at`, and records the start.
  - New RPCs: `caddie_allowance_status()`, `admin_caddie_failure_stats()` and `admin_list_caddie_failures()` (admin gate, user ids only).
  - Backfill: all `complete` rows are charged at their created_at; every row counts as 1 start.
- **App**: `analysisFailure()` reads the category first. New kinds `providerDown`, `videoTooLarge` and `couldNotFinish`, plus "This didn't use one of your daily analyses". After 4 attempts the app hides Try again and asks for a new clip. Every analyze-swing refusal now has a stable `code`, mapped to localized copy, including the limit/cooldown/ceiling/retry-cap toasts, the network error and the storage upload failure (raw "Load failed" is no longer shown). All 6 locales. The admin dashboard Caddie section shows failures by category × stage and the recent failures.
- **Checks**:
  - `npx tsc -b` and `npm run lint` pass (0 errors; the 21 pre-existing warnings).
  - `node --test tests/*.test.ts supabase/functions/_shared/*.test.ts`: 394/394. New: `tests/caddieRedact.test.ts` and `tests/caddieTaxonomy.test.ts`, which includes drift checks against the migration's CHECK lists and constants.
  - `deno check` on both functions: only the pre-existing `EdgeRuntime` name error.
  - The Deno harness `tests/deno/analyzeSwing.harness.ts` runs the real function with fetch/Supabase stubbed: 18/18 pass on this branch, 18/18 fail against origin/main's function. Run it with `deno test --no-check --allow-env --config tests/deno/deno.json tests/deno/analyzeSwing.harness.ts`.
  - The paste files `caddie-failures-apply.sql`, `-check.sql` and `-rollback.sql` were validated on PGlite against all repo migrations replayed: check `all_true`; mutations are caught; nothing persists; rollback, then re-apply, both work.
  - `npm run build` passes with dummy env.
  - Headless demo at 390×844: /caddie, a demo detail page and /caddie/analyze render with no console errors. The failure card itself can't be seen in demo, which has no failed analyses.
- **Deploy order (Micky's call; each step is separate)**:
  1. Paste `caddie-failures-apply.sql` in the SQL editor, then `caddie-failures-check.sql`, which must return one row with `all_true`.
  2. `supabase functions deploy analyze-swing` and `supabase functions deploy translate-caddie-analysis`.
  3. Vercel push (client + `api/extract-frames.ts` + `api/trim-video.ts` ship together).
  - Wrong order: functions before the SQL means every analysis fails (unknown columns/RPCs). A client before the SQL means the Caddie history doesn't load (unknown columns in the list select). A client before the functions only makes Try again create a new row, as before. The API routes before analyze-swing are harmless (old analyze-swing still maps their status codes).
  - The security-only patch can ship first on its own: functions + Vercel, no SQL.
- **Checks (after release prep + delete)**:
  - tsc and lint pass (0 errors, 21 pre-existing warnings); node tests 651/651; Deno harness 21/21; `deno check` shows only the `EdgeRuntime` error.
  - PGlite on all 102 migrations: the check returns `all_true` (now with 8 delete checks); the community/storage/no-refund/account-deletion tests pass; rollback, then re-apply, both work.
  - Build passes. Headless on `vite preview` of the production build: list, detail, analyze and the delete flow work with no console errors.
- **Not verified / risks**:
  - Real providers: Gemini's header auth is documented, but no live call was made.
  - Production `auth.users` triggers during the check (rolled back either way).
  - Physical-device Try again flow.
  - If the caller's JWT expires mid-run, the final save and the fail write both fail. The sweep then marks the row `worker_lost` later. Option A fixes this.
  - Option B leaves `stage`/`failure_*` client-writable (self-only).

## Chat: open-from-notification jump, send-scroll, new-messages pill, round chat layout — 2026-11-05 (branch `fix/chat-notif-jump`, uncommitted/not deployed; golfme-architect; no migration)

- **Notification jump** (Micky 2026-11-04): React Router 7 navigations are transitions, so DirectMessageThread's passive effects ran after its first paint and every open (Inbox, bell, banner, push) painted one frame with an unsized box (oldest messages, no composer, page ~3100 px and unlocked) before snapping to the newest message. A screencast of headless frames shows it. Fix: `boxTop` measured, page locked and list re-pinned in layout effects; the mount effects also key on `hasThread`, so a thread opened before the golfer's profile loaded (push/cold start: "Golfer not found" first) still gets measured. Per-frame after: box 633 px and pinned on the first thread frame on every path.
- **Sending always scrolls to the newest message** (DM was broken: it only pinned when you were already near the bottom). The shared rule is `lib/chatScroll.ts` `followNewMessages`/`messagesAfter`, used by DM and round chat. Your own bubble (optimistic, confirmed or "Not sent") always pins. Keyed on the bubble, not a "just sent" flag, so a real DM whose bubble lands after the RPC still pins. Someone else's message pins only when you're near the bottom; otherwise it raises `components/chat/NewMessagesPill.tsx` (zero-height anchor above the composer, doesn't take focus; `chat.newMessages` ×6).
- **Round chat full-screen thread** (`GroupChat` layout "page") now uses the DM layout via `lib/useChatViewportBox.ts`: box sized to the keyboard top / above the nav, only the list scrolls, page locked at 0. Before, docking scrolled the page 251–339 px, so the header/Back was off-screen while typing (one tap didn't leave), and on iPhone Safari the composer sat 195 px under the keyboard. The inline card on the round page still docks (unchanged). DM's own inline copy of this logic is left as is (frozen).
- Checks: tsc, lint, 367/367 node tests (new `tests/chatFollow.test.ts`), build, `reactions.mjs` 28/28, fake-keyboard before/after on every screen (DM unchanged; round chat now matches DM's composer profile). **iPhone checks pending (app + Safari):** open from banner/bell/push without a jump; round chat header stays put and Back works in one tap with the keyboard open; keyboard stays up after send; pill tappable with the keyboard open. Known leftover: DM reads `window.innerHeight` at render but re-renders only on `boxTop` changes (a Safari toolbar resize can leave it stale).

"50+ mi"); its upper-edge `distanceMiles` is never printed. Without a bucket (demo, or a database without the RPCs) a real distance reads in whole miles via `roundedMiles`. ## Interaction audit fixes — 2026-11-04 (branch `fix/interaction-audit`, includes `fix/chat-composer-keyboard`; not deployed)

Micky's "fix unnatural interactions and misleading feedback" request; audit + plan approved (remove both fake switches, add report columns, chat via the specialist).
- **Misleading switches removed** (Settings): "Email updates — weekly digest" and "Discoverable to nearby golfers" were `useState(true)`, never saved, nothing enforced (profiles RLS is `using (true)`, discovery RPCs ignore any visibility; no digest exists). Replaced by an honest note: signed-in golfers can see your profile and message you; block to stop someone. Real switches (push, new rounds, circle posts) stay disabled until their save answers.

Reports now carry post/comment/message ids (`lib/reportRow.ts`); until migration **`20261104120000_add_report_post_comment_ids` (APPLIED 2026-11-04)** exists, a missing-column error retries without them and appends `[post …, comment …]` to details. The migration adds `reported_post_id`/`reported_comment_id` (FK set null, partial indexes), puts them in the founder alert email and `admin_list_reports` (comment reports resolve their post); AdminDashboard links to the reported post. Review: Circle add + share prompt only after success.
- **Sheets** (`Modal`): Escape closes only the topmost sheet (`lib/modalStack`); focus moves in and returns to the opener (never back into a text field); labelled dialog; backdrop closes only on a full tap on the backdrop (no iOS click-through). `ConfirmDialog` can't be dismissed while its action runs (delete account, leave/cancel/complete round).
- **One counted page scroll lock** (`lib/scrollLock`) everywhere (Modal, media viewer, Coach Replay fullscreen, and via the chat branch PostDetail, GroupChat, DM thread, ProfileSetup): released in any order, never stuck.
- **Course search (Host a Round)**: minimal keyboard adjustment (`lib/minimalReveal`: smallest scroll that keeps the field + ~2.5 suggestions above the keyboard, none if it fits), re-checked only on keyboard/content resize; no 600 ms re-scroll, no snap-back. List in flow (not clipped in sheets), bounded, "no matching courses" row, combobox/listbox ARIA, arrow keys/Enter (IME-safe)/Escape, blur timer fixed.
- Search lists: CoursePicker keeps results while searching; LocationPicker reserved status line, stable rows, drops a location fix that arrives after close; golfer search drops a stale in-flight answer after clearing.
- Saves: avatar writes the profile before deleting the old file, errors shown, buttons disabled while busy; onboarding Continue waits for an uploading photo; profile Save and share-your-round can't double submit.
- Gestures: touchcancel springs back (tab carousel, notification popup, media viewer); RangeSlider pointercancel.
- Not done: block/unblock still fire-and-forget (callers in frozen DM thread / comments); a profile visibility setting (needs its own migration + discovery filters); share-your-round still stores a data-URL image.

## Chat / comment keyboard fixes — 2026-11-04 (branch `fix/chat-composer-keyboard`; not deployed)

Web-only change, no migration, no native config. DM thread's viewport/keyboard code untouched (only its body lock moved to `lockPageScroll`).
- **Composers dock only for their own field** (`src/lib/composerFocus.ts` rule + `src/lib/useComposerFocus.ts`): PostDetail's comment box and GroupChat docked on ANY keyboard (the plugin's events don't say which field), so an inline reply or a sheet's textarea docked the unrelated composer and locked the page, and the reveal hook then skipped the field being typed in. Now dock = keyboard open AND focus last landed inside that composer (another text field takes ownership away; a button tap or blur leaves it, so Post/Send and closing behave as before). The scroll-position capture moved from onFocus into the hook (runs before docking). On undock because another field took the keyboard, the composer no longer scrolls the page back. `useRevealFocusedFieldAboveKeyboard` now schedules its reveal before the "page locked?" check (re-checked a frame later), so a field that takes the keyboard from a docked composer is revealed once the lock is released.
- **Round chat no longer drags the page**: GroupChat called `scrollIntoView` on every message (scrolls the window too). Now DM's rule (`src/lib/chatScroll.ts`, 120 px): pin the list's own scrollTop only if near the bottom or for your own just-sent message. Also fixed: the list used `justify-end` on the scroller, which makes scrollHeight == clientHeight once it overflows (older round messages were unreachable, pins were no-ops); now DM's leading `mt-auto` spacer.
- **IME**: Enter while composing (isComposing / Safari keyCode 229) no longer sends: ChatComposer (DM + round), comment box, inline reply, Golf Calls search (`src/lib/isImeComposing.ts`).
- Inline reply: ref-based in-flight guard + disabled button (double Enter/tap posted twice). PostCard "Comment" on the detail page: native skips the smooth scroll and focuses with preventScroll (raced the dock's scroll). Body locks in PostDetail, GroupChat, DM thread, ProfileSetup -> `lockPageScroll` (Modal/FullscreenMediaViewer/CaddieSwingReplay are on a parallel branch).
- Tests: `tests/chatKeyboard.test.ts` in `npm run test:chat` (27). All suites, tsc, lint (0 errors), build pass. Headless (Capacitor bridge faked as iOS, keyboard events fired, demo): composer docks for itself; reply input -> composer not docked, page unlocked, reply above the keyboard; docked composer -> focus reply -> undocks and reply revealed; IME Enter keeps text, real Enter posts; reply double Enter posts once; round card: page stays at 0 on load/send, list pinned, own send pins while reading back; DM lock released on leave.
- **Needs iPhone**: DM open/close keyboard (unchanged), round chat card + `/messages/round/:id`, comments + inline reply + Report sheet over comments, tapping Reply while the comment box has the keyboard, Japanese/Chinese/Korean IME Enter in each composer.

## Caddie measured-verdict cue check + analysis language — 2026-11-03 (branch `fix/caddie-verdicts-language`; LIVE 2026-11-03, see Last updated)

Micky's plan items E + F. **Needs an Edge Function deploy only Micky can approve: `analyze-swing` and `translate-caddie-analysis` (both import the new `_shared/caddieVerdicts.ts` + moved `_shared/swingAssessment.ts`).** Then a web deploy for the client part. No migration.
- **E — why Gemini can't be shown the verdicts**: the measured verdicts (swingAssessment) need Gemini's own `phases` + `camera_angle`, which come back in the same single call that writes `checkpoint_cues`. Feeding verdicts in would need a second Gemini call or a server-side phase detector (out of scope). So the prompt is unchanged and the check runs after Gemini returns.
- **E — the check** (`withMeasuredCueCheck`, `supabase/functions/_shared/caddieVerdicts.ts`): the server measures exactly what the client draws (swingAssessment moved to `_shared`, `src/lib/swingAssessment.ts` re-exports it; one pose/phases JSON parser for both sides, used by RealCaddieContext too). A cue at address/top/impact is dropped (nothing stored, text never rewritten) only if it names body parts (keyword groups in en/es/ja/ko/zh-CN/zh-TW) and none fits a measured fault at that moment. No body part named, no reliable fault there, or follow_through -> kept. Never throws. translate-caddie-analysis runs it too, so older rows get it on translate. Client display is unchanged (pickCue already shows the fault's own cue).
- **F — language**: analyze-swing writes `analysis_json.language` = the validated request locale (client already sent `locale`); translate-caddie-analysis sets it to the new locale. CaddieAnalysisDetail shows "This feedback is in {language}." + the existing Translate button when it differs from the app locale (`src/lib/caddieLanguage.ts`, key `caddie.feedbackLanguageNotice`, 6 locales). Rows without the field keep today's behaviour (header Translate button, no notice).
- Tests: `tests/caddieVerdicts.test.ts` (11: server/client parity, 6-language body parts, look-alikes like 팔로우/不足/handle, drop/keep rules, malformed input) + `tests/caddieLanguage.test.ts` (2), in `npm run test:caddie` (98). All suites, tsc, lint (0 errors), build pass (landing prerender skipped: no env in the worktree). Not checked in a browser: the notice needs a real row with a language.
- Later (not done): a prompt hint listing the measured checks, so Gemini's cue agrees more often (needs a live A/B on real swings); a "good" direction check (a cue correcting a part measured as fine), held back because the coarse groups would drop valid cues (e.g. wrist hinge vs measured hand height).

## Course search + location selection — 2026-11-03 (branch `fix/course-location-search`, based on `fix/signin-support`; not deployed)

Micky: "need to expand scope for course search and location selection" (Rock Spring Golf Club not found when hosting). NYC round-journey must-fix #2.
- **Root cause, course search**: `course-search` sent the typed text to Geoapify Places as `text=`, a parameter Places doesn't have, so it was ignored and every "search" returned the 20 golf courses nearest the searcher whatever was typed. Courses outside that ring (Rock Spring, West Orange NJ, from Manhattan) could never be found. Now a name search asks Places with `name=` (golf-course category) and the Geocoding API with `text=&type=amenity`, keeps only golf courses (category or golf-like name, then the existing mini-golf/range filter), merges and de-duplicates (`supabase/functions/_shared/geoapifySearch.ts`). Either provider failing alone still returns the other's results.
- **Location selection**: new `place` mode on the same function: a typed town / neighbourhood / zip becomes a labelled point rounded to 2 decimals (~1 km); city/suburb/district/postcode/county only, never a street; nothing stored. LocationPicker (all 5 uses: onboarding, match preferences, Find, nearby courses) shows curated matches, then these results (real accounts, debounced 300 ms). Curated list gains the 5 boroughs, Jersey City, Hoboken, Newark, Yonkers, White Plains, Garden City, Farmingdale, Stamford (works offline and in demo). "Use X as my area" (name only, no coordinates) now says it disables nearby rounds and alerts. "Limited course data" shows in demo only.
- **Host a course that isn't listed**: real accounts must choose the course's town (LocationPicker) before Next, so every new round has coordinates (the town's, labelled "near {area} (approximate)"). Before, a typed course saved null coordinates and the round was invisible to distance features and new-round alerts.
- Tests: `tests/geoapifySearch.test.ts` (URLs really send the name, golf-only geocoder hits, merge/dedupe, area labels + rounding; synthetic fixtures, no network). All 225 tests, tsc, lint, build pass; the function typechecks standalone (Deno names aside). Demo headless check of the picker. **Needs Micky's OK to deploy `course-search`** (the web part works before it: place search just shows its "couldn't search" line, curated list still works). After deploy: search "Rock Spring" when hosting; type "Astoria" in the location picker.
- Not changed: existing rounds/profiles with no coordinates (they need the owner to re-pick; a prompt for that is a follow-up), the 25 mi default radius.

- `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: on pushes to main and on pull requests — `npm ci`, `npx tsc -b`, `npm run lint`, `node --test tests/*.test.ts supabase/functions/_shared/*.test.ts` (every test file, so one left out of a `test:*` script still runs), `npm run build`. ## Caddie cue consistency + oversized findings — 2026-11-03 (branch `fix/signin-support`; not deployed)

Micky's priorities plan, items B + D (client only; E, server-side verdict alignment, stays later).
- **B — the cue agrees with the card** (`pickCue` in `src/lib/coachReplay.ts`): Gemini writes `checkpoint_cues` without seeing the pose measurement, and its cue used to replace the cue of a measured fault, so a card could say "hands stayed low at the top" under a cue about something else. Now a measured fault (source `assessment.*`, already reliability-gated: tracked joints at a trusted phase) always takes its own fixed cue; a Gemini-sourced fix (score reason, work_on, focus) takes Gemini's cue; a card with only a measured "going well" line gets no cue; an analysis "going well" line keeps Gemini's cue. Each fixed cue was checked against its fault text (test covers all 7).
- **D — a finding taller than the card's page**: the no-scroll paging (`ce540d0`) kept a single oversized block whole, so a long Japanese/Korean/Chinese sentence could still grow the card past its 40% share. `paginateWithSentences` (`src/lib/coachPaging.ts`) pages such a block sentence by sentence (`Intl.Segmenter`, regex fallback; each sentence measured with its label); sentences on one page are shown as one finding, the label repeats on each page, nothing is cut. Blocks that fit page exactly as before.
- Tests: `npm run test:caddie` 85 (cue rule incl. the contradiction case; paging by sentence, every sentence once). Headless harness (temporary, removed) at 375x667 and 390x844: Japanese and English long text, every page ≤ 40% of the screen (max 39.7%), 0 overlap with the clip, no scroll, no cut text, every stored sentence shown. CJK font missing in the headless browser, so verify Japanese on an iPhone.

## NYC launch Phase 3 "round conversations" — 2026-11-03 (branch `fix/round-chat`, based on `fix/launch-trust`; branch, not deployed)

- **Chat inbox**: a Direct | Rounds switch (cleaner than a second section at 375px; the two lists have separate load states) plus a pinned **next-round card** (earliest open/full round you host or joined whose tee time hasn't passed: course, when, players, Open chat / View round). Rounds list: course, date/time, last message with sender, time; upcoming by tee time, then past/ended. Uses the Phase 1 load/error/empty states. Tab remembered per session.
- **`/messages/round/:id`** (`src/pages/RoundChatThread.tsx`): header (course + date, taps to `/golf-calls/:id`), back (history, else `/messages`), `GroupChat layout="page"` (fixed svh-based list height; docking code unchanged). The round page's chat card links to it ("Open chat"). Non-member / left ("You left this round — its chat is no longer available") / blocked / hidden rounds get a locked note, never messages; same rule on the round page (`src/lib/roundChat.ts` `roundChatAccess`). Blocked members (block either way with the host) are now locked on the round page too.
- **Pending / sent / failed** round messages in `RealRoundsContext` (in memory): sending (dimmed) -> sent (kept until the refetch has its id) -> or failed ("Not sent · Tap to retry" + Discard). Content-filter rejections remove the bubble and put the text back. RLS refusals refetch so the read-only state shows. Demo stays instant/local.
- **Read-only after the round**: completed/cancelled rounds show history with a read-only note instead of the composer. Server: migration **`20261103120000_lock_round_chat_after_round` (APPLIED 2026-11-03, see Last updated)** adds `status in ('open','full')` to the insert policy, keeping sender/membership/block checks; rollback + verify SQL in its header.
- My Rounds "Upcoming" now uses `isUpcomingRound` (tee time not passed); past-dated open/full rounds move to Past with "Not marked complete".
- Tests: `npm run test:roundchat` (25: next-round selector incl. NY/Tokyo edges, Rounds ordering, send lifecycle, access decisions, static policy/rollback guard). All suites, tsc, lint (0 errors), build pass. Demo screenshots at 390x844 (headless shell + `~/.cache/golfme-chromium-libs` LD_LIBRARY_PATH). Needs two real accounts + iPhone: keyboard docking in the full-screen thread, real failed/retry, the policy after applying.
- Not done (later): unread counts / push for round chats; the unfiltered `round_messages` fetch + refetch-on-every-change is unchanged (fine at current volume).

## NYC launch Phase 1 "trust fixes" — 2026-11-03 (branch `fix/launch-trust`, not deployed)

- **Notify me is honest**: `RoundAlertsCard` (`src/lib/roundAlerts.ts`) replaces the fake toast on Golf Calls / Auto-Match empty results. It turns on the existing new-round alerts (`profiles.notify_new_rounds` via `saveProfile`), says they cover new rounds near the home area within N mi (fan-out radius rule), not this search, max 3/day; states: on / off / saving / save failed + retry / push off or denied (-> Settings) / web (bell only) / no home area (-> `/profile/preferences`). Asks for push only on iPhone while permission is "prompt". Demo: local switch. No backend change. `find.willNotify`/`notifyMe`/`notifiedMe` removed, "we'll let you know" copy dropped (6 locales).
- **Load error vs empty**: RealRounds/RealCommunity/RealSocial expose `loadError`, `refreshFailed`, `retry()` (`src/lib/loadStatus.ts`); `hasLoaded` unchanged (first attempt settled). Golf Calls, Auto-Match, My Rounds, Chat inbox list, Community show loading / error + Try again / genuine empty; a later refresh failure keeps the data with a small notice. Round/post/golfer deep links show the error + retry instead of "not found" after a failed load.
- **Caddie sharing**: Share Caddie Feedback never posts twice when the shared mark fails (mark retried once, then logged). CreatePost removes the public `swing-<uuid>` copies/uploads a failed attempt created (own folder, community-media only, only if no post row references them).
- Tests: `npm run test:trust` (32, mocked, no network) + existing suites pass; tsc, lint (0 errors), build clean. No screenshots (headless shell lacks system libs here). Needs a real account + iPhone: the alert save, the native push prompt from the card, and error states with the network off.

The golfer can Remove/Change it there or later on Profile; it's only offered during onboarding, so a removed photo never comes back. Apple never sends a photo, so Apple sign-ins keep initials.

Needs a real Google sign-in check on web + iPhone: photo shows at setup, is copied (URL is `.../storage/v1/object/public/avatars/...`), and Remove leaves initials. No migration.

## New-round + Golf-Circle-post notifications — 2026-10-30 (LIVE since 2026-11-02)

- Approved by Micky ("go with your recommendations"). **New open real round** -> golfers who could join: course inside the recipient's `travel_radius_miles` (<=0 -> 25, capped at the slider max 100) from their saved `playing_area_lat/lng` (rounds or recipients without coordinates are skipped). Skill excludes only a 2-tier gap where both sides are known (`skill_level`, else handicap tiers). Availability is checked only if the golfer saved slots (time unreadable -> weekday/weekend only). Excludes the host, blocks either way, banned (`auth.users.banned_until`) / deleted / not-onboarded accounts, anyone already joined, and `private.notification_fanout_muted_users`. **Max 3 per recipient per rolling 24h.** **New community post** (swing included) -> the poster's followers (`follows.following_id = poster`) minus blocks / banned / not onboarded, **max 1 per poster->recipient per 2h**, and skipped for a follower already told about the round the post shares.
- Bell + push are one unit: over a cap or switched off -> neither; push off or no device -> bell only (still counts toward the cap). Copy in the recipient's `profiles.language` (6 locales, null -> English), e.g. "New round near you" / "New round at Dyker Beach · Tue 12:15 · 3 spots open" (date added when >6 days out); post push = poster name / text preview (DM preview rule) or "Shared a new post"/"…swing video". Taps: `/golf-calls/<id>`, `/community/<id>` (existing `link_to` routing).
- Settings -> Notifications: "New rounds near me" / "Posts from my Golf Circle", default ON, stored as `profiles.notify_new_rounds` / `notify_circle_posts` (same pattern as `push_enabled`, via `saveProfile`). Demo keeps them local and never fans out (demo never writes to Supabase).
- Async + idempotent: AFTER INSERT triggers on `golf_calls` / `community_posts` -> `private.notification_fanout_jobs` (unique `(kind, source_id)`) -> pg_net POST `{job_id}` (sent after commit) to the new **`notify-fanout`** Edge Function (verify_jwt off, same `x-internal-secret` as send-push) -> `claim_notification_fanout()` (service_role only) selects recipients in ONE set-based statement under a global advisory xact lock (exact rate limits), records them in `private.notification_fanout_deliveries` (PK `(job_id, recipient_id)`), writes bell rows only for newly recorded recipients, and returns pushes owed. The function batches token lookup + APNs (`_shared/apns.ts`, 50 in flight, dead tokens removed), then `finish_notification_fanout()`. Retried claims never duplicate rows; the next run sweeps stale jobs (`list_stale_notification_fanout_jobs`); jobs >6h old expire unsent. Push delivery is at-least-once only in the narrow crash-between-APNs-and-finish window. `send-push` is untouched.
- Tests: PGlite on the real profiles/courses/golf_calls/messaging/follows/community migrations + this migration: 39/39 (radius in/out and per-user radius, no coords, both block directions, banned, not onboarded, toggles off, skill/availability, already joined, muted, 3/day cap + rolling window, post 2h pair cap, round-shared-in-post dedupe, idempotent enqueue/claim/finish, cancelled/deleted/expired/banned-actor skips, no-secret path, 6-locale copy, client roles denied on RPCs/private tables); apply + rollback paste files 41/41 incl. rollback-then-reapply. `notify-fanout` deno check + mocked-fetch runtime smoke (401/400, 2 sent / 1 dead removed, sweep). tsc, lint (0 errors), build clean. No browser screenshot this pass (headless Chrome is missing system libraries in this environment).
- Go-live (Micky): apply file -> optionally mute reviewer/test ids -> `supabase functions deploy notify-fanout --no-verify-jwt` -> web deploy. Paste/rollback/README handed over out of band. Verify on two real accounts + a TestFlight phone.

## Current phase

Phases 1-6 of the real-backend migration are implemented. Phase 1: real Supabase Auth. Phase 2: real profile/preferences/avatar/location persistence. Phase 3: real course search/discovery (Geoapify, via a Supabase Edge Function) — **`GEOAPIFY_API_KEY` is now set and confirmed working live** (verified 2026-09-21: real Geoapify-sourced courses in `courses`, including Hong Kong entries, most recently synced the same day — see "Supabase configuration status" below). Phase 4: real multiplayer Golf Calls — fully implemented and verified live with two real accounts. Phase 5: real DMs, blocks, reports, and in-app notifications — fully implemented and verified live, including catching and fixing a real RLS infinite-recursion bug via direct SQL testing before ever calling it done. Phase 6: round completion, real reviews, real basic credibility, account deletion, and a full real-vs-mock/security audit — **implemented; see `BACKEND_STATUS.md` for the full audit output** (system classification, security findings + fixes, TestFlight readiness lists, and an exact TestFlight test script — note that doc's own snapshot is from Phase 6/Phase 7-addendum and is now stale in the same way this file was; treat its classification table as directionally right but re-verify anything load-bearing). Phase 7 (2026-08-16): real Golf Circle/Following, a messaging-speed fix, an i18n completion pass, a real Find Friends/username feature, and — the largest piece — Community migrated off `localStorage` to a full real Supabase backend (posts/comments/votes/saved/hidden, real media/Swing Post video storage, an honest placeholder-only swing-analysis architecture, and Community embedded directly into Home). Community's sub-phase (7e) shipped long ago (confirmed live in production, real `community_posts` rows exist). Real swing analysis is **no longer placeholder-only** — see Phase 22: a real Roboflow+Gemini pipeline (`analyze-swing` Edge Function) has been live since before Phase 22 and has processed 56 real analyses (54 complete, 0 failed, as of 2026-09-21) — `src/lib/swingAnalysis.ts`'s `SwingAnalysisProvider` interface language elsewhere in this file describing it as "provider-ready only" is stale; the actual provider is Roboflow (pose) + Gemini (assessment), not that interface. GolfMe Reputation V1 (tier progression, replacing "Building Credibility") shipped 2026-09-18 — see Phase 22. As of 2026-09-21: 24 real users, 22 onboarded, 11 Golf Calls, 27 Community posts, 45 messages — this is genuinely-used beta, not a pre-launch shell.

Verified clean on both security and performance advisors.

All mock CRUD (golf calls, community, reviews, etc.) untouched — that array stays the same shared demo world every account still sees this phase.

Google button calls real OAuth; Email button collects an address and sends a real magic link with a "check your email" confirmation state; Apple button removed (out of scope this phase, was never real); "Try Demo Account" unchanged.

"Remove" deletes the Storage object for real accounts before clearing `photo_url`.
