Advisors clean.
  - `supabase/functions/course-search/index.ts` — new Edge Function, deployed and ACTIVE. Calls Geoapify's Places API (`categories=sport.golf`) server-side using a `GEOAPIFY_API_KEY` secret that never reaches the browser bundle (chosen over a client-side-with-referrer-restriction key, per Micky's call). Uses the caller's own forwarded JWT (not a service-role key) to upsert results into `courses`, so writes still go through that table's normal RLS. Returns a clear JSON error (never fake data) if the key is missing, the provider errors, or the request is malformed — verified live via direct curl (missing-key case returns exactly the intended message; a request with no Authorization header is correctly rejected with 401 by Supabase's own JWT gate before the function code even runs).
  - `src/lib/realCourseSearch.ts` (new) — real accounts' implementation of the same conceptual surface as the existing mock `courseSearch.ts` (search/nearby/getById/recommended), calling the Edge Function + `courses` table instead of the static fixture. `courseSearch.ts` itself is untouched — it's now explicitly demo mode's implementation (and still separately powers Golf Calls' distance display for existing mock round data, unrelated to this phase).
  - `src/lib/useCourseSearch.ts` (new) — two hooks (`useNearbyCourses`, `useCourseTextSearch`) that branch demo (synchronous fixture) vs. real (async, debounced 300ms on text search, loading/error state) so the three consumer components didn't need to duplicate that logic.
  - `src/components/ui/CourseSearchStatus.tsx` (new) — shared loading spinner / error+retry row, used by all three consumers. Real-mode-only; demo's search is synchronous and never errors, so this never renders for demo accounts.
  - `src/components/profile/CoursePicker.tsx`, `src/components/golfcall/CourseAutocomplete.tsx`, `src/pages/ProfileSetup.tsx` (onboarding's nearby-courses step) — all three switched from the old synchronous mock-only calls to the new hooks; demo mode's visible behavior is unchanged (confirmed by a Playwright regression pass — search, pick, add-to-preferred all still work, zero Edge Function calls fire in demo mode).
  - Location-aware ranking (New York + "Eisenhower" ranks the nearby course first) is handled by Geoapify's own `bias=proximity:lon,lat` parameter server-side, with a client-side haversine re-sort on top as a second pass — not purely trusting the provider's own ordering.
  - `src/data/regions.ts` and `src/lib/courses.ts` (the old 13-course NY-only fixture) are untouched — still demo mode's data, and still what Golf Calls' distance display uses for existing mock round data.

- **Phase 4, implemented, applied to `golfme-dev`, AND verified live with two real accounts:**
  - `20260813050000_create_golf_calls_and_participants` migration — new `public.golf_calls` (course snapshot: `course_id` FK to Phase 3's `courses` cache + `course_name`/`course_area_label`/`course_lat`/`course_lng` copied onto the round; `tee_time_source`/`tee_time_provider`/`external_tee_time_id` columns present but locked to `'user_entered'` — future tee-time-provider integration needs no schema change) and `public.round_participants` (unique on `(round_id, user_id)`, no client-facing insert/update policy at all). Both tables added to the `supabase_realtime` publication.
  - `join_golf_call()`/`leave_golf_call()` — SECURITY DEFINER functions, the *only* way membership changes: `join_golf_call` row-locks the round (`for update`) before counting joined participants against `total_spots`, so two golfers hitting "join" on the last spot at the same instant are serialized by Postgres itself rather than racing. This is the actual database-side atomic solution the phase asked for, not a frontend check — see live test results below for verification.
  - `20260813051500_add_host_golf_call_function` migration — `host_golf_call()`, a second SECURITY DEFINER function that atomically creates the round *and* seats the host as a `round_participants` row in one transaction (the host occupies one of `total_spots`, same as the existing mock's `joinedGolferIds` having the host at index 0 — this was a correctness gap caught and fixed *before* it ever shipped: without it, `join_golf_call`'s fullness check would have undercounted by one and let a full round accept one extra player).
  - `src/lib/golfCall.ts` (new) — maps a real round + its participants onto the existing (mock-shaped) `GolfCall` type, the same trick as Phase 1's profile mapper. This is why so little UI needed to change: `GolfCallCard`, `GolfCallDetail`, `MyRounds`, `GolfCalls` (Find/browse), compatibility scoring, and round filters all work unchanged for real rounds — they just consume whatever `golfCalls` the branch below hands them.
  - `src/context/RealRoundsContext.tsx` (new) — fetches all real rounds + participants + resolves participant profiles (batch query against `profiles`), exposes `hostRound`/`joinRound`/`leaveRound`/`cancelRound`, and holds one global Realtime subscription (`postgres_changes` on both tables) that triggers a refetch on any change — satisfies "membership changes appear across devices without restarting" without per-route subscription lifecycle management.
  - `src/context/DataContext.tsx` — `golfCalls`, `getGolfCall`, `getGolfer` now branch demo vs. real (real participant ids resolve against `RealRoundsContext`'s profile cache, not the shared mock `golfers` array); `joinGolfCall`/`leaveGolfCall`/`cancelGolfCall` are now `Promise`-returning and branch the same way, surfacing real atomic-join failures (e.g. "This round is full.") to the UI instead of silently no-op'ing.
  - `src/pages/CreateGolfCall.tsx` — real hosting is "Starting Fresh" only (Fill My Foursome's friend-invite needs a real Golf Circle/Following that doesn't exist yet — approved scope trim) and instant-join only (request-to-join's approval flow deferred — also approved). Real course picks now carry the picked course's `courses.id`/lat/lng through to the host call, not just its name.
  - `src/pages/GolfCallDetail.tsx`, `src/components/golfcall/GolfCallCard.tsx` — join/leave/cancel now `await` and show a toast on failure instead of assuming success; the "🧪 Simulate round completion" prototype button is now demo-only (no real "round is over" detection exists yet for real rounds, out of scope this phase).
  - Chat and reviews on real rounds are explicitly out of scope this phase (not in the phase brief) — a real round's chat tab is a local, non-synced mock thread; `isCompleted` never becomes true for a real round, so the review flow never triggers. Flagged before building, not silently skipped.

- **Phase 5, implemented, applied to `golfme-dev`, AND verified live (including catching a real bug before shipping it):**
  - `20260813210000_create_messaging_blocks_reports_notifications` migration — `conversations`/`conversation_participants`/`messages` (1:1 DMs, found-or-created via `get_or_create_dm_conversation()`), `blocks`, `reports` (now with the `status` field the old mock never had), `notifications`. Block enforcement lives in `messages`' own INSERT policy, not just client UI — blocking someone makes new messages to them fail at the database. `notifications` has no client-facing insert policy at all; every row comes from one of 4 trigger functions (`notify_round_joined`/`notify_round_left`/`notify_round_cancelled`/`notify_new_message`) that fire automatically off `round_participants`/`golf_calls`/`messages` changes — a notification is a guaranteed side effect of the real event, never something the client has to remember to also do.
  - `20260813211500_lock_down_notification_trigger_functions` — same class of fix as Phase 1's `handle_new_user()` lockdown: the 4 trigger functions were directly callable via `/rest/v1/rpc/notify_*` by anon/authenticated by default (Postgres grants EXECUTE to PUBLIC on every new function). Not exploitable as written (NEW/OLD are unassigned outside real trigger context, so a direct call just errors), but closed off anyway — caught by re-checking advisors after the first migration, not left for later.
  - **`20260814000000_fix_conversation_rls_recursion` — a real bug caught via direct SQL testing before this phase was ever reported done.** `conversation_participants`' own select policy subqueried `conversation_participants` itself ("am I a fellow participant of this conversation") — a raw self-referencing subquery inside an RLS policy re-triggers that same policy's evaluation on every row, which is infinite recursion, not just slow. `conversations`' and `messages`' select policies (and the block-check half of the messages insert policy) hit the same recursion indirectly, since they subquery `conversation_participants` too. Fixed with the standard pattern: two new SECURITY DEFINER helper functions (`is_conversation_participant()`, `conversation_other_participants()`) whose internal queries bypass RLS entirely, breaking the recursion. Verified fixed by re-running the exact query that had failed.
  - `src/context/RealSocialContext.tsx` (new) — same shape as `RealRoundsContext`: fetches the caller's conversations/messages/blocks/notifications, resolves participant/actor profiles (batch query against `profiles`), one global Realtime subscription (`messages`/`notifications`/`blocks`) that refetches on any change.
  - `src/context/DataContext.tsx` — `canMessage`/`dmConversations`/`messagesWithGolfer`/`sendDirectMessage`/`markConversationRead`/`isBlocked`/`blockedIds`/`blockUser`/`unblockUser`/`reportUser`/`notifications`/`unreadNotificationCount`/`markNotificationRead`/`markAllNotificationsRead` all branch demo vs. real now; `getGolfer` extended to also check `RealSocialContext`'s profile cache (DM partners, blocked users, notification actors — not just round participants). `sendDirectMessage`/`markConversationRead` are now `Promise`-returning for real accounts.
  - Genuinely good news this phase: the existing mock DM system already matched two of the phase's hard constraints without any changes — `canMessage` never required Following, and the DM route/UI was already keyed by golfer id (not a separate conversation id), so `Inbox.tsx` and `DirectMessageThread.tsx` needed **zero changes** beyond `handleSend` becoming `async` for the new `Promise<boolean>` return.
  - `src/components/notifications/NotificationsPanel.tsx` — added icons for the 4 new real notification types (TypeScript's own exhaustiveness check on the icon map caught this was needed, not a manual audit). The bell's open/close/outside-click/Escape behavior in `TopBar.tsx`/`Modal.tsx` was **not touched at all** — confirmed still working via live click-through (same-click toggle, backdrop click, and Escape all independently verified after this phase's changes landed).

- **Phase 6, implemented and applied to `golfme-dev`:**
  - `20260814010000_create_round_reviews_and_credibility` migration — `public.round_reviews` (one row per reviewer/reviewee/round, `UNIQUE(round_id, reviewer_user_id, reviewed_user_id)` and `CHECK(reviewer_user_id <> reviewed_user_id)` at the database level, not just app logic) and `get_credibility_stats()` (computes Completed Rounds / Would Play Again % / Show-Up % / On-Time % / Handicap Confidence live from `round_participants`/`round_reviews`, genuine zeros for a new account, never fabricated) plus `submit_round_review()` (SECURITY DEFINER, enforces same-completed-round-required and no-self-review at the function level too, defense in depth alongside the constraints).
  - `src/context/RealRoundsContext.tsx` — added `completeRound` (host-only, direct `golf_calls` update relying on the existing `golf_calls_update_host` RLS policy — "keep beta logic simple" per the phase brief, no auto-detection of "the round happened"), `hasReviewed`/`submitReview` wired to the new RPC.
  - `src/lib/useCredibility.ts` (new) — `useCredibilityStats()` hook, calls `get_credibility_stats()` for real accounts only, falls through to the existing (unmodified) `src/lib/credibility.ts` baseline for demo accounts — that file already degraded correctly to an honest baseline when fed real zeros, so it was reused as-is rather than forked.
  - `src/pages/ReputationDetail.tsx`, `src/pages/Profile.tsx`, `src/pages/GolferProfilePage.tsx` — wired to the new hook. `GolferProfilePage.tsx` needed the hook call moved above its early-return `if (!golfer)`/`Navigate` guards to satisfy rules-of-hooks (caught by oxlint as an error, not just a lint warning).
  - `src/pages/GolfCallDetail.tsx` — new real-only "Mark Round Completed" button (host-only, guarded by `isHost && !isCompleted && !isCancelled`).
  - `supabase/functions/delete-account/index.ts` (new) — deletes the caller's own `auth.users` row (never a client-supplied id, derived from the verified JWT) via `auth.admin.deleteUser()` using the service-role key, which stays server-side only. Cascades through every table that references the deleted user; `reports.reported_user_id` is `on delete set null` on purpose, so a report survives the reported person's deletion as a moderation record.
  - `src/pages/Settings.tsx` — real-only "Danger Zone" section, confirm dialog, calls the new Edge Function.
  - **CORS bug found and fixed** (see `BACKEND_STATUS.md` Security audit for full detail): neither `delete-account` nor `course-search` had ever been successfully callable from a real browser — both rejected the CORS preflight `OPTIONS` request outright. Fixed on both, redeployed (both now at Edge Function version 2), reverified via direct `OPTIONS` requests (204 + correct `Access-Control-Allow-*` headers).
  - **Privacy bug found and fixed**: `conversation_other_participants()`, a `SECURITY DEFINER` helper meant only for internal RLS use, was directly RPC-callable by any signed-in user with no membership check, leaking who else is in an arbitrary DM thread. Fixed via `20260814020000_move_conversation_helpers_to_private_schema` (below) — moved it (and `is_conversation_participant()`) into a non-API-exposed `private` schema, re-pointed the 4 affected RLS policies at the new location. Verified: direct RPC route now 404s; real messaging (tested via a live simulated session) still works; advisor warning gone.
  - Full real-vs-mock system classification, security audit, two-account test consolidation, and TestFlight readiness lists/script: written up in the new `BACKEND_STATUS.md`, not duplicated here.

This is the deterministic half of requirement E (does the guard reject a full round); the race-both-clicking-simultaneously half is covered by code review of the `for update` row-lock pattern (a standard, well-understood Postgres idiom for exactly this problem), not by literally firing two simultaneous requests, since only two real accounts were available to test with.

## Two-provider real course architecture (2026-08-16)

Replaces the single-provider (Geoapify-only) course design from Phase 3 with a canonical-course-plus-mappings architecture, driven by a live finding: GolfCourseAPI (a real, free-tier course-data API Micky brought in, ~30k courses) turned out to have **no location search capability at all** — confirmed live before building anything (searching "Farmingdale," a real town with real courses, returns 0 results; searching "New York" only matches a club literally named that) and **no coordinates in its response**, only address/city/state/country text. That ruled out "replace Geoapify with GolfCourseAPI," which was the first plan, once it was clear GolfCourseAPI can't do the one thing ("nearby courses near your location") that was the actual point.

Revised, implemented architecture:
- **Geoapify stays the location-discovery provider** (`course-search` Edge Function, unchanged provider, same real/nearby search it's had since Phase 3) — it's the one of the two that can actually search by coordinates and already returns lat/lng.
- **GolfCourseAPI becomes an on-demand, best-effort enrichment provider** (`course-enrich`, new Edge Function) — given a course GolfMe already found via Geoapify, searches GolfCourseAPI by name and, on a confident match (name-token overlap AND a city/state cross-check — never attaches across a location mismatch, verified against real data below), attaches real hole-count data. Deliberately on-demand per course (never batched over a search-result list) because the free tier is 50 requests/day; `courses.golfcourseapi_checked_at` guarantees a course is ever checked at most once.
- **`public.course_external_ids`** (new table) maps a canonical `courses.id` to each provider's own id, so the same physical course found by both providers ends up as one canonical row with two mappings, never a duplicate. `upsert_external_course()` (find-or-create, for course-search) and `attach_external_course_mapping()` (attach-to-known-course, for course-enrich) are the only two sanctioned write paths — same "no direct client insert" discipline as `round_participants`.
- Extended `courses` with `normalized_name`, `address`, `holes`, `course_type`/`website`/`phone`/`description` (nullable, unpopulated for now — GolfCourseAPI's real response doesn't actually include these last three, so they're schema-ready for a future provider that does, never fabricated from thin air).
- Real-only "Entered by the host — not confirmed with the course" disclaimer added to `GolfCallDetail`'s tee-time display, and `enrichRealCourse()` fires best-effort on viewing a real round's course.
- `src/lib/teeTimeProvider.ts` (new) — a `TeeTimeProvider` interface + a `MockTeeTimeProvider` that returns nothing (never fake slots), scaffolding for a future real tee-time integration. Not wired into any UI this round, per explicit scope decision (interface/schema only, no real tee-time provider yet).

**A real bug caught and fixed mid-build, not left in**: the first version of the migration's `revoke execute ... from public` on `upsert_external_course()` didn't actually strip `anon`'s ability to call it — this project's database auto-grants `EXECUTE` to `anon`/`authenticated` independently of the `PUBLIC` default on every new function (the same gotcha already documented for the Phase 5 notification triggers). Caught via `get_advisors`, fixed with an explicit `revoke ... from anon` in a follow-up migration, reverified via `information_schema.routine_privileges`.

**Verified live, not just code-reviewed:**
- Both provider APIs called directly with the real keys: Geoapify's original nearby/search behavior (unchanged since Phase 3, still blocked on `GEOAPIFY_API_KEY` never being set — see below) and GolfCourseAPI's actual `/v1/search` response shape, confirming no lat/lng field exists and confirming the no-location-search finding above.
- The enrichment name-matching heuristic, run against a real GolfCourseAPI response for "Bethpage": correctly picked "Bethpage State Park / Yellow" (score 1.00) over four other real Bethpage tee-course siblings (score 0.50 each, correctly below the winner), and correctly rejected every one of those same real results when tested against an unrelated "Pebble Beach, CA" course (the city/state cross-check working as designed, not just an assumption).
- `upsert_external_course()` and `attach_external_course_mapping()` end-to-end via direct SQL simulation of a real session: created a canonical "Bethpage Yellow" course from a simulated Geoapify result, then attached a simulated GolfCourseAPI match to the *same* course_id — confirmed exactly one canonical course row, both provider mappings present, `holes: 18` populated, name/city/region untouched (enrichment correctly didn't overwrite what Geoapify already had right).
- CORS preflight on the new `course-enrich` function: `204` with correct headers.
- Demo-mode regression (Playwright): Match Preferences' course picker still works on the mock fixture, an existing mock round's detail page still renders with zero errors and the new disclaimer correctly hidden, zero calls to `course-search`/`course-enrich`/either provider fired in demo mode.
- `npx tsc -b`, `npm run build`, `npm run lint`: all clean.

**Not verified live**: the full authenticated-browser round trip through the deployed Edge Functions (real login -> real nearby search -> real enrichment call), including the Hong Kong and New York test locations Micky specifically asked for. Blocked by two things: (1) the disposable test-inbox service used for magic-link testing was unreliable today (intermittent 500s/malformed responses, unrelated to GolfMe itself), and (2) **`GEOAPIFY_API_KEY` still has never been set** (open since Phase 3) — `course-search` would return its honest "not configured" 503 regardless of the inbox issue, since Geoapify is still the only real location-discovery provider. Both the HK/NYC live tests need `GEOAPIFY_API_KEY` set before they can run for real, independent of anything built this round.

**Legit courses only (2026-10-22; merged to main; `course-search` deployed live as v36 via the Supabase CLI, verify_jwt unchanged, v34 source backed up before deploy).** Micky saw Hong Kong's Nearby list show street names ("Ho Chung Road", "Shing Mun Catchwater", "Pak Kong Road"), a mini-golf course and driving ranges. Root cause: `course-search` named an unnamed Geoapify feature after `address_line1` (its street) and passed every `sport.golf_course` feature through. Fix: shared pure filter `supabase/functions/_shared/courseFilter.ts` (tests: `npm run test:courses`). Server: unnamed features dropped (no address fallback); OSM tags in `datasource.raw` (`leisure=miniature_golf`, `golf`/`golf_course`=driving_range/miniature, a non-course `leisure` such as sports_centre/pitch) and Geoapify's `entertainment.miniature_golf` category dropped; name keywords (EN/ZH/JA/KO) as a last resort, with "Golf Club"/"Country Club"/球會/ゴルフ場/골프장 protecting a course from range keywords. Filtered features are no longer cached. Geoapify is now always asked for 20 and the list cut back to `limit`. Response carries `courseFilter: 1`. Client (`realCourseSearch.ts`, nearby + search only, never `getRealCourseById`): same name check always; a street-name heuristic only while the response lacks `courseFilter` (old function). No migration: discovery lists never read the `courses` table, they only show what this Geoapify call returned, so old bad rows simply stop appearing; rounds that point at them keep working. Ship: web deploy = client guard; `course-search` deploy = the real fix. Follow-up (v36): a club word only shields a range keyword when the name joins them ("&"/"and"/與/及/和), so a club's own range ("珀麗高爾夫球會高爾夫球練習場") is dropped. Micky confirmed the HK Nearby list on iPhone.

**Swing video backdrop (2026-10-22).** Portrait swing-post videos in the feed letterboxed in black; `PostCard` now fills the sides with a blurred copy of the post's own thumbnail (iOS Photos style), dark slate when a post has no thumbnail. Other media and fullscreen viewers unchanged.

## Phase 7 — real Follow, messaging speed, i18n completion, Find Friends, real Community (2026-08-16)

Four sequenced sub-phases, each approved individually before the next started (user chose "one fix at a time i approve all get to work" after answering sequencing questions up front), followed by a separately-briefed Community overhaul.

**7a. Real Golf Circle / Following.** `20260816130657_create_follows.sql` — `public.follows` (composite PK `(follower_id, followed_id)`, `follows_no_self` check constraint, RLS: open select, insert/delete scoped to own `follower_id`). Found and fixed the same class of bug later found again in Community: several `DataContext.tsx` helpers (`hasPlayedWith`, `playedWithIds`, `visibleGolfers`) still read the raw mock `data.golfCalls`/`data.currentUserId` instead of the branched `golfCalls`/`currentUser.id`, so real accounts saw demo-world golfers bleeding through. `RealSocialContext.tsx` extended with `allProfiles`/`discoverableGolfers`/`follows`/`isFollowing`/`followingGolfers`/`followUser`/`unfollowUser` (optimistic update + rollback on failure).

**7b. Messaging speed.** Root cause of reported "lag": `sendDirectMessage()` pushed the message, then always called a trailing `refetch()` — a full reload on every single send, on top of the realtime subscription's own refetch already covering the same update. Fixed with an optimistic-render pattern: a client-generated pending message renders instantly, a `useEffect` watching the canonical realtime-fetched data drops the pending entry once a matching real row appears; the redundant trailing `refetch()` was removed.

**7c. i18n completion pass.** `GolfCallDetail.tsx` had never been wired to `useLocale()` at all (~35 new keys under `golfCallDetail.*`); `Settings.tsx`'s Danger Zone/delete/logout dialogs and `GolferProfilePage.tsx`'s Follow button text were hardcoded English. All new keys added across all 6 locale files (`en`/`es`/`ja`/`ko`/`zh-CN`/`zh-TW`), not just `en`.

**7d. Find Friends + username system.** `20260816133123_add_username_and_search_rpc.sql` — `profiles.username` (format-checked `^[a-zA-Z0-9_.]{3,30}$`, case-insensitive uniqueness via a partial unique index on `lower(username)` so multiple `NULL`s are allowed) + `search_golfer_profiles(p_query, p_limit)` RPC returning a deliberately narrow field set (name/username/avatar/handicap/area/credibility summary — not the same broad `SELECT *` the rest of `profiles` exposes, see the known broad-read risk in `BACKEND_STATUS.md`). New `src/pages/FindFriends.tsx` (real-only, redirects demo via `<Navigate>`), `src/lib/useFriendSearch.ts` (300ms-debounced hook). Existing users are prompted to pick a username in Profile edit but never blocked from using the app without one.

**7e. Community — full real migration (separately briefed).** Investigated before touching anything: Community had **zero real backend** — 100% `localStorage`-backed for every account, demo or real, exactly as `BACKEND_STATUS.md` already documented. The brief's premise ("posts should sync through the existing Supabase-backed Community system") was corrected before building, not assumed. Root cause of the reported "post creation fails to publish" bug: same identity-mismatch class as 7a — `createPost()` attributed every post's `authorId` to the fixed mock `data.currentUserId` placeholder rather than the real signed-in user, so real posts were created but misattributed to a mock persona, not actually failing to upload.

- `20260816143600_create_real_community.sql` — `community_posts` (type check includes `'swing'`, `swing_analysis_status` check), `community_comments` (one-level-deep replies via `parent_comment_id`), `community_post_votes`/`community_comment_votes` (composite-PK dedup, same pattern as `follows`), `saved_posts`/`hidden_posts` (select restricted to owner only, unlike the broadly-readable posts/comments/votes), all RLS, plus a new `community-media` Storage bucket (200MB limit, public read, own-folder-write) replacing base64 data-URLs for real accounts.
- `20260816143618_add_community_notification_triggers.sql` — extends `notifications.type`'s check constraint, adds `notify_community_comment()`/`notify_round_created_from_post()` trigger functions (locked down correctly on the first attempt this time — revoked `EXECUTE` from `public, anon, authenticated` together up front, not discovered piecemeal like the Phase 5/course-mapping gotchas).
- `src/context/RealCommunityContext.tsx` (new) — same shape as `RealRoundsContext`/`RealSocialContext`: parallel fetch, one global realtime subscription, optimistic-post-then-reconcile rendering, full real CRUD (create/delete/vote/save/hide/comment).
- `src/context/DataContext.tsx` — entire Community section renamed to `demoX` + real-branching wrappers, same convention as Follow. Caught by `oxlint`'s `exhaustive-deps` rule (not TypeScript): the main `value` `useMemo` was missing `posts`/`comments` from its dependency array — a real stale-closure bug, not a lint nitpick, fixed.
- **Swing Posts**: `src/lib/swingAnalysis.ts` defines a `SwingAnalysisProvider` interface kept deliberately separate from Community UI; the only implementation always throws rather than fabricating a result, matching the explicit "do not fake precise AI/CV measurements" instruction — `SwingAnalysisPanel.tsx` renders an honest "Swing analysis processing" placeholder instead. Real video upload to `community-media` (200MB client-side check matching the bucket's own limit) is fully wired; no code path ever transitions `swing_analysis_status` past `'pending'` since there is no real provider connected.
- **Home integration**: `src/pages/Home.tsx` now embeds a real Community feed section (newest 3 posts via `visiblePosts()`, reusing `PostCard` — no Home-only duplicate component) between the preferences nudge and the page's end, with a "See all" link to `/community`; replaced the old bottom-of-page plain-text `t("home.community")` hyperlink (key removed from all 6 locales, it had no other callers). Falls back to a compact "create the first post" CTA when `recentPosts` is empty rather than rendering an empty section.
- A real, separate pre-existing bug fixed along the way: `CreatePost.tsx`'s `posting` state was set `true` on submit but never reset to `false` on any code path (harmless in the common case since `navigate()` unmounts the component, but a real latent bug given the brief's explicit "prevent duplicate submissions" ask) — fixed via proper try/catch/finally handling in the rewritten `handlePost()`.

**Verified this phase:**
- `npx tsc -b`, `npm run lint`, `npm run build`: all clean after every sub-phase, including the final Home-integration change (zero new errors/warnings beyond the same pre-existing ones already present before this phase).
- Community's `notify_community_comment` trigger: verified via direct SQL role-simulation — caught and corrected a test-methodology bug along the way (the first check queried `notifications` while still impersonating the wrong user, RLS correctly hid the real, correctly-created row; switching the JWT claim to the actual recipient before the `select` showed the notification was there all along).
- `upsert_external_course`-style anon-grant gotcha was **not** repeated for `search_golfer_profiles`/`notify_community_comment`/`notify_round_created_from_post` — revoked from `public, anon, authenticated` together from the start this time, confirmed via `information_schema.routine_privileges`.

**Not verified live (browser, real two-account click-through):** Follow/unfollow between two real accounts, Find Friends search UX, and the full Community flow (text post, media post, Swing Post upload, cross-account visibility, mobile layout) — repeatedly blocked this session by disposable-inbox (mailinator) flakiness and Supabase's auth email-send rate limit, the same infrastructure constraints noted throughout Phases 1-6. Where live browser testing wasn't possible, correctness was instead verified via direct SQL role-simulation (`set local role authenticated` + `request.jwt.claims`), which is a real check of RLS/trigger/function behavior but not a substitute for a live click-through — flagged honestly rather than claimed as fully tested. **A caution self-caught mid-session**: while hunting for a throwaway test account, a magic-link email was accidentally sent to Micky's own real account (`mickylyu@gmail.com`) — caught immediately, no further action taken against that account, switched to a fresh disposable inbox instead.

Sub-phases 7a-7d (real Follow, messaging speed, i18n completion, Find Friends) are already committed and pushed. **Sub-phase 7e (Community) is implemented in the working tree but not yet committed, pushed, or deployed** — per standing project rule, awaiting explicit go-ahead for this round of changes.

## Phase 8 — restricted tee-time system for Skyway + Dyker Beach (2026-08-17)

Briefed as a full live-availability system (fetch real slots, auto-fill round creation, booking-status tracking, caching/freshness). Investigated both courses' real booking systems live before writing any code, per the brief's own priority order (public API > partner API > legitimate scrape > deep-link):

- **Skyway Golf Course at Lincoln Park West** (Jersey City, Hudson County-operated, 9 holes) books through **Lightspeed Golf** (formerly Chronogolf) — confirmed live, skywaygolfcourse.com's own "Members Area" link points at `members.chronogolf.com`. Lightspeed publishes an official Partner API (`partner-api.docs.chronogolf.com`) that can read a real tee sheet, but it's explicitly partner-gated (apply + get approved), not a public/anonymous API.
- **Dyker Beach Golf Course** (Brooklyn, one of 6 NYC courses operated by American Golf, 18 holes) books through **Tee It Up** (NBC Sports Next) at a NYC-American-Golf-specific site. Same story: no public API found.
- Both providers' real booking pages are JS-rendered and sit behind bot protection — returned a flat 403 to every plain server-side fetch attempted during research. Per explicit hard constraint (no bypassing anti-bot protection), this was not worked around.

**Conclusion, confirmed with Micky before building (AskUserQuestion, all 3 answered with the recommended option):** genuine live in-app availability isn't legitimately buildable today for either course — this is an access wall, not a shortcut being taken. Built exactly the fallback the brief's own Step 15/16 describes for this situation: the full modular provider architecture, honestly returning no availability, with "View Live Tee Times" (a plain link to each course's real official booking page — no invented/unverified URL query params, since neither provider's real page could be loaded to confirm deep-link params are honored) as the primary action. Integration scope was also explicitly narrowed on Micky's say-so to just the course+date picker and booking link — no round/Foursome/community-post auto-fill, no booking-status tracking, since there's no real selected slot to attach that data to yet.

- `src/services/teeTimes/types.ts` (new) — `SUPPORTED_TEE_TIME_COURSES` (exactly Skyway + Dyker Beach), `TeeTime`, `TeeTimeProvider`, `TeeTimeStatus`, `SelectedTeeTime` (types only; not wired into a UI yet, since no real selection flow exists — see integration-scope note above).
- `src/services/teeTimes/providers/skyway.ts`, `providers/dykerBeach.ts` (new) — each implements `TeeTimeProvider`; `getTeeTimes()` always resolves `[]` (never fabricated), `getBookingUrl()` returns the real, live-confirmed official booking URL for that course.
- `src/services/teeTimes/index.ts` (new) — `getProviderForCourse(courseId)` registry; adding a third course later is add-to-list + write-a-provider + register-here, nothing else changes.
- `supabase/functions/get-tee-times/index.ts` (new, deployed, ACTIVE, v1) — `GET ?course=&date=`, validates both params, returns an honest `teeTimes: []` payload (`liveAvailability: false` + a clear message) plus the real `bookingUrl`/`provider`/`holes`/`lastUpdated`. Same CORS-header convention as `course-search`/`course-enrich` (including `x-client-info`, learned the hard way earlier this project). **Live-verified via curl**, not just code-reviewed: OPTIONS preflight (204, correct headers), no-auth GET (401, Supabase's own JWT gate), valid GET for both courses (200, correct honest payload), unsupported course (400), malformed date (400).
- `src/lib/useTeeTimes.ts` (new) — hook wrapping `supabase.functions.invoke("get-tee-times?course=...&date=...", { method: "GET" })`, loading/error/retry state.
- `src/pages/TeeTimes.tsx` (new) — the 2-course list, entry point.
- `src/pages/TeeTimeCourseDetail.tsx` (new) — date picker (native `<input type="date">`, min=today) + loading/error/empty states + "View Live Tee Times" button (opens the real official page in a new tab) + a last-updated indicator. The live-teeTimes-list rendering branch is implemented (per the architecture requirement) but structurally unreachable today, same honesty posture as `SwingAnalysisPanel`'s "complete" branch.
- `src/App.tsx` — routes `/tee-times`, `/tee-times/:courseId` added.
- `src/pages/Find.tsx` — new entry-point card ("GolfMe -> Tee Times" per the brief's flow) above the existing Rounds/Golfers tabs.
- Removed `src/lib/teeTimeProvider.ts` — an earlier, unused (zero call sites, confirmed via grep) scaffold for a more general tee-time-provider concept tied to the main `courses` table; superseded by this narrower, brief-specified architecture at `src/services/teeTimes/`.
- `.env.example` — documents (comment-only, no real values) the two future partner-API secret names this would need, marked not-yet-set.
- All new UI strings localized across all 6 languages from the start (`teeTimes.*` keys), matching the discipline established in Phase 7.

**Deliberate deviation from the brief's literal `TeeTimeProvider` interface**, flagged rather than silent: `getBookingUrl()` takes `{ courseId, date? }` instead of `{ teeTime: TeeTime }` — since `getTeeTimes()` never returns a real `TeeTime` today, there's nothing to pass. Revisit this signature the day real availability exists and a specific slot needs its own booking URL.

**Not verified live:** clicking through the actual booking URLs in a browser (both `chronogolf.com/club/skyway-golf-course` and `new-york-american-golf.book.teeitup.com` returned 403 to WebFetch, the same bot-protection found during research) — their provenance is solid (read directly off each course's own official site navigation, not guessed), but "the link opens and shows the real booking page" is not something this session could click-test. Also not verified: any real live availability at all (impossible today, by design — see above). Mobile layout review was code-level only (reused `CLICKABLE_CARD_CLASS`/`EmptyState`/`GolfMeLoader`, all already mobile-verified elsewhere), not click-tested on a phone.

`npx tsc -b`, `npm run lint`, `npm run build`: all clean, zero new errors/warnings.

## Phase 9 — Caddie (AI swing-analysis history) + Home/Play/Caddie/Me nav (2026-08-17)

Nav restructured from Home/Find/My Golf/Profile to **Home/Play/Caddie/Me**: "Play" is the old "Find" relabeled (still merges Golf Calls + Discover + Tee Times + Auto-Match + Host a Round, none of them a separate destination); "Me" is the old "Profile" relabeled; **My Golf moved off the root tabs** — its page (`/my-rounds`, unchanged) is now reached via a row on the Me page instead of its own tab, since a 4-slot nav had to give something up for Caddie. `RootTabCarousel.tsx` (the swipe-between-root-tabs component, easy to miss since it hardcodes its own panel list separately from `nav.ts`) was updated to match — the earlier `MyRounds` panel dropped, `Caddie` panel added.

**Caddie** is a new, real, per-user, private AI swing-analysis history — `public.caddie_analyses`, RLS strictly `owner_id = auth.uid()` on every operation (no other role can ever select a row, live-verified via SQL role-simulation: a second real account got exactly 0 rows querying another user's analysis). No real CV/AI provider exists (`src/lib/swingAnalysis.ts`'s `swingAnalysisProvider` still always throws) — every analysis, direct-upload or Community-sourced, is created with real, persisted metadata (real video URL, real timestamps, real ownership) but `status` stays `'pending'` forever until a real provider is connected; the UI shows an honest "Caddie is taking a look..." state, never a fabricated result, matching the same discipline already established for Swing Posts.

- `supabase/migrations/20260817090000_create_caddie_analyses.sql` — the table + RLS.
- `supabase/migrations/20260817091500_enforce_caddie_ask_owner_only.sql` — "Ask Caddie" is owner-only for this beta (explicit product decision, deferred the open question of analyzing a stranger's swing) — **DB-enforced, not just hidden in the UI**: a trigger rejects any `community_post`-sourced analysis whose `owner_id` doesn't match that post's real author. Live-verified: a second real account's attempt to bypass the UI gate and directly insert an analysis against the first account's post was rejected with the intended error, zero rows leaked.
- `src/context/RealCaddieContext.tsx` (new) — same shape as the other `RealXContext`s: owner-scoped fetch + realtime subscription + `createAnalysis()`, which fires the (currently always-failing) real provider call and leaves the row honestly `pending` on the expected throw.
- `src/data/caddie.ts` (new) — demo mode's own illustrative (openly fictional, same posture as every other demo fixture) 2-analysis mock history, completely separate from real accounts' data.
- `src/pages/Caddie.tsx`, `AnalyzeSwing.tsx`, `CaddieAnalysisDetail.tsx` (new) — home screen (Analyze a Swing CTA + Recent list, empty state), direct-upload flow (reuses the exact `community-media` Storage upload mechanics `CreatePost.tsx` already established for Swing Posts — no second media pipeline), and the result view (strengths/issues/recommendations, "From Community Post" breadcrumb when relevant, "Share to Community").
- **Ask Caddie in Community**: `PostCard.tsx` — swing posts the viewer owns show "Ask Caddie" (creates a `community_post`-sourced analysis referencing the post's existing video, no re-upload) or, once one exists, an inline "✨ Caddie Swing Analysis · View Analysis" row — satisfies the later persistence-brief's #6/#11 ask that a post's Caddie analysis stay visible on the post itself, not only inside Caddie's own history.
- **Share to Community**: `CaddieAnalysisDetail.tsx`'s "Share to Community" hands off to `CreatePost.tsx` via `navigate(..., { state })` with the real (already-uploaded) video URL + a caption — `CreatePost.tsx` was extended to accept this prefill and, critically, to **reuse that URL directly instead of re-uploading a second copy of the same file**. User must still review and tap Post; nothing is auto-published.
- `src/pages/Profile.tsx` — new "My Golf" row (first in the list) linking to `/my-rounds`; `src/pages/MyRounds.tsx` gained a back button since it's no longer a root tab.
- `src/pages/Home.tsx` — one optional, compact Caddie card (latest analysis only, shown only if one exists) between the preferences nudge and the Community section.
- All new UI text localized across all 6 languages from the start.

**A separate, later brief (2026-08-17) asked to make Community posts persistent** on a mistaken premise — Community has been fully real/Supabase-backed since Phase 7e (verified again this phase: real accounts' posts, comments, votes, and now Caddie references all survive refresh/restart/navigation because they were never local React state to begin with). Corrected before doing any redundant rebuild; the one genuinely new piece from that brief (Caddie analysis visible inline on the post) is the "Ask Caddie" row above, not a separate change.

**Verified this phase:** RLS privacy (cross-account read blocked), DB-enforced Ask-Caddie ownership (cross-account bypass attempt rejected), direct-upload and Community-sourced analysis creation, `status` defaulting honestly to `'pending'` — all via SQL role-simulation with two real accounts, zero test-data footprint left behind (confirmed by count after). `npx tsc -b`, `npm run lint`, `npm run build` all clean throughout every step, not just at the end. Security advisors clean (no unexpected findings on the new table or trigger function).

**Not verified live:** an actual browser click-through of the nav swipe/tap behavior, the direct-upload flow, or the Community Ask-Caddie button — this phase's verification is compile-clean + DB-level SQL simulation only, no live Playwright pass was run.

Nothing in this phase has been committed or pushed yet — awaiting explicit go-ahead, per standing project rule.

## Phase 10 — notification popups, chat clear/delete, composer bug fix, Caddie nav icon (2026-08-17)

No cAddIe rebrand (explicitly dropped mid-brief — "Caddie" stays as-is). Four independent pieces:

**In-app notification popups.** `src/components/notifications/NotificationPopupHost.tsx` (new), mounted once in `AppShell.tsx` so it's present on every authed route. Deliberately driven by `useData().notifications` (already branched demo/real, already kept live by `RealSocialContext`'s existing realtime subscription) rather than a second dedicated Supabase channel — diffing that array for ids not seen before correctly catches only genuinely new rows regardless of whether the underlying refetch was triggered by an INSERT or an unrelated UPDATE (e.g. mark-as-read), and this get demo-mode parity for free since demo notifications flow through the same field. Popups only for `new_message`/`round_joined`/`round_cancelled`/`post_reply`/`comment_reply` (skipped `round_left`/`round_created_from_post`/`post_became_round` and the "round matching your preferences" idea — the latter has no backing notification type at all and would need new backend logic, deferred per explicit instruction). Small sequential queue (oldest first), ~4.5s auto-dismiss, manual X, tap navigates via the existing router then dismisses. Dedup via a `Set` of already-shown ids in a ref, so reconnects/refetches never double-show the same one. Dismissing never touches the underlying notification row — Notification Center is unaffected.
**Known limitation carried forward, not introduced here**: notification `.text` is generated server-side (by the Phase 5/7 trigger functions) in English at row-creation time regardless of the recipient's locale — the popup surfaces whatever that text already is, same as the existing bell panel already did. Real per-locale notification text would need the triggers themselves reworked to store a template key + params instead of pre-rendered text; out of scope for this pass.

**Chat Clear History / Delete Conversation.** `supabase/migrations/20260817120000_add_chat_clear_and_delete.sql` — added `cleared_before`/`hidden_at` to `conversation_participants` (reused, not a new table — already the per-(conversation,user) row, already had `last_read_at`). "Clear Chat History" sets `cleared_before`; **enforced by rewriting the `messages_select_participant` RLS policy** to additionally require `created_at > caller's own cleared_before` — a real DB-level hide, not a client-side filter, verified via SQL role-simulation (Michael cleared, immediately saw 0 messages; mailinator's own view was untouched; mailinator then sent a new message and Michael correctly saw exactly that one new message, old ones still hidden). "Delete Conversation" sets `hidden_at`; enforced client-side in `dmConversations` (`RealSocialContext.tsx`) by excluding a conversation whose latest message is older than the viewer's own `hidden_at` — reappears automatically the moment a newer message exists, no explicit "undelete" needed. Cross-user RLS verified: mailinator's attempt to clear/hide Michael's own `conversation_participants` row affected 0 rows. Never a raw `delete from messages` — messages are always retained for the other participant. Demo mode gets a lightweight, non-persisted (resets on reload) local equivalent for feature-parity preview; the real, DB-enforced behavior above is what's actually tested.

**Community composer bug ("swing video disappears when switching tabs").** Root cause confirmed exactly as suspected: `CreatePost.tsx`'s `chooseAttachment()` used one piece of state for both "which picker tab is open" and "what's actually attached," so opening a different tab cleared the previous selection as a side effect. Fixed by splitting these: `activeTool` is now UI-only; `imageUrl`/`videoFile`/`videoPreviewUrl`/`courseTag`/`golfCallId` persist independently and are only ever cleared by an explicit Remove, or by attaching a genuinely different kind of content — which now prompts a "Replace attachment?" confirmation first rather than silently destroying the previous selection (community_posts.type is a single value, so true multi-attachment coexistence isn't schema-supported; the fix is about never losing content to incidental tab-switching, not adding multi-attachment).

**Caddie nav icon.** `src/components/icons/CaddieNavIcon.tsx` (new) — a hand-built outline SVG (rounded robot head, visor band with "AI" lettering, visor bill, two dot eyes), `currentColor`-based, dropped into `nav.ts`'s Caddie entry only. `NAV_ITEMS`' icon type was loosened from `typeof Home` (which only accepts lucide-react's exact component type) to a small structural type matching what BottomNav/SideNav actually pass (`size`/`strokeWidth`/`className`), so a hand-built component and lucide's icons can both satisfy it.

**Verified this phase:** RLS/reappearance behavior for chat clear/delete (SQL role-simulation, two real accounts, zero footprint left behind). `npx tsc -b`, `npm run lint`, `npm run build` clean after every step.
**Not verified live:** the notification popup's actual on-screen appearance/timing/tap behavior, the composer fix's click-through, and the nav icon's rendered appearance at real mobile sizes — this phase's verification is compile-clean + (for chat) DB-level SQL simulation only, no live Playwright/browser pass was run.

Nothing in this phase has been committed or pushed yet — awaiting explicit go-ahead, per standing project rule.
