## Phase 12 — Coach Reviewer access system (2026-08-18)

New feature, built server-first per the brief: a generic, admin-managed role system so Micky can grant selected users (starting with friends, later verified coaches/partners) the ability to leave structured "Coach Review" feedback on swing posts, without ever editing the database by hand.

- **`20260818040000_create_coach_reviewer_system` migration**: `public.user_roles` (generic `user_id`/`role`/`granted_by`/`granted_at`/`revoked_at`/`active`, unique per user+role — designed so future roles like Verified Coach only need a CHECK-constraint follow-up, not a schema change), `public.reviewer_invites` (unguessable `hex(24 random bytes)` token, RLS enabled with **zero** policies — completely unreachable via the REST API, every interaction goes through the RPCs below), `public.coach_reviews` (post_id/reviewer/author/strengths/improvements/suggested_drill/additional_comments, unique per reviewer+post, immutable — no update/delete policy, so a later revoke can never silently delete a reviewer's past feedback). `private.has_role()` helper for RLS. Bootstrap: the earliest existing account (`mickylyu@gmail.com`, looked up by email not a hardcoded id) was granted `admin` directly in the migration — every subsequent grant goes through the dashboard.
- **Every privileged mutation is a `SECURITY DEFINER` RPC that checks the caller's own role internally**, never a client-facing insert/update policy — same convention as `join_golf_call()`/`host_golf_call()`. `grant_coach_reviewer`/`revoke_coach_reviewer`/`admin_search_users`/`admin_list_coach_reviewers`/`create_reviewer_invite`/`admin_list_reviewer_invites`/`revoke_reviewer_invite` all raise `Not authorized.` for a non-admin caller (verified live via role-simulated SQL, not just code review — see below). `redeem_reviewer_invite` is the one RPC any authenticated user can call; its safety comes from an atomic `update ... where status = 'pending' and expires_at > now() returning *`, which under Postgres row-level locking lets only one concurrent caller ever win a given token (verified live: redeeming the same token twice correctly fails the second time). `20260818041500_add_is_coach_reviewer_check` adds `is_coach_reviewer(user_id)`, open to any authenticated caller (public profile fact, same visibility as `verified_golfer`) for the profile badge.
- **Live-verified end-to-end via role-simulated SQL** (`set local role authenticated; set local request.jwt.claims = ...`), not just code review: admin search/list RPCs succeed for the admin and are rejected for a non-admin; an invite was created, redeemed by a real (non-admin) account, confirmed via `my_active_roles()`, re-redemption of the same token correctly failed; that account then successfully inserted a `coach_reviews` row on the one real swing post, while a non-reviewer account's insert attempt was correctly rejected by RLS; confirmed `reviewer_invites` returns zero rows via a direct `select` even for the admin, proving it's only reachable through the RPCs. All test rows cleaned up afterward — production data left exactly as it was before testing except for the schema itself.
- **`src/pages/AdminReviewers.tsx`** (`/admin/coach-reviewers`, not linked from nav, gated on `useRoles().isAdmin` client-side and re-verified by every RPC call server-side) — search any user (name/username/email), grant/remove Coach Reviewer, see active + disabled reviewers with granted-by/granted-at, create/copy/revoke invite links, invite status list (Pending/Redeemed/Expired/Revoked). Reachable by a real admin via a hidden row on their own Profile page (`isAdmin`-gated). **Deliberately plain English, not routed through the i18n system** — this page is reachable by nobody but admins, unlike every other page in the app; scoped down to avoid inventing machine translations across 6 locale files for an internal tool. Flagged to Micky, not silently decided.
- **`src/pages/CoachInvite.tsx`** (`/coach-invite/:token`, standalone route outside both `GuestOnly` and `AuthedLayout` for the same reason `/ready` is) — already-authed real accounts redeem immediately and see success/error right there; not-yet-authed visitors get the token stashed in `sessionStorage` and are sent to Log In/Create Account. `src/lib/usePendingReviewerInviteRedemption.ts` picks that stored token up the moment a real session exists (mounted in `AppGate`, fires even mid-onboarding — the role isn't gated on `has_onboarded`, so a brand-new signup's invite activates before they ever reach `/ready`). Demo mode shows a "log in with a real account" message; a real Supabase auth session is required, same reasoning as Find Friends.
- **`src/components/community/CoachReviewSection.tsx`**, embedded in `PostDetail.tsx` between the post card and the comment thread — renders only for `type: "swing"` posts, real accounts. Shows existing reviews (reviewer avatar/name/Coach Reviewer badge/date, via `formatRelativeTime`) kept visually and structurally separate from `community_comments`; "Leave Coach Review" button only for an active reviewer who hasn't already reviewed that post and isn't its own author, opening a 4-field form (what looks good / what can improve / suggested drill / additional notes) via the existing `Modal` component. New standalone `src/lib/useCoachReviews.ts` hook (not folded into `RealCommunityContext` — narrow, real-only, admin-gated feature, same reasoning `useFriendSearch`/`useRoles` stay outside `DataContext`).
- **"Coach Reviewer" badge** (`src/components/ui/Badge.tsx`, already existed) shown next to the credibility badge on both `Profile.tsx` (own profile, via `useRoles()`) and `GolferProfilePage.tsx` (viewing someone else's, via the new `src/lib/useIsCoachReviewer.ts` hook + `is_coach_reviewer()` RPC).
- **Not yet done**: live browser click-through (everything above was verified via `tsc`/`lint`/`build` clean plus direct role-simulated SQL, not an actual UI session — same tooling-limitation caveat as every icon/UI change this session, flagged rather than silently assumed working). The invite flow's "already logged in" path auto-redeems on landing rather than showing an explicit "Confirm" click the brief's wording implied — a deliberate simplification (clicking a personally-sent invite link already is the confirmation, and revoke exists as a safety net) rather than an oversight; easy to add a confirm step later if Micky wants it.

## Phase 13 — Public pre-TestFlight waitlist landing page (2026-08-18)

Replaces the old bare "Get Started" splash at `/welcome` with a full marketing landing page whose primary action is joining a waitlist, not self-serve signup — `/onboarding`/`/signup`/`/login` all still work unchanged for anyone who reaches them directly, this page just stops advertising self-serve signup as the front door. The authenticated app (Home/Play/cAddIe/Me, real Supabase auth) is completely untouched.

- **`20260818050000_create_waitlist_signups` migration**: `public.waitlist_signups` (email/normalized_email/home_region/referral_source/status, unique on `normalized_email`), the **first anon-writable table in this project** — every other table requires a real `authenticated` session, but a marketing-page visitor has neither an account nor a reason to create one (waitlist signup and GolfMe auth stay fully separate, never auto-creates an account). `normalized_email` and `status` are computed/reset server-side by a `BEFORE INSERT` trigger, never trusted from the client. **Zero select/update/delete policy for anon or authenticated, on purpose** — nobody, including the person who just signed up, can read the table back through the API; reading it (region concentration counts, moving someone `waiting → invited → beta`) is a founder-only operation via the Supabase dashboard/SQL, not exposed through the app.
- **Real bug caught and fixed via live testing, not just code review**: Postgres RLS enforces a table's SELECT policy against an `INSERT ... RETURNING`'s output, not just plain `INSERT` — with zero SELECT policy (by design, see above), requesting the inserted row back made even a *successful* insert fail with a misleading "violates row-level security policy" error. Confirmed via a real anon-key REST call (not just SQL role-simulation) before writing any frontend code. Fix: `useWaitlistSignup.ts` never chains `.select()` on the insert — success is just "no error was thrown," which is all the UI needs and keeps the privacy design intact.
- **Duplicate prevention**: relies entirely on the `normalized_email` unique constraint, not a read-then-write check (which the zero-read-access design wouldn't even allow) — a duplicate insert fails with Postgres `23505`, which the client catches and turns into "You're already on the GolfMe waitlist." Verified live: same email in a different case → correctly caught as a duplicate.
- **`src/pages/Welcome.tsx`** rebuilt as the full landing page (hero → product explanation/3 pillars → How It Works → waitlist form → bottom CTA → footer), using the brief's exact copy, with new `src/components/landing/LandingNav.tsx` (minimal public nav: logo, How It Works, cAddIe, Log In, Join Waitlist — never the authenticated app's Home/Play/cAddIe/Me nav) and `src/components/landing/WaitlistForm.tsx` (email + a founder-curated home-region picker — New York/Long Island, Boston, New Jersey, Los Angeles, Miami, Other — deliberately distinct from `src/data/regions.ts`'s generic city list built for the authenticated app's course search, which doesn't even contain most of these areas). `?ref=` query param captured silently into `referral_source` (section 20's lightweight source tracking — no UI, no referral system). All new copy added as proper `landing.*` i18n keys across all 6 locales (this is the public-facing entry point every prospective visitor sees, unlike the Coach Reviewer admin dashboard, which stayed English-only as an internal tool).
- **No Privacy Policy page exists in this app** — checked before building (`grep` across `src/`, only a Settings section *labeled* "Privacy" for block-list management, no legal document/route) — per the brief's own instruction, no policy link was added and no legal claims were invented; flagged to Micky rather than fabricated.
- **Live-tested end-to-end with a real headless Chromium via `playwright-core`** (already a project dependency; a cached browser binary was already present locally) against the Vite dev server and the real Supabase project — not just `tsc`/`lint`/`build`, an actual click-through: 320/375/390/430px + 1440px desktop (no horizontal overflow, hero + CTA visible, zero console errors), a brand-new email + region → real row created, refresh after signup → no broken state, same email in a different case → correctly rejected as a duplicate, a second distinct email + different region → separate signup succeeds, an invalid email → blocked by native `type="email"` validation before ever reaching the network, "Already have access? Log In" → correctly navigates to the existing real `/login`. **Caught one real bug this way**: at exactly 320px the nav's "Join the GolfMe Waitlist" button (correct on the hero/bottom CTA per the brief's own copy) was too wide next to the logo + Log In and overflowed the viewport by 20px — fixed by giving the nav its own shorter `landing.nav.joinWaitlist` ("Join Waitlist") key, which is also what section 17's own nav mockup literally specifies, re-verified clean after. All test rows deleted from `waitlist_signups` afterward.

## Phase 14 — Tee-time trust: booking proof attachment (2026-08-18)

Three trust levels for a round's tee time: `manual` (host typed it in, unchanged default), `user_verified` (host attached supporting proof — this phase), `provider_verified` (reserved, nothing sets it, no fake verification implemented). GolfMe never claims to have independently confirmed a booking — every surface says "the host provided proof," not "verified."

- **`20260818070000_add_tee_time_verification` migration**: widened `golf_calls.tee_time_source`'s CHECK constraint (was locked to the single value `'user_entered'`, since renamed `'manual'`) rather than adding a parallel status column — it already existed for exactly this purpose. Added public-safe `golf_calls.booking_source`/`verification_created_at` (same open-select RLS as the rest of the row, meant to be visible to anyone). New `public.booking_proofs` table holds the sensitive bits (`storage_path`, `booking_reference`) with its **own** narrow RLS (host or admin only) — deliberately not columns on `golf_calls`, since that table's blanket "any authenticated user can select" policy would otherwise leak a booking confirmation number to every GolfMe user regardless of what the UI renders. Three new host-only SECURITY DEFINER RPCs (`attach_booking_proof`, `remove_booking_proof`, `edit_golf_call_tee_time` — the last one is also the **first** post-creation round edit capability this project has ever had, previously only cancel/complete existed, both single-column status flips). A new private `booking-proofs` Storage bucket (unlike avatars/community-media, not public-read — a booking screenshot is more sensitive than a profile photo). A trigger notifies already-joined participants once, the moment proof is attached to a round they're in.
- **Editing course/date/time on an already-verified round auto-invalidates the proof** in the same transaction (deletes the `booking_proofs` row, reverts `tee_time_source` to `manual`) — never lets proof from one booking silently "verify" a different one. Verified live via role-simulated SQL, not just code review.
- **Live-verified end-to-end via role-simulated SQL and a real notification check**: a non-host's `attach_booking_proof` call correctly rejected; the host's own call correctly flips the round to `user_verified`; a non-host's direct `select` on `booking_proofs` (where the sensitive reference number lives) returns zero rows while the host's own select correctly sees it; editing the tee time correctly deleted the proof row and reverted the status; attaching proof correctly fired exactly one `booking_proof_attached` notification to the one other joined participant, with the right text and link. All test rows cleaned up afterward.
- **Frontend**: `src/components/golfcall/TeeTimeTrustBadge.tsx` (small "✓ Booking Proof Attached" badge — renders nothing at all for `manual`, per the brief's "simply avoid a verification badge" instruction), `BookingProofExplainer.tsx` (tap-to-expand public explanation, includes "GolfMe has not independently confirmed live course availability"), `BookingProofHostControls.tsx` (host-only add/replace/remove, file+PDF upload with a sensitive-info warning, booking source/reference fields), `EditTeeTimeModal.tsx` (host-only, scoped to just course/date/time, warns up front if proof will be invalidated). Wired into `GolfCallCard.tsx` (subtle badge only, cards stay uncluttered for `manual`), `GolfCallDetail.tsx` (badge/explainer replaces the existing host-entered disclaimer only when verified; host-only edit/proof controls; a "Report Round" entry via the *already-existing* `ReportModal`/`reports.context = 'round'` plumbing from Phase 4/5, which had the DB/type support built but was never actually wired to any UI button until now), and `CreateGolfCall.tsx`'s step 2 (optional, collapsed-by-default "Verify Your Tee Time" section — uploads after the round itself is created, since Storage needs a real `golf_call_id`; a failed proof upload never blocks or fails the hosting action itself). `GolfCalls.tsx`'s compatibility-sorted (`?matched=1`) view gives verified rounds a small (+2 of ~100) score nudge — enough to break a near-tie, never enough to override a real compatibility difference; the plain date-sorted default view is untouched. Auto-Match/round-card/detail all show "✓ Booking proof attached" as an *additional* line after the real compatibility reasons, never competing for one of their slots or counted as a personal-preference match.
- **i18n**: ~36 new keys (badge/explainer/host-controls/edit-modal/report copy) across all 6 locales — this is regular in-app UI, not an admin-only tool, so it follows the same full-localization convention as the rest of the product.
- **Tested live via a real headless Chromium** (demo-mode click-through, since completing real magic-link auth isn't possible from this session — same constraint noted for every prior phase's "real two-account" tests): demo login, Play list, and Host a Round wizard all render with zero console errors; confirmed the new host-only UI (Edit Tee Time, Add Booking Proof, Verify Your Tee Time) correctly renders nowhere in demo mode, and the pre-existing demo hosting flow still works unchanged — no regression. **Not yet done**: an actual two-real-account click-through of the host/proof-upload/badge-viewing flow end to end (blocked by the same real-auth-session limitation as every previous phase's live UI tests) — backend correctness for that exact flow was instead verified via role-simulated SQL, per the section above.
- One pre-existing test round (Hong Kong Golf Club, hosted by the founder account) had its `tee_time_label` changed to "11:40 AM" as a side effect of live-testing the edit RPC — cosmetic only, flagged to Micky rather than silently left.

## Phase 15 — Admin platform dashboard: waitlist + registered users (2026-08-18)

Directly answers the two `0a`/`0b` "no admin UI yet" gaps flagged at the end of Phases 13/14 — a hidden `/admin/dashboard` page (same access model as `/admin/coach-reviewers`: not in nav, gated on `isAdmin` client-side, re-verified server-side) showing:
- **Waitlist**: total count, a status breakdown (waiting/invited/beta/declined), a region-count table (the thing Phase 13 specifically structured `home_region` cleanly to support), and the full recent-signups list (email/region/referral/status/date).
- **Registered users**: total + onboarded counts, and a roster (avatar/name/username/email/member-since, with admin/coach-reviewer/mid-onboarding badges).

Two new admin-gated RPCs (`20260818090000_add_admin_dashboard_rpcs` migration): `admin_list_waitlist_signups()` (waitlist_signups has zero read policy for anyone at all by design — this is now the one sanctioned way to read it, still admin-only, not opened up broadly) and `admin_list_users()` (joins `profiles` to `auth.users` for email, same pattern as `admin_search_users()`). Verified live via role-simulated SQL: non-admin correctly rejected, admin call returns the expected rows. Reachable via a new "Platform Dashboard" row next to "Coach Reviewers" on the founder's own Profile page. Regression-tested via a real headless browser in demo mode — link correctly absent, zero console errors. English-only, same reasoning as the Coach Reviewer dashboard (internal tool, not user-facing).

## Phase 16 — Fix Match Preferences save race + add a confirm signal (2026-08-18)

Bug report: the Home page's "Complete your profile — Update Preferences" reminder (`src/pages/Home.tsx`, driven by `selectedPreferenceCount(currentUser) < 3` in `src/lib/preferenceMatch.ts`) sometimes wouldn't disappear even after the user had genuinely set 3+ preferences, and there was no confirmation that a tap on a preference pill had actually saved.

- **Root cause (real accounts only)**: `updateCurrentUserProfile` (`src/context/DataContext.tsx`) fired an independent `UPDATE → re-SELECT` round trip per field change with nothing sequencing them. Every individual `UPDATE` always landed correctly in Postgres, but the re-SELECT *responses* could resolve out of order under normal network jitter — whichever one's `setProfileRow` call happened to land LAST won, which isn't guaranteed to be the one for the most-recently-clicked pill. Result: the in-memory `currentUser` (and anything computed from it, like the Home reminder) could regress to a state missing one of several rapid changes, even though the database was already fully correct. Demo mode was never affected — its save path is a synchronous local merge with no network round trip to race.
- **Fix**: `updateCurrentUserProfile` now serializes real-account saves through a ref-held promise queue (`profileSaveQueueRef`) — each save's full round trip finishes before the next one starts, so completion order always matches initiation order. It also now returns a `Promise<void>` (previously fire-and-forget/`void`) so a caller that wants to know when a save actually lands can await it; existing callers that don't award it are unaffected (a default `.catch()` still logs failures, same as before, so nothing became noisier).
- **Confirmation signal, v2 — superseded the first attempt**: an initial fix kept live per-field auto-save and added a debounced "Preferences saved." toast, but explicit follow-up feedback asked for something more deliberate: no auto-save at all, a single bottom Save button, everything committed in one bundle. `MatchPreferencesDetail.tsx` was rewritten around local draft state (`draftPrefs`/`draftAvailability`/`draftArea`, covering the pill grid, availability slots, and playing-area location together) that's diffed against `currentUser` to drive a sticky bottom "Save changes" button — disabled until something actually changed, calls `updateCurrentUserProfile()` exactly once with the full merged patch, shows the save/error toast, then re-disables. Mirrors the draft-then-commit pattern `FindRoundModal.tsx` already used for its own (temporary, per-search) preference override — just persisted here instead of thrown away. Known trade-off, not yet handled: navigating away (the back button) with unsaved changes discards them silently, no confirm-dialog guard.
- Regression-tested via a real headless browser in demo mode: Save button starts disabled, enables the instant a pill changes, no toast/network activity before it's tapped, tapping it saves + shows the confirmation toast + disables again, zero console errors. The underlying real-account save-ordering race (still fixed via the serialized queue above) can't be demonstrated in demo mode itself — confidence there comes from code/Promise-semantics review, not a live repro.
- **Follow-up**: `handleSave` now navigates to `/` (Home) only after `updateCurrentUserProfile` resolves, per explicit request. This also likely explains the "reminder doesn't show as completed" complaint on its own — real-account saves are a genuine network round trip, and if the user checked Home before that finished (easy to do without an explicit save button forcing a wait), they'd see `currentUser` before it had actually updated. Waiting for the await before navigating means Home always renders with the just-saved data. Verified live: URL correctly lands on `/` after Save, zero console errors.

## Phase 17 — Email the founder on every waitlist signup (2026-08-18)

GolfMe had zero outbound-email capability before this (the app only ever sent Supabase Auth's own magic-link login emails, a separate system that can't be repurposed). Micky signed up for Resend and provided an API key over Telegram.

- **`20260818110000_add_waitlist_signup_email_notification` migration**: enables `pg_net` (async HTTP from Postgres — new to this project) and adds an `AFTER INSERT` trigger on `waitlist_signups` that calls Resend's API directly via `net.http_post`, emailing `mickylyu@gmail.com` the new signup's email/region/referral source. **The actual API key is deliberately NOT in this migration file** — same reasoning `.env.local` is gitignored, a real secret must never land in a tracked file. It was inserted directly into Supabase Vault via a one-off `execute_sql` call outside of any migration (`vault.create_secret(...)`), referenced by the trigger only by name (`vault.decrypted_secrets where name = 'resend_api_key'`), readable only by the trigger's own `SECURITY DEFINER` context. If the vault secret is ever missing, the trigger no-ops silently rather than blocking or failing the actual signup.
- **Chose this over adding a new Edge Function** specifically because there's no way for this session to set an Edge Function secret (that's a dashboard/CLI-only step — same limitation documented below for `GEOAPIFY_API_KEY`, still unresolved). A Vault secret, by contrast, is settable via SQL, so this path could be finished completely today without requiring Micky to do anything else manually.
- **Verified live with a real send, not a mock**: inserted a real test row through the actual REST API (anon key, exactly how the frontend does it), confirmed via `net._http_response` that the HTTP call to Resend returned `200` with a real email id — the notification email genuinely sent. Test signup deleted afterward.
- **Known, unfixable advisor warning**: `pg_net` installs its extension registration into the `public` schema and doesn't support `ALTER EXTENSION ... SET SCHEMA` (confirmed by trying) — a common, low-severity (`WARN`, not `ERROR`) finding for this specific extension on Supabase; its actual callable functions already live properly isolated in the `net` schema regardless.
- Uses Resend's shared `onboarding@resend.dev` sending address (works immediately, no domain verification needed) — fine for founder-only notification emails; would need a verified custom domain before sending anything to end users.

## Phase 18 — Fix dead Comment button, replace Upvote with Like (2026-08-18)

Small, targeted Community fix — no schema/migration changes at all (voting/comments already had a real Supabase backend; this phase touched only the frontend).

- **Root cause of the "Comment button doesn't work" bug**: `PostCard.tsx`'s Comment button called `goToDetail()`, which is a no-op whenever `linkToDetail` is `false` — exactly the prop `PostDetail.tsx` passes when rendering a post's own detail page (so the card's own tap-anywhere handler doesn't re-navigate to itself). Result: tapping "Comment" while already on a post's detail view — where the full comment thread and composer are already on-screen below it — did literally nothing. On the feed (`linkToDetail` defaults `true`) it technically worked (navigated to detail), just redundantly with tapping the card itself.
- **Fix**: new `goToComments()` replaces the Comment button's handler — on the feed it still navigates to detail; on the detail page itself it now scrolls to and focuses the existing comment composer (`document.getElementById("comment-composer-input")`) instead of doing nothing.
- **Upvote → Like**: swapped the arrow-up icon for `lucide-react`'s `ThumbsUp` (outline when unliked, `fill="currentColor"` when liked) in `PostCard.tsx`'s action bar, added a subtle `active:scale-95` press response, added `aria-label`s. **Internal naming intentionally untouched** — `community_post_votes` table, `isPostUpvoted`/`postUpvoteCount`/`togglePostUpvote`/`PostVote` all keep their existing names, per explicit instruction to avoid unnecessary migration/rename risk; only user-facing text and the icon changed. Comment-level upvoting (`CommentItem.tsx`'s own arrow-up button) was deliberately left alone — out of the brief's stated scope (post-level "Community post action bar" specifically), flagged rather than silently expanded.
- New `community.*` i18n keys (like/liked/likes/comment/comments/writeCommentPlaceholder/postComment/noCommentsYet/beFirstToComment) added across all 6 locales; `PostDetail.tsx`'s previously-hardcoded "Add a comment..."/"Send"/"No comments yet — be the first." now route through these. Pre-existing hardcoded English elsewhere in `PostCard.tsx`/`CommentItem.tsx` (menu items, toasts, etc.) was left as-is — not introduced by this change, out of scope for a targeted fix.
- **Live-tested via a real headless browser in demo mode**: confirmed the Like button's toggle is a true, symmetric on/off (count +1/-1, `aria-pressed` flips correctly, returns to the exact original count after two clicks); confirmed the Comment button on the detail page now genuinely focuses the composer (previously a dead click); posted a real comment and confirmed the visible comment count updates and the new comment text renders; checked 320/375/390/430px for horizontal overflow on the Community feed — none. Zero console errors throughout.
- **Not independently re-verified this phase** (pre-existing, unchanged): comment persistence/realtime/notification-on-comment (`RealCommunityContext.tsx`'s realtime subscription + `notify_community_comment()` trigger, both already live since Phase 7e) and vote persistence/uniqueness (`community_post_votes`' composite primary key `(post_id, voter_id)` already enforces one vote per user per post at the database level, unchanged by this phase) — confirmed correct by reading the existing code/schema, not by a fresh live two-real-account test this round (blocked by the same real-magic-link-auth limitation as every other phase).

## Phase 19 — Real video thumbnails for Swing Posts (2026-08-19)

Bug report (with screenshot): Swing Post videos in Community rendered as a plain black rectangle until tapped, instead of showing a preview frame like Instagram Reels — a `<video>` with no `poster` just shows nothing.

- **`20260819050000_add_video_thumbnail_url` migration**: adds nullable `community_posts.video_thumbnail_url`. No RLS change needed (the table's existing open-select/insert-own policies already cover every column generically). Existing posts simply have no thumbnail until re-posted — not a backfill target, no fabricated thumbnail was worth inventing for them.
- **`src/lib/image.ts`'s new `captureVideoThumbnail()`**: loads the actual just-picked video file into an off-DOM `<video>`, seeks a touch past frame 0 (some encoders leave frame 0 black — same idea as Instagram's own auto-thumbnail), draws that real frame to a canvas, and resolves a JPEG blob. Never a placeholder/fake image — always a genuine frame from the golfer's own video.
- **`CreatePost.tsx`**: after a direct video upload (the `kind === "swing" && videoFile` branch — real accounts only, matching Swing Posts' existing no-demo-equivalent rule) succeeds, best-effort captures and uploads this thumbnail to the same `community-media` bucket and passes its URL through to `createPost()`. A thumbnail-capture failure (e.g. an unsupported codec) is caught and logged but never blocks publishing — the video itself already uploaded successfully by that point.
- **`PostCard.tsx`**: the post-card `<video>` now sets `poster={post.videoThumbnailUrl}` and `preload="metadata"`.
- **Threaded through the existing types**: `CommunityPost.videoThumbnailUrl`, `RealCommunityContext`'s `PostRow`/`CreateRealPostInput`/`rowToPost`, and `DataContext`'s `CreatePostInput` (present but inert on the demo path, matching `videoUrl`'s own existing pattern).
- **Extended same-day to also cover Caddie's "Share to Community" handoff** (that path reuses an already-uploaded video by reference — `prefilledVideoUrl` — rather than re-uploading, so a thumbnail generated at share time would mean fetching the remote video back down first). Instead, `caddie_analyses` now carries its own `thumbnail_url` (`20260819053000_add_caddie_analysis_thumbnail_url` migration), populated at the two real upload points: `AnalyzeSwing.tsx` captures a real frame the same way `CreatePost.tsx` does (direct-upload path), and `PostCard.tsx`'s "Ask Caddie" copies `post.videoThumbnailUrl` straight across when the analysis originates from an existing Community post. `CaddieAnalysisDetail.tsx`'s `shareToCommunity()` now carries `analysis.thumbnailUrl` forward in the router-state handoff into `CreatePost.tsx`'s `CreatePostSwingPrefill`, so posts made this way get a real poster frame too instead of falling back to black. `CaddieAnalysisDetail.tsx`'s own video preview also now sets `poster={analysis.thumbnailUrl}` for the same reason. Threaded through `RealCaddieContext`'s row/mapper/create-input and `DataContext`'s demo-mode branch.
- **Testing**: `tsc`/`lint`/`build` clean for both the original fix and this same-day extension. Attempted a real isolated test of the capture technique (record a real video in-browser via `MediaRecorder`, feed it through the exact same seek-and-capture logic, verify the output is a real non-black image) — `MediaRecorder` itself worked fine in this headless environment, but the headless Chromium build installed here couldn't play the resulting video back for capture (a known limitation of headless builds' video decode path, not something that reflects real Chrome/Safari/mobile browsers, which handle this exact video-thumbnail pattern reliably and are what real users are on). Confidence here comes from that plus code review of a well-established, widely-used technique, not a completed live repro — flagged rather than claimed as verified.
- **Follow-up same-day request: Reels-style autoplay** — "the video should start playing by itself like on Instagram but remains muted until user clicks into the video." `PostCard.tsx`'s swing-post `<video>` dropped `controls` and gained a per-card `IntersectionObserver` (threshold 0.6) that calls `.play()`/`.pause()` as the card scrolls in/out of view, `muted`/`loop`/`playsInline`, a tap-to-toggle-mute handler (stops propagation so it doesn't also navigate to the post), and a small speaker-icon overlay showing the current mute state. Starts muted, matching browser autoplay policy (unmuted autoplay is blocked everywhere) and the brief's own wording. Applies everywhere `PostCard` renders (Community feed, Home, My Posts, Saved Posts, Post Detail) since they all share this one component. **Not live-tested in-browser**: demo mode has no seeded Swing Post with a real video to click through, and (as noted above) this headless Chromium build can't play back video at all — verified via `tsc`/`lint`/`build` and code review only; real-device testing still recommended before treating this as fully confirmed.

## Phase 20 — Chat as a main nav tab, messaging reliability fixes, booking/round notifications (2026-08-19)

Large brief: Home greeting wording/casing, Chat promoted to a first-class centered nav destination (Home/Play/Chat/Caddie/Me), the old Profile-page Messages row removed, two real messaging-reliability bugs found and fixed, the DM inbox rebuilt as the Chat tab, the in-app popup suppressed when already viewing that exact conversation, and two new notification types for tee-time changes / booking-proof removal wired into the existing popup system.

- **Greeting**: `greeting.morning/afternoon/evening` in `en.ts` changed from sentence-case ("Good morning") to the requested title-case ("Good Morning"). Also fixed a real boundary bug in `src/lib/greeting.ts`'s `greetingKeyForHour()`: the old `hour < 12` / `hour < 18` / else logic meant hours 0–4 (just after midnight) fell into "morning" instead of "evening" — fixed to `hour >= 5 && hour < 12` / `hour >= 12 && hour < 18` / else, matching the brief's explicit 05:00–11:59/12:00–17:59/18:00–04:59 ranges. Verified live via a clock-mocked Playwright run at 2 AM.
- **Nav**: `src/lib/nav.ts`'s single `NAV_ITEMS` array (already the one source both `BottomNav.tsx` and `SideNav.tsx` read from) gained a `nav.chat` entry pointing at `/messages`, centered between Play and Caddie. A second, independently-hardcoded panel list in `RootTabCarousel.tsx` (needed for the swipe gesture, since it needs real page components, not just icons/labels) was updated in lockstep to keep swipe order matching the nav order. `AppShell.tsx`'s root-tab detection derives from the same nav array, so `/messages` automatically became a swipeable root tab while `/messages/:id` (an individual thread) automatically did not — no separate accounting needed there. Unread dot (matching the existing Bell-icon pattern exactly, not a numeric badge) added to both `BottomNav.tsx` and `SideNav.tsx`'s Chat item via `hasUnreadMessages`.
- **Removed the old entry point**: the "Messages" row on `Profile.tsx` (which duplicated the new Chat tab) is gone, along with its now-dead `profile.messages`/`profile.newMessages` translation keys across all 6 locales. `GolferProfilePage`'s "Message" button is untouched — still starts/opens a conversation via `/messages/:id`, exactly the "profile → start chat" vs "nav → see all conversations" split the brief asked for.
- **Diagnosed root causes of unreliable messaging** (both real, both fixed):
  1. `get_or_create_dm_conversation()` had a documented, accepted race (no locking, unlike `join_golf_call()`'s row-lock) — two people messaging each other for the first time at the same instant could each create a *separate* conversation row for the same pair, silently splitting their messages across two conversations. Fixed in `20260819060000_fix_dm_conversation_race.sql` with a transaction-scoped Postgres advisory lock keyed on the sorted pair of user ids (no pre-existing row to `for update` lock here — that's the whole race — so an advisory lock stands in for one). Verified live via SQL: two sequential calls for the same pair return the identical conversation id, and exactly one conversation row exists for that pair afterward.
  2. `RealSocialContext.tsx`'s `refetch()` was guarded by a `fetchingRef` flag that silently *dropped* any realtime event arriving while a previous refetch was still in flight, with no re-queue — a fast burst of messages could mean one just never loaded until something unrelated happened to trigger another fetch. Fixed with a `pendingRefetchRef` flag: a dropped-due-to-in-flight request now triggers exactly one more refetch right after the in-flight one finishes, so no event is lost.
- **Chat inbox**: `Inbox.tsx` rewritten as a root-tab page (no back-button chrome) titled "Chat" with a search-by-name filter over the existing `dmConversations` (which already correctly queries "every conversation the authenticated user is a participant in," not by already knowing the other golfer's id — that part was already right, just buried behind a Profile row). Real empty state (no demo chats) with a Find Friends CTA when there are zero conversations, a distinct "no results" state when a search matches nothing. Every string fully localized across 6 locales (new `chatInbox.*` keys), including the inline "You: " message preview prefix that was previously hardcoded English.
- **Popup suppression**: `NotificationPopupHost.tsx` now skips queuing a `new_message` popup when the user is already on `/messages/:actorId` for that exact sender — the message already appears live in the open thread via the realtime subscription, so a popup on top of it would be redundant. Everything else about the popup system (dedup by notification id, queue-not-stack, ~4.5s auto-dismiss, one reusable component for every notification type) already existed from Phase 5/14 and needed no changes — it was already built to the brief's spec.
- **New notification types**, `20260819070000_add_tee_time_change_notifications.sql`: `tee_time_updated` (fires when `edit_golf_call_tee_time()` actually changes course/date/or tee time, guarded inside the trigger body against no-op updates) and `booking_proof_removed` (opposite direction of the existing `booking_proof_attached` trigger, on the same `tee_time_source` column — catches both an explicit `remove_booking_proof()` call and `edit_golf_call_tee_time()`'s own invalidate-on-change path, since both revert to `'manual'` the same way). Both follow the existing precedent of every other notification type in this table: plain hardcoded-English `text` generated inside the `SECURITY DEFINER` trigger function, not a new client-side localization scheme for just these two — a deliberate consistency call, not an oversight (see below). Added to `NotificationType`, `NotificationsPanel.tsx`'s icon map (`Clock`/`ShieldOff`), and `NotificationPopupHost.tsx`'s `POPUP_TYPES`. Verified live via role-simulated SQL: a host attaching proof, then editing the course/date/tee-time (which both changes the details AND invalidates the now-stale proof), then an explicit removal, correctly produced `booking_proof_attached` → `tee_time_updated` + `booking_proof_removed` (two notifications from the one edit call, since it's genuinely two distinct facts) → nothing further from the redundant explicit removal (proof was already gone). Test round/notifications deleted afterward.
- **Host-side "proof added" confirmation** (brief's "Round confirmed" local feedback ask) already existed — `BookingProofHostControls.tsx` already shows `showToast(t("golfCallDetail.bookingProofAddedToast"))` ("Booking proof added.") after a successful attach. Left as-is rather than rebuilt into a richer multi-line card, since the existing plain-toast system already satisfies "local confirmation feedback, never implying provider verification" without a new component.
- **Deliberately left out of this pass** (flagged, not silently dropped): Golf Call *group* chat (inside a hosted round, separate from 1:1 DMs) still writes only to `localStorage` for real accounts — never touches Supabase, so it doesn't sync across devices/participants. Found during this investigation, confirmed out of scope with Micky (the brief was specifically about profile-to-profile DMs), left untouched. "Round becomes full" and future provider-verified-booking popups were listed by the brief as *reasonable* future triggers, not mandatory ones (no provider integration exists to fake), and weren't built.
- **Testing**: `tsc`/`lint`/`build` clean. Role-simulated SQL for both the conversation-race fix and the new triggers (above). Demo-mode Playwright: greeting wording + midnight-boundary fix, 5-item nav with Chat centered, Chat tab opens with no back-button chrome and a working search box, Profile page confirmed to no longer have a Messages row, no console errors across Play/Caddie/Community, no horizontal scroll and 5 nav items fit cleanly at 320/375/390/430px. **Not verified**: a true two-real-account click-through (send from A, receive live on B, refresh/logout/login persistence) — same standing limitation as every earlier phase, real magic-link auth can't be completed unattended from this session; the conversation-race and notification-trigger fixes were instead verified by direct role-simulated SQL, which is what actually exercises the fixed logic path.

## Phase 21 — Shorten new-account onboarding (2026-09-16)

Audit-first per the brief; full audit (steps, required-vs-DB-required fields, downstream fallback check) reported and approved before any code was touched — see the questions/answers below. src/-only, no schema/RLS/migration changes.

- **Old flow**: Auth → `ProfileSetup.tsx` 4-step wizard (You: photo/name/gender/18+ → Your Golf: handicap or skill band → Where You Play: location + optional favorite course → How You Play: round length/vibes/walk-cart/budget/travel radius) → `Ready.tsx` (Find a Round / Host a Round buttons) → Home. **New flow**: Auth → one Quick Profile screen (photo optional, name, 18+, location, handicap-or-"Not sure") → `Ready.tsx` adapted into the one intent question ("What are you looking for?", multi-select of Find people / Join a round / Host a round / Improve my swing, CTA "Start Golfing") → Home. 4 form steps + a 2-button ready screen collapsed to 1 form screen + 1 intent screen.
- **Fields moved out of mandatory onboarding** (still written with the same neutral defaults the old wizard's own pre-seeded state already shipped with — never a new fabricated value): gender ("Prefer not to say"), favorite course (none), round length preference ("No Preference"), vibes (["Casual & Social"]), walk/cart ("Either"), budget ($45–$75, i.e. the old $60 default's own ±15 band), travel radius (25mi). All confirmed genuinely editable afterward: round length/vibes/walk-cart/budget/travel-radius/preferred-courses via `MatchPreferencesDetail.tsx`, favorite courses via `Profile.tsx`'s edit modal (pre-existing). **Gender had no edit surface anywhere in the app** (Profile's edit modal only ever covered ageRange/handicap/favoriteCourses/bio/username) — found during the "confirm these remain editable" check, not assumed; added a gender field (pills + custom, same pattern as the old wizard) to `Profile.tsx`'s edit modal so removing it from onboarding doesn't strand it as permanently unset. Location and 18+ stayed mandatory gates (location because it's the one field that materially degrades "nearby" functionality if skipped; 18+ because it reads as a legal/App-Review gate, not a data field — it still isn't a DB column, same as before).
- **`age_range` fabrication removed**: found during the pre-implementation audit that both the demo and real signup paths hardcoded `age_range: "25-34"` even though onboarding never actually asked for it — exactly the "fabricate a value to satisfy a required field" anti-pattern. `GolferProfile.ageRange` is now `AgeRange | undefined` (`types/index.ts`); `lib/profile.ts`'s row mapper and placeholder profile no longer default to `"25-34"` (real `undefined` when unset); `golferPatchToProfileRow` now writes `?? null` instead of a bare passthrough for this field specifically, since a patch clearing it back to unset carries `undefined`, which `JSON.stringify` silently drops from the request body — without the `?? null` fix, "clear my age range" from Profile would have silently no-opped. Traced every consumer before touching anything: `autoMatch.ts`'s `backgroundBonus()` age bonus/penalty now filters the roster to golfers with a real `ageRange` first and skips the bonus entirely (neutral, not a fabricated band) when nobody in the roster has one set; `GolferCard.tsx` now conditionally renders the "· 25-34" suffix instead of always showing it; `Profile.tsx`'s age-range `<select>` gained a blank "Not set" option. `DataContext.tsx`'s `NewGolferInput.ageRange` is optional too. Confirmed via clean `tsc --noEmit` that every call site (including the mock-data seed files, which still populate a real ageRange for existing mock golfers) type-checks correctly with the field now optional.
- **Tutorial shortened in the same pass** (brief's conditional E — "if it can be done cleanly without increasing risk"): confirmed clean because the existing `TutorialProvider`/`TutorialOverlay` architecture is generic over `TUTORIAL_STEPS`' length, so this was a pure data change, zero component-logic edits. `lib/tutorialSteps.ts`'s `TUTORIAL_STEPS` trimmed from 6 (opening Home-welcome card, Play, Chat, Community, Caddie, closing "you're ready" card) to exactly 3 — Play, Chat, Caddie — reusing existing, already-written copy (`tutorial.play.*`/`tutorial.chat.*`/`tutorial.caddie.*`) that already closely matches the brief's own example text. No opening/closing screens anymore; it now starts by spotlighting Play immediately. Already short/dismissible/one-time before this change (fires after Home loads, not before; Skip always visible except on the true last step; auto-starts once per real account via `onboarding_tutorial_completed`) — this pass made it shorter, not architecturally different. One pre-existing, very minor cosmetic quirk left as-is (not touched, to keep this a zero-risk data-only change): the progress counter (`"N of TUTORIAL_LAST_INDEX"`) is hidden on both the first and last step by existing logic that assumed a separate non-counted closing screen, which no longer exists — with 3 steps this means only the middle step (Chat) shows a progress badge. Cosmetic only, not a functional bug.
- **Intent storage**: exactly what was asked — client-side only, `localStorage` under `golfme:onboardingIntent`, no new DB column, no new table. Selected on the adapted `Ready.tsx` screen, written once when "Start Golfing" is tapped. **Not wired into Home personalization in this pass** — the brief's "subtly use intent" section was explicitly marked optional ("if simple/safe") and instruction D was explicit that the fast path should ship first without the progressive-nudge/personalization layer. The data is captured and available in localStorage for a future pass to read; Home's own rendering logic was not touched, keeping this pass at zero risk to the explicitly-protected "DO NOT CHANGE" Home surface.
- **`onboardingDraft.ts`** simplified to match the single-screen form (`name`/`is18Plus`/`areaLabel`/`playingAreaCoords`/`hasHandicap`/`handicap` only — gender/customGender/favoriteCourse/skillBand/vibes/walkOrCart/budget/travelRadiusMiles/roundLength all removed, since there's no longer a UI step producing them). Confirmed safe beforehand: only consumed by `ProfileSetup.tsx` (rewritten together with it) and `Settings.tsx`'s dev-only "Reset Onboarding" (calls the no-arg `clearOnboardingDraft()`, no type dependency).
- **"Not sure" handicap**: kept as a real, honest "not sure" rather than reintroducing a fabricated skill tier. The old wizard's "I don't have a handicap" path made the user pick a Beginner/Intermediate/Advanced skill band, which silently wrote `skill_level: "Intermediate"` if left at its default. Since the brief's own field list says just "allow 'Not sure,'" the skill-band sub-picker was dropped entirely — picking "Not sure" now writes `handicap: null, skill_level: null`, which `lib/format.ts`'s `handicapLabel()`/`skillTierFromHandicap()` and `compatibility.ts`'s `skillScore()` already handle gracefully (same null-handling convention as everywhere else), confirmed before relying on it.
- **Existing-user / partial-onboarding safety**: unchanged, already robust (`has_onboarded` on the one never-re-created profiles row, `App.tsx`'s route guards) — re-verified, no changes needed.
- **Analytics**: no new vendor, no new event names added. `profile_basic_completed` still fires at the end of Quick Profile (unchanged call site). Intent completion now piggybacks on the existing `onboarding_completed` event (was defined but never actually fired anywhere before this), carrying the selected intents as its properties. "Home reached" has no separate event — it's implicit immediately after `onboarding_completed` since there's no more gating screen; flagging this as an honest gap rather than adding a new event type to `AnalyticsEvent` for it, since the brief said not to add machinery beyond what's minimally needed.
- **i18n**: new keys (`profileSetup.quickProfileTitle`/`quickProfileSubtitle`, `profile.ageRangeNotSet`, `ready.intentFindPeople`/`intentJoinRound`/`intentHostRound`/`intentImproveSwing`) added and translated across all 6 locales; `profileSetup.noHandicap`'s copy changed from "I don't have a handicap" to "Not sure" (same key, all 6 locales); `ready.title`/`ready.subtitle` repurposed in place (same keys, both were only ever consumed by `Ready.tsx`) from "You're ready to golf" / "What do you want to do?" to the intent question and its "pick as many as you like" subtitle. Orphaned keys from the removed wizard steps (gender/round-length/vibes/budget/travel-distance copy, the old 4 `stepXxx` labels, `startGolfing`... actually still reused) were deliberately left in place rather than swept out across 6 locale files — harmless, unreferenced, lower priority than the functional change.
- **BottomNav / chat keyboard architecture**: untouched, not referenced by any file in this pass.
- **Testing**: `tsc --noEmit`, `oxlint`, and `npm run build` all clean (oxlint's pre-existing warnings are unrelated fast-refresh/exhaustive-deps notices in files this pass didn't touch). Not tested: an actual physical-device/browser click-through of the new Quick Profile → intent → Home flow, photo-skip, location-denial, and the shortened tutorial's spotlight positioning — same standing limitation as every earlier phase (no live/interactive session available here). No native build or new TestFlight archive is required — every changed file is under `src/`, nothing under `ios/`.

## Phase 22 — Reputation V1, Caddie V2 reel architecture, trust/security fixes, Caddie crest + nav UX (2026-09-18 through 2026-09-21)

Reconstructed from `git log` and code review across several separate sessions, not one continuous build — that session's own commit messages are unusually detailed (each explains root cause/scope/what-was-verified), so this is a faithful summary rather than a guess, but it is not a live session narration the way Phases 1-21 mostly are. 31 commits total; grouped below by theme, chronological within each group. Every item is `src/`-only (or Edge-Function-only) unless noted — no `ios/` changes, so none of this needed a new TestFlight archive on its own.

- **GolfMe Reputation V1** (`975cfec`, 2026-09-18): replaces "Building Credibility" with a 13-tier progression (Newcomer/Established/Trusted/Premier/Elite × I/II/III) computed live by a new `get_reputation_state()` Postgres function — no stored tier column, can't go stale or be client-set. Signals: qualifying completed rounds (host + ≥1 other real joined participant) + whether hosted, tenure as a gate only (never points by itself). Anti-farming: 2-person rounds capped at 3 credited completions with the same repeat partner, verified against hand-calculated expected output in a rolled-back transaction test. Excludes review-percentage/no-show (no real data exists for either). Migrations: `20260918170000_add_reputation_tier_system`, `20260918170100_fix_reputation_tier_ambiguous_column`. A follow-up same-day commit (`d121726` era) fixed an ambiguous-column bug in the function. `reputationTiers.ts` is presentation-only + a demo-mode formula mirror; `useReputationState.ts` is the real hook. `CompareModal` deliberately left on the old credibility system (feeds match-reasons, out of scope).
- **Reputation crests + navigation/pill UX + Caddie loading scenes + BottomNav ring fix** (commits `014d533`/`9bee980`/`d38be0d`/`5112848`, 2026-09-20/21, this session): `ReputationEmblem.tsx` (new) replaced the generic Lucide icons with custom SVG shield+flag+dimple crests across all 4 render surfaces (`ReputationBadge.tsx` wraps it, so no other call site changed); fixed a real bug where `useReputationState` fell back to an inaccurate client-side tier guess on every fresh mount (visible as a wrong-tier flash on Me → Reputation → Back → Me) via a session-lifetime cache, and made the profile Reputation pill itself tappable. Separately, `CaddieLoadingScenes.tsx` (new) replaced the single held practice-swing icon with 4 rotating decorative golfer scenes on the Caddie processing screen (tee-up/practice-swing/putt-line-up/putt), fully independent of real analysis state. Separately, fixed the Caddie "analyzing" ring in BottomNav getting clipped when the floating nav collapses — root cause was the icon-wrapper box never being sized for the ring, only the bare icon; fixed via one shared `caddieRingSize()` formula so the two can't drift apart again. All four committed, pushed, and deployed live to `golfme.app` this session — see "Vercel deployment status" below.
- **Verification badges: real signals only, self-grant hole closed** (`1d56b9e`, 2026-09-19): audit found `profiles.phone_verified`/`email_verified`/`verified_golfer` were self-settable by any signed-in user via the normal update RLS policy — a real, exploitable hole, not just fake UI. Migration `20260919030000_freeze_unverified_trust_columns` adds a `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger freezing all three against every client update (verified via rolled-back test transaction). Email verification is now real (reads `auth.users.email_confirmed_at` directly, plus a real working resend-confirmation action). Phone verification honestly shows "Coming soon" (no phone number is even collected yet, no SMS provider chosen). Demo mode's fake toggle flow unchanged.
- **Post-round review reworked** (`4127321`, 2026-09-19): conditional flow — secondary questions (on-time, handicap accuracy) only asked when showed-up=true; a no-show gets an optional "What happened?" folded into the private note as a bracketed tag instead. No Yes/No question pre-selected anymore. Migration `20260919210000_nullable_review_secondary_fields` makes `round_reviews.on_time`/`.pace` nullable (NULL = not asked, never fabricated) and adds a 500-char private-note constraint. `get_credibility_stats()`'s `on_time_pct` now divides by count(on_time is not null), not total reviews — verified against hand-calculated expected output in a rolled-back test before trusting it. Whole modal now runs through i18n (previously the one 100%-hardcoded-English holdout). Reputation scoring confirmed untouched — reviews still don't feed `get_reputation_state()`.
- **Caddie reliability fixes** (2026-09-19, two commits): `f68e035` — Caddie state going stale after backgrounding: the Realtime subscription had zero fallback for iOS WKWebView dropping its WebSocket while backgrounded, so a missed "flipped to complete" event left the in-memory list stale forever. Fixed with one `Capacitor.appStateChange` listener re-running the existing `refetch()` on foreground — no schema/Edge Function change. `d1fb696` — two independent data-loss bugs: `Caddie.tsx` hardcoded `.slice(0, 5)` permanently hid anything past the 5 most recent analyses (nothing was deleted, just unreachable — a real test account had 16, only 5 ever visible; now lazy "show more" paging); `analyze-swing`'s `fail()` used to **delete** the row on any pipeline failure (real data loss, plus orphaned the already-uploaded Storage video — confirmed 81 stored videos vs 53 DB rows before this fix). Now marks `status='failed'` with `error_message` instead, using columns that already existed for exactly this. Videos orphaned *before* this fix are not recovered — still needs a separate cleanup decision if that matters.
- **Caddie V2 reel-style architecture + declutter pass** (`711c953` then `1967c1f`, 2026-09-19/20): 3 new scored segments (shoulder_line, knees, wrists) with per-segment `anchorKeypoint`/`reliabilityReason`; new on-video anchored callout bubbles (`SwingCallout.tsx`) reusing the existing overlay's coordinate math, capped at one validated Needs-Improvement + one validated Good, docked to left/right rails with a leader line, never overlapping. Skeleton line/dot weights quieted so validated segments stand out by contrast. Phase row switched from flex-wrap to horizontal-scroll (fixes the old two-row layout bug). Zero changes to `analyze-swing`, the Roboflow workflow id, the Gemini prompt/schema, or any DB/schema — the whole segment/reliability layer is a pure client function over data already stored. Not yet verified on a physical device per that session's own flag.
- **Sign-in reliability**: `8007e5b` (2026-09-19) — an expired/already-used magic link (common cause: email clients' link-prefetch scanners consuming the one-time link before the person taps it) silently did nothing instead of showing an error; now surfaces a real toast via `AuthContext`'s `authError`. `dc3ff99` — GitGuardian flagged a hardcoded Apple App Review demo password in `scripts/setup-apple-review-account.ts`; rotated the live Supabase Auth password (verified via a real sign-in call), removed the literal, now reads `APPLE_REVIEW_PASSWORD` from env. Old (now-inert) password string still visible in one historical commit's diff on GitHub — not rewritten/force-pushed.
- **Golf Courses Near You** (`87bdaee`, 2026-09-19): standalone `/courses/nearby` screen + a Home preview card, entirely reusing existing infrastructure (Geoapify nearby search, GolfCourseAPI enrichment, Haversine distance, existing `LocationPicker`/browser geolocation) — no new provider, migration, or native permission. Radius always follows the golfer's saved `travelRadiusMiles` (no 10/25/50 pills in V1, falls back to 25mi only if that value is non-finite). Booking/tee-time actions only appear for the 2 hand-verified supported courses; every other course stays informational. "Open Rounds Near You" using real distance explicitly deferred (Phase 2).
- **Course-matching hardening** (`c42dd41`, 2026-09-19): `course-enrich` now rejects a GolfCourseAPI match outright when both sides name a distinguishing token (color/direction/number, e.g. "Black"/"Red"/"North") and they disagree — closes a real multi-course-facility mismatch risk (e.g. "Bethpage Black" matching a "Bethpage Red" candidate). Also cross-validates coordinates via Haversine when GolfCourseAPI provides its own (v1.1) and rejects outright on >1mi disagreement rather than silently accepting with lower confidence (GolfMe doesn't display confidence anywhere yet). No schema change; Edge Function redeployed.
- **Visual/branding polish** (several small commits, 2026-09-17/18): app-icon gradient applied consistently to the primary Button, TopBar/SideNav logo chip, Home's CTA, and Find/Community tab selectors; Splash/Auth logo matched to the real icon gradient (splash shortened to 2.75s); brand green/gold hex literals consolidated into shared CSS tokens (`d121726` — the same tokens this session's Reputation crest work reused); Caddie score/label text recolored to brand dark green; Caddie's language-pill focus ring fixed (was double/mismatched, then found still cut off, two commits); loading-cart roof darkened for visibility; Chat tab's unread badge fixed after getting clipped by BottomNav's `overflow-hidden` (same general class of bug as this session's own Caddie-ring clipping fix, different surface); Profile's "member since" line restyled as a badge; handicap-number color progression added to Profile/GolferProfilePage (`cb3dd33`, bands verified by hand against all 15 boundary values, also fixed a pre-existing plus-handicap sign display bug on those two surfaces only); "Golf Me" → "GolfMe" product-name copy fixed everywhere (`3cad255`).
- **Testing across Phase 22**: every commit's own message confirms `tsc`/`lint`/`build` clean at commit time; several explicitly flag "not yet verified on a physical device" (Caddie V2 reel architecture in particular). No live two-account or physical-device pass across this whole phase is recorded anywhere — worth treating the same as every earlier phase's "not yet verified live" caveats, not as quietly resolved.

## Remote/local sync note (2026-08-12)

The `create_profiles` and `lock_down_handle_new_user_execute` migrations were applied straight to `golfme-dev` in an earlier session (likely mobile/claude.ai/code — "remote control"), without a corresponding local commit. This session discovered the drift via `list_tables`/`list_migrations` against the live project, and wrote matching files into `supabase/migrations/` so the repo is reproducible again. **Lesson: always check `list_migrations` against the remote project at the start of a Supabase-touching session, don't trust local `supabase/migrations/` alone to reflect DB state**, since this project has no CLI-linked local Postgres to diff against.

## Remaining / next up

**2026-09-21 audit note**: items 1 and 2 below are resolved (confirmed live — see "Supabase configuration status"), item 11 is resolved (Community has been live for weeks, 27 real posts exist), and item 12 is resolved (real Roboflow+Gemini swing analysis has been live since before Phase 22, 56 real analyses processed). Left in place below rather than renumbered/deleted, since the surrounding "why" context is still useful — but treat those four as done, not open.

0g. **2026-09-25 rollout status: Steps 1–3 live, backfill NOT started.** Step 1: migration `20260925090000` applied and verified (bucket private, 3 owner-only policies, 57/57 rows valid, anon sees 0 objects). Step 2: `analyze-swing` v30 + `delete-account` v23 deployed via the Supabase CLI; a real TestFlight analysis on the old public-URL client passed (row `ae15a0d0`, 87.5 s, no errors). Step 3: this web deploy switches NEW uploads/playback to `caddie-media`. Next: Micky's 14-item physical-iPhone checklist, then the staged backfill (each stage separately approved); public originals stay until a 7-day grace period + approval; orphan sweep (Caddie orphans only) separately approved. Design: new private `caddie-media` bucket (owner-only select/insert/delete) + `caddie_analyses.source_media_path`/`thumbnail_path` (migration `20260925090000`, rollback-tested live in a rolled-back transaction). New direct uploads go private; rows store object paths, the app renders 1-hour signed URLs (`src/lib/caddieMedia.ts`, re-signed near expiry / on resume / on playback error); Share to Community copies the files into public community-media at publish time; deleting a Swing Post first gives its Ask-Caddie analyses a private copy (`privatizeAnalysesOfPost`) so the post leaves nothing public; analyze-swing reads private videos via a server-side signed URL made with the caller's JWT, trims into caddie-media, and only accepts the caller's own storage objects (fixes P2-9; `api/extract-frames.ts`/`api/trim-video.ts` also refuse non-Supabase-Storage URLs); delete-account purges caddie-media. Existing files: `scripts/migrate-caddie-media-private.ts` (dry run by default; copy -> verify -> repoint -> verify -> 7-day grace -> delete-public -> orphans, each a separate approved run). Rollout order: migration -> deploy analyze-swing + delete-account -> web deploy -> iPhone checks -> backfill. Dry run 2026-09-25: 48 direct-upload rows / 96 objects (418 MB) to copy, 14 public objects stay (used by 7 shared posts), 82 public originals (343 MB) removable after grace; orphans: 76 `caddie-*` (602 MB) + 7 other (59 MB). Original item text: **move private Caddie uploads to a genuinely private Storage bucket with authenticated/signed access.** `20260923120000_restrict_community_media_listing` stopped anonymous *discovery* of Caddie media, but direct-upload Caddie videos/thumbnails still live in the public `community-media` bucket, so anyone who already holds (or previously enumerated) an object URL can still fetch it — public URLs never expire. Also covers the orphaned Caddie files left by the pre-2026-09-19 delete-on-failure bug (more `caddie-*` objects in Storage than direct-upload rows). Needs its own design pass (Caddie architecture — architect agent): upload path, analyze-swing/extract-frames fetch, replay, and the Share-to-Community reuse of the same URL all change.

## Manual configuration required

Not done by any session — needs dashboard/browser/third-party-account access this environment doesn't have:

- ~~`GEOAPIFY_API_KEY`~~ — **done**, confirmed live 2026-09-21: real Geoapify-sourced courses exist in `public.courses` (40 cached as of that date, including real Hong Kong entries — the pending live test), most recently synced same-day. Course search is fully real now.
- ~~`GOLF_COURSE_API_KEY`~~ — done, Micky set this in the dashboard 2026-08-16 (Edge Functions > Secrets). Powers `course-enrich`'s on-demand detail enrichment.
- ~~Supabase > Authentication > Providers > Google~~ — **done**, confirmed live 2026-09-21: 21 of 24 real users are signed in via Google (5 via email), queried directly from `auth.identities`.
- ~~Supabase > Authentication > Providers > Email~~ — already enabled and working, nothing to do here.
- ~~Supabase > Authentication > URL Configuration~~ — implicitly confirmed done: real users are completing real Google OAuth sign-ins in production (see above), which can't happen with a misconfigured Site URL/redirect list.
- ~~Google Cloud Console~~ — implicitly confirmed done, same evidence as above.
- ~~Vercel env vars~~ — done, confirmed working via two live production deploys this session (2026-09-21).

- **`CADDIE_DISABLED` (optional, Supabase > Edge Functions > Secrets)** — Caddie kill switch, added 2026-10-30 (`caddie/reliability-a`). Leave unset normally. Set to `1` to stop all new Caddie analyses without a deploy (provider outage, runaway spend): `analyze-swing` then returns 503 `code:provider_busy` before auth, any row insert, or any Roboflow/Gemini call, and the app shows "Caddie is busy, try again in a minute" and deletes the video it just uploaded. Unset it (or set `0`) to turn Caddie back on. Only takes effect once the phase-A `analyze-swing` is deployed.

**Nothing outstanding in this section as of 2026-09-21** — every item that was open as of Phase 6/21 is now confirmed resolved via live evidence (real users, real course data), not just "believed configured." If a genuinely new manual-config need comes up, add it here.

Found and fixed since this project started (newest first) — full Phase 6 detail in `BACKEND_STATUS.md`'s Security audit:
  - **Health Audit P3 cleanup** (2026-09-24): P3-1 fixed in Batch D; P3-2 deep-linked round/post/golfer pages wait for `hasLoaded` (new on RealRounds/RealSocial/RealCommunity) instead of flashing "not found"; P3-3 round dates are read by their UTC calendar day everywhere (`roundCalendarDay()` in `src/lib/golfCall.ts` — labels, past-round check, Today/Tomorrow/Weekend filters, availability matching; demo fixtures pinned to noon) so every viewer sees the host's intended day (hosts at UTC+12..+14 read one day early — accepted); P3-4 initial JS 1.37 MB → 0.90 MB (27 lazy routes via `lazyPage()` with a one-time reload on stale chunks; non-English dictionaries load on demand); P3-5 Caddie daily limit enforced in-DB (`20260924140000`: client delete policy dropped, `created_at` frozen, per-owner-locked BEFORE INSERT limit trigger) — the optional `analyze-swing` 429-status change is in the repo but **not yet redeployed** (repo ahead of deployed v29 by that one status-code tweak + comments; deploy from a logged-in Supabase CLI); P3-6 stale comments fixed.
  - **Health Audit Batch D, repo/server side** (2026-09-24): push had never worked, for two independent reasons. (1) The iOS project has no Push Notifications capability / `aps-environment` entitlement (never added since `957f3a2`), so `register()` fails silently and no device token is ever produced — **needs Xcode on the Mac + a new TestFlight archive** (checklist in the Health Audit doc / this session's notes: add the capability, confirm App ID + .p8 key have APNs, archive shows `aps-environment: production`, commit `App.entitlements` + pbxproj). (2) `send-push` 401'd every trigger call — `PUSH_INTERNAL_SECRET` on the function missing or not equal to the Vault `push_internal_secret` — **Micky must set both to one new value in the dashboard** and confirm the `APNS_*` secrets. Fixed and live: `send-push` v7 deletes tokens only on 410/`BadDeviceToken`/`Unregistered` and logs every failure reason; P3-1 (`removeAllListeners()` dropped the push-tap router); new `register_device_push_token()` RPC (`20260924130000`) so a phone's token moves to whichever account signs in (the old upsert hit RLS 42501 for the second account); `checkPushPermission` returns `unavailable` instead of `denied` when the plugin throws.
  - **Health Audit Batch E** (2026-09-24): P2-1 real golfer distance (was 0 mi for every real golfer); P2-2 golfer cards, join modal, Discover reputation sort and Following list read `get_credibility_stats`/`get_reputation_state`; P2-11 real Golf Circle = followed golfers you've completed a round with; P2-10 post delete goes through `delete_community_post()` (`20260924110000`), which returns the caller's now-unreferenced media paths for the client to remove (never files a kept Caddie analysis or another post uses), and `delete-account` v22 purges the user's folders in avatars/community-media/booking-proofs after the auth delete; P2-6 `RealSocialContext` fetches messages incrementally, refreshes the profile directory every 5 min, polls 30 s while realtime is up (6 s when down), memoizes its value so idle ticks don't re-render the app; P2-7 `RealCaddieContext` queues a follow-up refetch instead of dropping mid-fetch events and no longer selects `roboflow_analysis_json` for the list (lazy `loadPoseData` on the detail screen); P2-8 swing callouts only anchor to high/medium-confidence phases, tighter frame tolerance, highlight window 0.45 s → 0.25 s. ~50 pre-existing orphaned Storage objects not swept.
  - **Health Audit Batch C** (2026-09-24, 7 migrations `20260924100000`..`20260924100600`, applied live via the Supabase MCP): P1-2 deleting a Swing Post with an Ask-Caddie analysis failed ("Source post not found.") — the analysis is now kept and converted to `direct_upload` (account deletion was already fine, the audit was wrong on that); P1-3 users could write `member_since`/legacy reputation counters on `profiles`, and hosts could forge `tee_time_source='user_verified'` or edit course/date/time/`total_spots` directly — frozen via triggers (Reputation formula untouched); P1-4 server half — `join_golf_call` rejects once `now() > date_iso + 12h`, completion only once `now() >= date_iso`, other direct status transitions refused; P1-5 blocks (either direction, host or any joined participant) now stop joins, host blocks stop round-chat posts, and `DataContext` hides rounds whose host is blocked either way; P2-3 `conversation_participants` keys frozen; P2-12 `leave_golf_call` refuses hosts and ended rounds; P2-4 direct `courses` insert/update removed, coordinates now go through `attach_external_course_mapping(p_latitude, p_longitude)`. `course-enrich` redeployed as v22 on 2026-09-24 (coordinates via the RPC; CORS preflight 204).
  - **Health Audit Batch B** (2026-09-24, web-only, commits `35fda1b`..`2fe09e3` + the nav-ring follow-up): P1-1 same-user auth events (hourly `TOKEN_REFRESHED`, resume) remounted the whole app and a failed profile fetch routed onboarded users into `/profile-setup`, which then overwrote their preferences; P1-6 a stuck (abandoned) Caddie `processing` row spun the nav ring / Swing Post "Analyzing…" forever — now uses the same 5-min `isStaleProcessing` threshold as the Caddie list; P1-7 a Caddie result deep link (push from a killed app, web refresh) bounced to `/caddie` before data loaded — `RealCaddieContext.hasLoaded`; P1-4 client half — past-dated rounds hidden from Find/Home and unjoinable (`isPastRound`), confirm dialog before "Mark Round Completed". Not yet verified on a physical device.

## Architectural decisions

- **Mocked-first, Supabase-second**: UI and product flows were built against a localStorage mock so product iteration wasn't blocked on backend design. Migration to Supabase happens entity-by-entity now that the shape of the data model is proven out by the mock.
- **Anon key only in the frontend, ever**: `VITE_`-prefixed env vars are inlined into the shipped bundle by Vite. The service_role/secret key must never be referenced in frontend code or committed anywhere — RLS policies are the access-control boundary, not key secrecy. See the comment header in `src/lib/supabase.ts` and `.env.example`.
- **Single repo, single `main` branch, GitHub as source of truth**: no long-lived feature branches; small commits with clear messages so any session (laptop or claude.ai/code mobile) can `git log`/`git pull` and immediately understand recent history. Push and Vercel deploy always require explicit confirmation from Micky before happening.
- **No unattended/scheduled agents**: development sessions are always actively driven (by Micky, from the laptop or from claude.ai/code in a mobile browser) — no cron-triggered or background cloud agents are configured to push commits unsupervised.
- **Reputation counters stay denormalized on `profiles` for now**: `completed_rounds`, `show_up_rate_pct`, etc. live directly on the row (same shape as the old mock) rather than being computed from a `reviews` table, since `reviews` doesn't exist yet. Revisit once reviews are migrated — likely becomes a computed/aggregated value at that point.

- `20260811163457_create_profiles` — creates `public.profiles`, enables RLS + 3 policies (select-any-authenticated, insert-own, update-own), `set_updated_at()` trigger, `handle_new_user()` signup trigger.
- `20260811163630_lock_down_handle_new_user_execute` — revokes public `EXECUTE` on `handle_new_user()` to close the direct-RPC-call privilege-escalation surface (SECURITY DEFINER functions in `public` are otherwise callable by anon/authenticated by default).
- `20260812180000_add_profile_skill_level_and_language` — adds nullable `skill_level` (check-constrained to Beginner/Intermediate/Advanced) and `language` columns to `public.profiles`, needed by Phase 1's real onboarding write-back. Applied directly to `golfme-dev` via the Supabase MCP and reconciled into this file. Advisors clean after.
- `20260812200000_add_avatars_storage_bucket` — public `avatars` Storage bucket + 4 RLS policies on `storage.objects` (public read, own-folder-only insert/update/delete), needed by Phase 2's real avatar upload. Applied via the Supabase MCP, reconciled into this file. Advisors clean after (aside from one pre-existing, unrelated dashboard-level warning about leaked-password protection — irrelevant since this project uses magic-link, not password, auth).

Needed by Phase 4's real multiplayer rounds. Applied via the Supabase MCP, reconciled into this file.
