## Supabase configuration status

- Project: `golfme-dev` (dev-only, no production project yet), ref `adokdenbmpshqgjbzshb`.
- Client wiring: done (`src/lib/supabase.ts`), and now live — real auth (Google OAuth + email magic link) and real profile read/write both run through it for non-demo accounts. Demo accounts still never touch it.
- Secrets: anon key lives in `.env.local` (gitignored, not committed) **and is now also set in Vercel** (Production/Preview/Development), added via `vercel env add` — the site would otherwise crash on load, since `src/lib/supabase.ts` throws if these are unset (see "Vercel deployment status" below). Service-role key has never been used in this codebase and should stay that way for client code.
- RLS: enabled on every table (`profiles`, `courses`, `course_external_ids`, `golf_calls`, `round_participants`, `conversations`, `conversation_participants`, `blocks`, `reports`, `notifications`, `follows`, `community_posts`, `community_comments`, `community_post_votes`, `community_comment_votes`, `saved_posts`, `hidden_posts`), verified via advisors after every migration (0 unexpected security/performance lints, including Phase 7's new tables).
- Auth providers: Email (magic link) and **Google OAuth are both enabled and working in production** — confirmed 2026-09-21 via `auth.identities`: 21 real users via Google, 5 via email. The "Google not yet enabled" note from Phase 6 is resolved.
- Edge Functions, all ACTIVE as of 2026-09-21: `course-search` (v25, real Geoapify results — `GEOAPIFY_API_KEY` is set), `course-enrich` (v21), `delete-account` (v21), `analyze-swing` (v29 — the real Roboflow+Gemini swing-analysis pipeline, 56 real analyses processed, 54 complete/0 failed/1 stuck as of that date), `get-tee-times` (v12), `translate-caddie-analysis` (v6), `send-push` (v6), plus two diagnostic/temp functions (`roboflow-test-temp`, `zz-diagnostic-image-check`) — **both deleted 2026-09-26** after a final dependency check (no app/script/function/SQL/trigger caller, no log invocations; `analyze-swing` calls Roboflow directly). 7 functions remain.
- Realtime: enabled on `golf_calls`, `round_participants`, `messages`, `notifications` (via the `supabase_realtime` publication) — live-verified working across two real accounts in Phase 4 and Phase 5's tests.
- Real usage as of 2026-09-21 (live counts, not the two Phase 4/5 test accounts below): **24 real users** (22 onboarded), 11 Golf Calls, 26 round participants, 40 cached courses, 56 Caddie analyses, 27 Community posts, 45 messages, 171 notifications, 0 device push tokens (see "Remaining / next up").
- Two real test accounts also still exist in `golfme-dev` from Phase 4/5's live testing (`golfme-phase1-smoketest@mailinator.com`, `golfme-phase4-accountb@mailinator.com`), plus a little leftover test data (a cancelled round, a couple of test messages/notifications/blocks — the block was undone) — harmless, clear it if you want a clean dev DB.
- Security/performance advisors as of 2026-09-21: nothing new or blocking. WARN-level items are the already-expected ones (`pg_net` in `public` schema, ~30 `SECURITY DEFINER` functions intentionally callable by `authenticated` — that's the access pattern for this project, not a bug, see comments at each function's own migration). One not previously flagged: **leaked-password protection is disabled** — likely inapplicable (this project is magic-link/OAuth only, no passwords), but cheap to enable if you want it silenced. INFO-level: `reviewer_invites` has RLS-enabled-no-policy (deliberate, see Phase 12), ~30 unindexed FKs and a few unused indexes (fine at current scale, revisit if it grows), `device_push_tokens`' RLS policies re-evaluate `auth.<fn>()` per-row (minor, same fix pattern as everywhere else in this file — `(select auth.<fn>())`).

## Vercel deployment status

- Live at `https://golfme.app` (production alias, confirmed via two successful `vercel --prod` deploys this session, 2026-09-21). Older aliases `https://golf-me-teal.vercel.app`/`https://golf-me-micky-s-ideas.vercel.app` still exist as pointers to the same project — `golfme.app` is the current canonical one, update any stale bookmarks/docs that still say `-teal`.
- `VITE_SUPABASE_URL`/`VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` are set as Vercel env vars (Production/Preview/Development) — confirmed working, not just configured (real users are signing in and using the live site).
- Fixed and deployed a real bug found via a live report (historical, Phase 6-era): `AuthContext`'s `getSession()`/profile-fetch calls had no error handling, so a rejected promise (flaky network, a restrictive in-app browser, etc.) could leave the app stuck on the loading spinner forever instead of falling through to Welcome. Both now degrade gracefully to "signed out" on failure.
- Real auth (Google/Email) is fully live and working — no longer blocked on provider config (see above). Demo mode remains fully functional alongside it.
- This session's deploy pattern: `git push origin main`, then `npx vercel --prod --yes` (a `vercel` plugin/CLI is available in this environment and successfully authenticates — an earlier bare `npx vercel` attempt without going through the plugin returned "Not authorized," so use the plugin-backed invocation, not a fresh unauthenticated `npx vercel`). Always ask before push and before deploy, per standing project rule — confirmed both explicitly this session before each of the 3 deploys.

## Exact next recommended action

**2026-11-03**: Coach Replay "what to work on", on branch `feat/coach-replay-work-on` (NOT pushed, NOT deployed, no migration). Micky: "coach replay should also capture what they should work on — right now it's all positives." Root cause: `v1Findings` only made a fix from a pose `needs_improvement` or a score criterion <= 8/20; typical swings score 9-15 (the silent middle band), and `work_on`/`focus`/`drill` were never shown because none is tied to a checkpoint.
- **Client (works on existing rows, no server change needed)** (`src/lib/coachReplay.ts`): the main `focus` (title + instruction, verbatim) is the fix on the weakest-scoring checkpoint (`weakestCheckpoint`: lowest of setupAndPosture/backswing/downswingSequencing/finish below 16, ties to the earlier checkpoint; none if all >= 16; balance never counts). A `work_on` item tagged with a moment (high/medium) is the fix there (rank 200, ahead of the focus at 300; measured pose faults 0-6 and score <= 8 reasons 100 still win). After the pass a closing "What to work on" card (`v1Summary`, `CoachSummaryCard`): the focus when no shown freeze carried it, up to two high/medium `work_on` items not already on a card (two lines max including the focus), and the drill (name + up to 3 steps). A plan with only a summary still coaches (slow pass, then the card). Text is stored fields only (`kind: "stored"`, sources `details.focus` / `details.workOn.<i>` / `details.drill`); labels are fixed keys (`coachReplay.summaryTitle/mainFocus/workOn/drill/done/summaryOnlyHint`, 6 locales). Cards with a strength and a fix show both; "Nothing flagged" copy unchanged. `useCoachVideoPauses` gained `ended`/`dismissEnded` (view state only; persisted-job state untouched). `CaddieAnalysisDetail` passes `coaching={details}`. Demo gets a matching closing card (`demoCoachSummary`, `coachReplay.demo.summaryWorkOn/drillName/drillStep1/2`).
- **Server (optional, additive)**: `analyze-swing` schema: optional `work_on[].checkpoint` enum (`address/top/impact/follow_through/whole_swing`), outside `required`; `withSanitizedWorkOnCheckpoints()` (`_shared/caddieCues.ts`) removes an invalid tag from its item (never the item) and never throws. Prompt + cue field description: each checkpoint cue is now a corrective "what to do" at that moment, not praise, null when nothing there needs changing (90-char/plain-text rules unchanged). `translate-caddie-analysis` copies the tag through like `confidence` (re-sanitized); no new translated text. Client re-sanitizes the tag on read (`RealCaddieContext.jsonToDetails`).
- **Checks**: `test:caddie` 69/69 (new cases in `tests/coachReplay.test.ts` and `tests/caddieCues.test.ts`), `test:chat`/`onboarding`/`courses` pass, `npx tsc -b`, `npm run lint` (exit 0, no new warnings), `npm run build` exit 0 (worktree without `.env.local`: landing prerender skipped, bundle fine). Edge Functions only syntax-checked with esbuild (no Deno here). Headless 390x844 screenshots, before/after, of a throwaway real-`<video>` harness (mid-band analysis, untagged and tagged), the in-app fullscreen, and the demo; the harness files were deleted.
- **Go-live** (Micky's call; no secret, no migration): client = push + Vercel (works on existing analyses by itself). Server = `supabase functions deploy analyze-swing` and `supabase functions deploy translate-caddie-analysis` (deploy both together; both bundle `_shared/caddieCues.ts`). Order doesn't matter: an old client ignores the tag; a new client without the tag falls back to focus placement.
- **Follow-up (same branch, 1791ecc)**, Micky: "the pause box must not cover the clip" and "users should still see their clip, the body points should pinpoint what's good and what needs work". In-app fullscreen: the card sits in a bottom band capped at 35% of the screen and the video shrinks to the space above it (slot height via ResizeObserver; the overlay canvas also observes the video box now). Before, the card covered 245-482 px of the clip. Inline: still below the video, capped at 40vh. Header and Continue/Done stay fixed; only the body scrolls, faded when more is below; an overflowing card doesn't auto-continue. Paused frame: `coachFocusSegments()` maps the card's `assessment.<segment>` findings to body parts and the overlay draws a halo, thicker line and joint rings in the measured colour, only where that verdict applies at that time and the keypoints were observed; that segment wins the callout. Score/focus/work_on findings get no highlight. Measured at 390x844 and 375x667 (inline + fullscreen, normal + long text): 0 px card/video overlap.
- **Follow-up 2 (same branch)**, Micky: cards must never need scrolling. The scrolling body, fade and overflow-hold from 1791ecc are gone. Each card now has a fixed compact shape: the phase label, one line per finding (title · body, cut with an ellipsis), the cue at most two lines with "Remember" inline, and 44px buttons. The summary card shows one line each for the focus/work_on items and the drill name (steps are in the results below). Body text is 13px. Auto-continue is back to its original behaviour (dwell by full text length, tap holds). The slot CSS height caps were removed on purpose: a cap the card exceeded would make it overflow onto the video. The compact shape keeps it at most 34.9% of a 375x667 screen (27.6% at 390x844) with 90-character cues and long Gemini text. Highlight, video-above-band and swipe zone from 1791ecc unchanged. Checked: 0 px overlap, no scrollable or cut-off element in any card, min body font 13px, buttons 44px, at both sizes, inline + fullscreen.
- **Not verified**: real Gemini output (how often it tags, whether tags agree with the weakest score, cue tone after the prompt change); iPhone (safe areas, the shrinking video and native controls in WKWebView, card body scrolling by touch); whether the focus, which is whole-swing advice, reads naturally on one moment for real analyses. If it doesn't, drop the focus from moments (one line in `v1Findings`) and keep it on the summary.

**2026-11-02**: Caddie per-checkpoint coaching cues, on branch `feat/caddie-checkpoint-cues` (NOT pushed, NOT deployed, no migration). Coach Replay cards can now show a cue Gemini wrote for that exact moment instead of only the fixed per-fault copy.
- **Server** (`analyze-swing`): the Gemini response schema has a new optional `checkpoint_cues` object (`address`/`top`/`impact`/`follow_through`, each a nullable string). It is outside `required` (and so are its fields), so a response without it is still valid. The prompt asks for one short, plain-language, actionable line per moment Gemini placed in `phases`, at most 90 characters, null rather than a generic tip. Before the save, `withSanitizedCheckpointCues()` (`supabase/functions/_shared/caddieCues.ts`) rewrites the field: unknown keys, non-strings, empty or markdown-only values, cues with a link, and cues for a moment `phases` didn't place are dropped; markdown/HTML is stripped; long cues are capped (word boundary, or code point for CJK) with an ellipsis; if nothing survives the field is left out. It never throws and never fails an analysis; kill switch, deadline, retries and failure codes are untouched. Stored inside `analysis_json` (existing jsonb, owner-only RLS), so no column and no migration.
- **Server** (`translate-caddie-analysis`): it spreads the original `analysis_json`, so a cue would otherwise stay in the original language next to translated text. Cues are now sent for translation when present (optional schema field) and re-sanitized; if none come back, the field is dropped and the card uses its own translated copy.
- **Client**: `RealCaddieContext.jsonToDetails` maps the field to `details.checkpointCues` through `checkpointCuesFromJson()` (re-sanitized with the same shared rules). `buildCoachPlan` takes an optional per-checkpoint cue map; the analysis's cue wins over the fixed `coachReplay.cue.*` copy, which stays the fallback. V1's finish checkpoint (its follow-through) takes `follow_through`. A cue alone never creates a freeze. Old rows have no field and render exactly as before. Pure view-state derivation; the persisted job/resume state is untouched. Demo cards are unchanged.
- **Tests**: `tests/caddieCues.test.ts` (new, in `test:caddie`) and new cases in `tests/coachReplay.test.ts`. `npx tsc -b`, `npm run lint` (0 errors), `test:caddie`/`chat`/`onboarding`/`courses` and `npm run build` pass (in a worktree without `.env.local` the landing prerender step logs a missing-env error; the bundle itself builds).
- **Go-live** (Micky's call, both functions, no secret or migration): `supabase functions deploy analyze-swing` then `supabase functions deploy translate-caddie-analysis` (same linked production project as earlier deploys; each bundles the new `_shared/caddieCues.ts`), plus the client (push + Vercel) to show the cues. Order doesn't matter for safety: an old client ignores the field, a new client without the deployed function just falls back. Deploy `translate-caddie-analysis` together with `analyze-swing` so a translated analysis never keeps an untranslated cue.
- **Not verified**: real Gemini output (cue quality, how often it fills all four, language compliance, whether the extra field shifts scores at all); iPhone card layout with a 90-character cue. After deploy, run a few analyses as admin (one non-English) and read the cards.
- **Open choice**: the server cue overrides the fixed cue even when the card's "fix" is a measured pose fault, so the cue may address something different from that fix line. If that reads oddly on real analyses, flip the one line in `buildCoachPlan` to prefer the fixed cue when the fix came from `assessment.*`.

**2026-11-02**: Caddie V3 "Coach Replay" first cut. Pushed to main for demo + admins (6850d78), then **switched on for all users** the same day on Micky's "push to main for all users" (`useCoachReplayEnabled()` now returns `true`; revert that one function to narrow it again). No migration, no Edge Function or prompt change. Client-only.
- **What it does**: in Slow Mode the replay freezes at up to four key moments (address, top, impact, finish) and shows a white card: what's going well, the one thing to fix (or "Nothing flagged at this moment", never "nothing to fix"), and a short cue. It carries on after a calm dwell (3.5-8 s, by text length; thin gold bar), on Continue, or on the native play control. Tapping the card holds it. Skip plays out the rest of that pass. A "Coach mode" switch gives the plain slow replay back. Under prefers-reduced-motion there is no auto-advance and no card motion. Freezes are view state only; `RealCaddieContext`'s persisted job/resume state is untouched.
- **Flag** (first cut; now `true` for everyone, see above): `src/lib/useCoachReplayEnabled.ts` was `isDemo || isAdmin` (the same server-derived `my_active_roles()` check as `/admin/dashboard`). Off for every other real user. No env var or secret. To widen the rollout, change that one function. To see it as an admin: open any complete analysis with pose data; Slow Mode + Coach mode are on by default.
- **Demo**: demo accounts have no video, so `/caddie/demo-caddie-1` (Driver) plays Coach Replay over the animated landing-page demo golfer (`CoachReplayDemo.tsx`; stage + gradients moved to `components/landing/swingStage.tsx`), with demo-only cards (`src/data/caddieCoachDemo.ts`) consistent with that demo analysis. Log in with `/login?demo` -> Try Demo Account -> Caddie -> Driver.
- **Adapter** (`src/lib/coachReplay.ts`, tests in `tests/coachReplay.test.ts`, part of `test:caddie`): a source gives *moments* (where to freeze, how sure) and *findings* (claims tied to a checkpoint, each with a `source` naming the stored field); `buildCoachPlan` merges them. Untrusted phases are skipped, moments out of swing order are dropped, and checkpoints with nothing to say are skipped. **V1 today**: times from `details.phases` with the same trust rule as Swing Check (high/medium only; impact only from a window < 0.3 s, frozen at its midpoint and tagged "Approx."; V1 has no finish, so the 4th freeze is the follow-through, labelled as such). Card text from swingAssessment's pose verdicts (existing translated strings, fixed per-segment cues for a fault) first, then the analysis's own `details.score.<criterion>.reason` sentence verbatim when points >= 16 (good) or <= 8 (fix): setupAndPosture -> address, backswing -> top, downswingSequencing -> impact, finish -> finish. Mid-band reasons are not shown. Gemini-sourced fixes get no cue (V1 never wrote one).
- **What V2 adds**: `v2Moments()` already maps V2 `keyFrames` (address/top/impact/finish, detected + high/medium; an unheld finish is labelled follow-through). V2 must still deliver (a) coach-validated thresholds that turn `measurements` into good/fix findings (they are values only today), (b) handedness, and (c) ideally a per-checkpoint cue from the server. Then a `coachPlanFromV2` feeds the same card.
- Checks: `npx tsc -b`, `npx oxlint src` (0 errors), `test:caddie`/`chat`/`onboarding`/`courses` all pass, `npm run build` OK (the landing prerender step warns "already contains a prerender block": a comment in `index.html` contains that literal; this is on `main` and not from this branch). Headless 390x844 runs: demo sequence (all 4 cards), plus a throwaway real-`<video>` harness (freezes landed at exactly 0.500/1.500/2.063 s, follow-through skipped for a mid-band score, Skip works). Landing demo golfer rechecked.
- **Not verified**: iPhone (WKWebView pause/seek precision at 0.25x, the card inside the in-app fullscreen, native controls interplay); an admin account on real analyses (how many V1 rows actually get 3-4 checkpoints vs 1-2).
- **Next**: Micky reviews the demo on his phone (needs a push/preview deploy, his call); try it on a few real analyses as admin; decide whether to add a per-checkpoint `cue` to the Gemini schema (backward compatible, old rows just show no cue).

**2026-10-30**: Caddie reliability phase A, on branch `caddie/reliability-a` (NOT pushed, nothing deployed, no migration). Micky approved prioritising Caddie reliability. Fixes on the live V1 path (V2 needs them too); same `caddie_analyses` row and statuses, no durable state moved client-side.
- **Wrong tips** (`src/lib/swingAssessment.ts`): only the LEAD arm is judged at the top (handedness isn't captured, so the straighter arm is taken as lead; an optional `handedness` option exists for later). The folded trail arm no longer gets "Right elbow bent". Hip sway is judged face-on only, knee flex down the line only, shoulder line is skipped down the line (`cameraAngle` now passed to `CaddieSwingReplay`). Skipped checks are `reliabilityReason: "not_applicable"` and hidden from Swing Check. Scoring is untouched (Gemini-side).
- **Failure codes**: `analyze-swing` writes `code:<pose_not_found|provider_busy|timeout|upload|unknown> [window:<s>-<e>] <detail>` into `error_message` (`supabase/functions/_shared/caddieReliability.ts`); `src/lib/caddieFailure.ts` maps them to specific copy in all 6 locales on the detail page, history list and Community post card. Old free-text rows show the old generic text; a stale `processing` row reads as a timeout.
- **Pose-presence gate**: before Gemini, fail `code:pose_not_found` ("Film again: whole body in frame, phone steady, one swing") when < `POSE_PRESENCE_MIN_RATIO` (0.1; set from a read-only check of the 50 latest production analyses on 2026-10-31: 0.5 would have refused 21, 0.1 refuses none since 2026-09-08) of answered frames show both shoulders, both hips and a wrist.
- **Retries**: Roboflow 429/5xx back off before the one retry; Gemini `generateContent` retries 2x with back-off on 429/5xx.
- **Deadline**: pipeline raced against 330 s -> `code:timeout`; an unexpected throw or a failed final save also marks the row failed. Terminal writes are conditional on `status = 'processing'`. Server stale cutoff 5 -> 6 min (above the deadline, below the client's 10).
- **Try again** resends the trim window the failed attempt used (from `error_message`), and the failed run's trimmed copy is deleted. The client refreshes the session before invoking when < 10 min of the JWT remain.
- **Kill switch** `CADDIE_DISABLED=1` (see Manual configuration).
- **Frames**: `analyze-swing` asks `api/extract-frames.ts` for frames <= 720 px on the long side; extract-frames reports `coordinateScale` (measured from the output JPEG) and `analyze-swing` scales keypoints back, so stored pose data and the replay overlay stay in source-video pixels. Either side can deploy first (an old extract-frames ignores `maxLongSide`; an old analyze-swing never sends it).
- **Putter** removed from the club picker.
- Checks: `npx tsc -b`, `npm run lint`, every `npm run test:*` (new `tests/swingAssessment.test.ts`, `tests/caddieFailure.test.ts` in `test:caddie`), `deno check` on `analyze-swing` (only the old `EdgeRuntime` name error, same as main), a local Deno harness that stubs fetch/Supabase/Roboflow/Gemini to run happy path, kill switch, no pose, Roboflow 429 and outage, Gemini 503 x2 and x3, timeout, late finish after timeout, failed save, failed trim, timeout after trim; a local Node harness of extract-frames on synthetic portrait, landscape, rotated and small clips. Nothing ran against production or a real vendor.
- **Not verified**: real Roboflow accuracy on 720 px frames (the model probably resizes internally, but check a few swings); how often real swings fall under the 0.5 pose gate (run the read-only coverage query in the phase-A report on recent `complete` rows before deploying); the new copy and Swing Check on a phone.
- **To go live (Micky's approval)**: (1) Vercel push for the client + `api/extract-frames.ts`; (2) `supabase functions deploy analyze-swing` (also ships `_shared/caddieReliability.ts`); (3) optionally note `CADDIE_DISABLED` for emergencies. Order doesn't matter.

**2026-10-26/28**: Chat gestures. Edge swipe back + replies are on `main` (80f478e, left-swipe version); the switch to swipe RIGHT is on branch `feat/chat-reply-right` (NOT pushed, nothing deployed). Micky approved both. (1) **Edge swipe back**: start within 32 px of the left edge on a DM thread and drag right; the thread follows over a dimmed backdrop, past 35 % or on a fling it slides out and goes back (`navigate(-1)` when opened from the inbox, else `/messages` with replace). `SwipeBackToInbox` wrapper around the `/messages/:id` route + `useEdgeSwipeBack` (passive touch listeners, one rAF transform); `DirectMessageThread` keyboard/viewport code untouched. (2) **Swipe RIGHT on a message to reply** (Micky's final call 2026-10-28, "it should be swipe right on message", replacing the 2026-10-27 left-swipe version that shipped to `main` as 80f478e). Rules in `src/lib/chatSwipe.ts` (`lockDirection` after 8 px + `classifySwipe`): right starting in the 32 px edge strip = back (wins, even on a bubble); right starting on a message bubble = reply (`useSwipeToReply`); right anywhere else = nothing; left anywhere (on or off bubbles) = reveal times (`useSwipeToRevealTimes`, exactly as before replies existed); vertical = scroll. Bubbles carry `data-reply-bubble` only while replying is possible. During a reply swipe only the bubble moves and a reply arrow fades in on its left; the other golfer's avatar (left) is untouched since the bubble moves away from it, and my own avatar (right, in the bubble's path) fades out over the first 16 px and back in on release, so they never overlap (chosen over capping the travel, which would leave only the 8 px gap), one light haptic at 60 px (`@capacitor/haptics`, already linked; `navigator.vibrate` on web, a no-op on iOS Safari), release past it sets the reply target and focuses the composer inside the gesture (like the send button's refocus). Composer shows "Replying to <name>" + snippet + X inside the existing composer container (the list's existing ResizeObserver handles the height change); sent replies show a quote (sender + 2-line snippet) that scrolls to and flashes the original, or "Original message unavailable" when it isn't loaded. Demo stores `replyToId` on the mock message. Real accounts: only offered once loaded rows carry `reply_to_message_id`; an insert refused for an unknown column (PGRST204/42703) is resent as a plain message. Needs migration `20261026120000_add_message_replies` (NOT applied; see the migrations list). Next: Micky reviews; on a physical iPhone check edge swipe vs. iOS's own back gesture, right swipe on bubbles vs. from the edge, left swipe on bubbles shows times, reply with the keyboard open and closed (focus after a swipe should open the keyboard exactly like a tap), the haptic, and that vertical scrolling over bubbles is unchanged; then apply the migration, then merge/deploy (with OK). `npm run test:chat` (20), tsc, lint, build clean; headless checks 26/26 reply + 15/17 swipe-back (the 2 'failures' are that script's vertical-scroll checks on a non-scrolling demo thread; passing on an overflowing thread in the reply script).

Caddie copy per Micky: score out of 100 + feedback + history by club. (3) **Prerender**: `npm run build` now ends with `node scripts/prerender-landing.mjs`, which `ssrLoadModule`s `src/prerender-entry.tsx` (`MemoryRouter` + `ForceLocale("en")` + `<Welcome/>`, `react-dom/server`) and injects the HTML as `<div id="prerender">` before `#root` in `dist/index.html` — fails soft (warns, leaves dist untouched) so it can never break the app build; `PRERENDER_SKIP=1` skips it. `index.html` inline guard runs before paint: native/standalone/signed-in (`golfme:isDemo` or any `sb-*-auth-token` key) or a non-landing path → `html.gm-app` hides the static copy, app boots exactly as before; signed-out `/` or `/welcome` → `html.gm-static-landing` keeps the static page on screen and hides `#root` (so the loader never flashes over it) until `Welcome.tsx`'s mount effect removes `#prerender` and the class (8 s safety timeout). `Reveal` renders fully visible when there is no `document` (SSR) or under `<html data-prerender>`. No schema, no native, no auth change. **Not validated here**: the npm registry was unreachable from this session, so tsc/lint/`vite build`/the prerender step/browser checks did NOT run — the first `npm run build` (locally or a Vercel preview) is the validation. Next: Micky's OK to push the branch for a Vercel preview; check (a) `curl -s <preview>/ | grep -c "Find your foursome"` returns >0, (b) the preview at 320/375/390/430/1440, (c) the iOS app still boots straight to Home with no landing flash, (d) a signed-in web session likewise, (e) FAQ/anchors/App Store link; then merge + deploy, add the property in Google Search Console + Bing Webmaster Tools, submit `sitemap.xml`, request indexing of `/`. **Interactive pass (same day, Micky: "more interactive, cool redesigns")**: `src/components/landing/Interactive.tsx` — `HeroShowcase` (tabs auto-rotate every 5 s with a progress underline, a tap pauses rotation 12 s, phone leans toward the mouse pointer on desktop), `MatchDemo` (Level / When / Style chips re-rank a 5-golfer example list using the app's real labels "Great/Strong/Good/Possible Match"; `GolfersScreen` now takes a `golfers` prop), `FoursomeDemo` (tap Join → seats fill with a spring animation, spots count down, "Round full", Replay), `CaddieDemo` (Analyze → scan line → pose skeleton pulses over a silhouette → 81/100 ring + three feedback lines; Replay), `CourseTicker` (CSS marquee of NYC public courses, pauses on hover), `FloatingCta` (mobile-only App Store bar after 640 px of scroll; footer gets bottom padding for it), `useActiveSection` (nav underline follows the section in view). All demos render a sensible first frame with no JS (prerender-safe) and are labelled illustrative. `index.css`: explicit `.animate-ring-draw/float/drift` utilities (the project declares `animate-*` classes by hand in `@layer utilities`, so the earlier tokens alone would not have produced classes) plus marquee / pose-pulse / seat-fill / scan. 3 new keys (`landing.demo.tryIt`, `landing.demo.replay`, `landing.cta.floating`) in 6 locales. Still not compiled here. **2026-10-22 (feed lag)**: branch `feat/swing-video-backdrop`, merged to `main` and live 2026-10-23. Micky: the Home feed felt laggy when dragging, and opening/closing swing videos and posts was unstable. Root causes (golfme-architect, read-only audit): the in-app media viewer rendered inside the card (a card's `:active` translate made it the viewer's containing block, its touches reached the tab-swipe listener, and its dismiss drag scrolled the feed underneath); the 1 s native-fullscreen fallback also opened the in-app viewer when the golfer dismissed the native player within that second; photo-carousel swipes were taken over as root-tab swipes; Back from a post always scrolled Home to the top (and the 5 root pages re-rendered on every carousel state change); the live CSS `blur(40px)` backdrop under playing video, and the transparent full-width video box that let WebKit's black fullscreen-exit box show as a band. Fixes, one commit each: opt-in perf HUD (`src/lib/perfHud.ts`, `localStorage.gmPerfHud="1"` or `?perfhud=1`); canvas pre-blurred backdrop + video box sized to its picture; 10 px tap slop on card/video/photo taps, begin-fullscreen flag for the fallback, viewer portaled to body with body scroll lock and vertical-only touchmove cancel, `data-no-swipe` on feed photo carousels, card press reset now `hover:active:` only; ScrollToTop restores position on back/forward (per history entry) and resets new pages before paint, plus a 600 ms double-open guard on posts; root-tab panels memoized. No schema, no native change. tsc/lint/build/test:courses pass; not browser-tested here (no working headless browser in WSL). Next: Micky's OK to push for a Vercel preview, HUD readings on the iPhone: "before" from a preview of the HUD-only commit (production has no HUD), "after" from the branch tip preview; then merge + deploy.

**2026-10-23**: Host a Round course search goes back in-flow, on branch `fix/course-search-inline`, merged to `main` and live 2026-10-23 (6611272). Micky: tapping "Search all courses" jumped the field into a fixed panel under the header that covered the page title. The dock (0f51b56) is gone: the field stays in the form, suggestions open in normal flow under it and push Next down (`CourseAutocomplete` prop `dockAboveKeyboard` renamed `inlineSuggestions`; EditTeeTimeModal keeps its overlay dropdown). iOS app: on keyboardWillShow the window scrolls the field to just under the header, back on close. Root cause of the earlier failed scroll (21e8de9): under Keyboard resize:"body" only body's inline height shrinks, so the document's scroll range never grows for the keyboard, and this step is shorter than one screen (scroll range 0; measured 268 px of range with the old keyboard-height spacer vs ~405 px needed). Fix: an invisible absolutely-positioned spacer under the field from focus on, sized so the field can always reach the header. Same branch, app-wide follow-up (Micky: Notes textarea on Host a Round step 4 hidden behind the keyboard): new `src/lib/useRevealFocusedFieldAboveKeyboard.ts`, mounted in `AppGate`, native only. On focus of a text field on a normal (window-scrolling) page it adds an invisible abspos spacer so the page has scroll room, and on keyboardWillShow / focus change scrolls the field a gap above the keyboard (never under the header). Skips `[data-keyboard-reveal="off"]` (PostDetail composer, GroupChat, the inline course search), the DM thread by route (frozen, untouched), fields in fixed sheets or their own scroller, and scroll-locked pages. The four plain sheets that held text fields (EditTeeTimeModal, ShareRoundPrompt, VerifyStepModal, BookingProofHostControls) now use `keyboardAware` Modal. Native config untouched. Next: Micky's OK to merge + deploy, then the iPhone check.

**2026-09-26**: Health Audit improvement **Batch F** — pushed and live 2026-09-27 (`36a21d8`):
- F1 Swing Post video tap: iOS `webkitEnterFullscreen()` throws when the feed video has no metadata yet (autoplay suppressed, e.g. Low Power Mode), so the tap did nothing. `enterNativeVideoFullscreen()` now returns whether it entered; `PostCard` falls back to the in-app `FullscreenMediaViewer` if it didn't, or if native fullscreen hasn't appeared within 1 s.
- F2 round chat: `DataContext.sendMessage` resolves true/false instead of swallowing errors; `GroupChat` shows a warning toast and restores the typed text (and draft) on failure.
- F3 past rounds: host wizard and Edit Tee Time reject a past date, or today with a parseable start time already passed (today + later time stays valid; an unparseable time label on today is allowed). The host wizard's default date now uses the local day, not UTC; Sunday weekend prefill no longer lands on yesterday. Client-side only — no DB constraint.
